## `CLAUDE.md`

```markdown
# Project Routine — 24H Work Dashboard

Instructions for any AI assistant (or developer) working on this project. **Follow this routine after EVERY code change — it is not optional.**

## Documentation routine (mandatory)

1. **docs/CODE.md** — auto-generated. Never edit by hand. It regenerates on every `npm run dev` / `npm run build`; after changes made outside a build, run `npm run docs`.
2. **docs/APP-SPEC.md** — update the relevant section (features / formulas / sheet mapping / UI-UX / architecture) and bump the version + date in the header whenever behavior, formulas, screens, or APIs change.
3. **docs/MEGA-PROMPT.md** — update whenever a requirement, formula, or data-format lesson changes, so the prompt can always rebuild the current app faithfully. Bump its version line.
4. **QA-REPORT.md** — append an addendum describing what changed, the root cause (for fixes), and what was verified.
5. **docs/USER-GUIDE.md** — update whenever a user-facing flow changes (screens, buttons, setup, troubleshooting).

## Verification routine (mandatory)

- Keep `tsc --noEmit` clean (`node node_modules/typescript/lib/tsc.js --noEmit -p tsconfig.json`).
- Pure logic (lib/date.ts, lib/parse.ts, lib/calc.ts, lib/seed.ts) must stay testable without Next.js — test via `node --experimental-strip-types` (use `import type` for type-only imports).
- Reference values for regression checks (from the real spreadsheet): July 2026 → target 198.0, day 1 = 9h (07:30–16:30); reserve days 2023:61, 2024:243, 2025:30, 2026:60 (all צו 8).

## Project invariants (do not break)

- All Google Sheets/Drive access stays server-side (`server-only`); the private key must never reach the client or appear in `GET /api/setup`.
- Writes to legacy (user-owned) sheets touch ONLY raw cells B:C and J:N; calculated columns belong to the sheet's formulas. App-created sheets (`computeDerived: true`) also get D:I, V:Y.
- "הגדרת תקן" journal rows are settings storage, not day records — they reuse first-of-month dates.
- Settings priority: `WorkdayStandards` tab → app tab `הגדרות תקן` → "הגדרת תקן" journal rows; quota: `Settings!AnnualVacationAllowance` → app tab.
- Journal tab detection requires BOTH A1~"תאריך" and B1~"כניסה".
- `#...!` formula-error cells parse as empty; totals/standards/day-names fall back to derivation from raw times and the calendar.
- Sheets API payloads must not contain empty inner arrays (`[]`).
- UI is Hebrew RTL; charts render in `dir=ltr` islands with day 1 / newest year on the right.
```

## `app/api/admin/backup-user/route.ts`

```ts
import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from "next/server";
import { createSupabaseServerClient } from "@/lib/supabase/server";
import fs from "fs";
import path from "path";

export const dynamic = "force-dynamic";

const ADMIN_EMAILS = new Set(["michael.liarzi@gmail.com"]);
const BACKUP_USER_DIR = path.join(process.cwd(), "backups", "users");

function escapeSqlValue(val: any): string {
  if (val === null || val === undefined) return "NULL";
  if (typeof val === "number") return val.toString();
  if (typeof val === "boolean") return val ? "TRUE" : "FALSE";
  if (val instanceof Date) {
    const y = val.getFullYear();
    const m = String(val.getMonth() + 1).padStart(2, "0");
    const d = String(val.getDate()).padStart(2, "0");
    if (val.getHours() === 0 && val.getMinutes() === 0 && val.getSeconds() === 0) {
      return `'${y}-${m}-${d}'`;
    }
    return `'${val.toISOString()}'`;
  }
  if (typeof val === "object") return `'${JSON.stringify(val).replace(/'/g, "''")}'`;
  return `'${String(val).replace(/'/g, "''")}'`;
}

async function fetchAllUserReports(supabase: any, userId: string): Promise<any[]> {
  const allReports: any[] = [];
  const pageSize = 1000;
  let from = 0;
  while (true) {
    const { data, error } = await supabase
      .from("reports")
      .select("*")
      .eq("user_id", userId)
      .order("date", { ascending: true })
      .range(from, from + pageSize - 1);

    if (error) {
      throw new Error("שגיאה בשליפת דיווחי השעות: " + error.message);
    }
    if (!data || data.length === 0) break;
    allReports.push(...data);
    if (data.length < pageSize) break;
    from += pageSize;
  }
  return allReports;
}

/** POST /api/admin/backup-user - Backup data for a specific user */
export async function POST(req: NextRequest) {
  try {
    const supabase = createSupabaseServerClient();
    const {
      data: { user },
      error: authError,
    } = await supabase.auth.getUser();

    if (authError || !user || !user.email || !ADMIN_EMAILS.has(user.email.toLowerCase())) {
      return NextResponse.json({ error: "גישה נדחתה. דרושות הרשאות מנהל." }, { status: 403 });
    }

    const body = await req.json().catch(() => ({}));
    const targetUserId = typeof body.userId === "string" ? body.userId.trim() : null;
    let targetEmail = typeof body.email === "string" ? body.email.trim() : null;

    if (!targetUserId && !targetEmail) {
      return NextResponse.json({ error: "נא לציין מזהה משתמש או אימייל לגיבוי." }, { status: 400 });
    }

    // Resolve user profile
    let profileQuery = supabase.from("profiles").select("*");
    if (targetUserId) {
      profileQuery = profileQuery.eq("id", targetUserId);
    } else if (targetEmail) {
      profileQuery = profileQuery.eq("email", targetEmail);
    }
    const { data: profiles, error: profileErr } = await profileQuery;
    if (profileErr || !profiles || profiles.length === 0) {
      return NextResponse.json({ error: "פרופיל המשתמש לא נמצא." }, { status: 404 });
    }

    const profile = profiles[0];
    const resolvedUserId = profile.id;
    targetEmail = profile.email || targetEmail || resolvedUserId;

    // Fetch all reports using pagination to prevent 1000 limit
    const userReports = await fetchAllUserReports(supabase, resolvedUserId);

    // Fetch workday standards
    const { data: standards, error: standardsErr } = await supabase
      .from("workday_standards")
      .select("*")
      .eq("user_id", resolvedUserId);

    if (standardsErr) {
      console.warn("Could not fetch workday standards for user:", standardsErr.message);
    }

    const userStandards = standards || [];

    const now = new Date();
    const dateStr = now.toISOString().slice(0, 10);
    const timeStr = now.toTimeString().slice(0, 8).replace(/:/g, "");
    const safeEmail = targetEmail.replace(/[^a-zA-Z0-9_-]/g, "_");
    const fileName = `backup-user-${safeEmail}-${dateStr}-${timeStr}.sql`;

    // Build SQL Dump
    const lines = [
      `-- ============================================================`,
      `-- User Data Backup: ${targetEmail}`,
      `-- User ID: ${resolvedUserId}`,
      `-- Date: ${now.toISOString()}`,
      `-- Total Reports: ${userReports.length}`,
      `-- Total Standards: ${userStandards.length}`,
      `-- ============================================================`,
      `BEGIN;`,
      ``,
      `-- 1. Profile`,
      `INSERT INTO public.profiles ("id", "email", "annual_vacation_quota") VALUES (${escapeSqlValue(profile.id)}, ${escapeSqlValue(profile.email)}, ${escapeSqlValue(profile.annual_vacation_quota)}) ON CONFLICT (id) DO UPDATE SET "annual_vacation_quota" = EXCLUDED."annual_vacation_quota";`,
      ``,
      `-- 2. Workday Standards (${userStandards.length} rows)`,
      `DELETE FROM public.workday_standards WHERE "user_id" = ${escapeSqlValue(resolvedUserId)};`,
    ];

    for (const std of userStandards) {
      const keys = Object.keys(std);
      const cols = keys.map((k) => `"${k}"`).join(", ");
      const vals = keys.map((k) => escapeSqlValue(std[k])).join(", ");
      lines.push(`INSERT INTO public.workday_standards (${cols}) VALUES (${vals});`);
    }

    lines.push(``);
    lines.push(`-- 3. Reports (${userReports.length} rows)`);
    lines.push(`DELETE FROM public.reports WHERE "user_id" = ${escapeSqlValue(resolvedUserId)};`);

    for (const rep of userReports) {
      const keys = Object.keys(rep);
      const cols = keys.map((k) => `"${k}"`).join(", ");
      const vals = keys.map((k) => escapeSqlValue(rep[k])).join(", ");
      lines.push(`INSERT INTO public.reports (${cols}) VALUES (${vals});`);
    }

    lines.push(``);
    lines.push(`COMMIT;`);
    lines.push(`-- Backup completed for user ${targetEmail}.`);

    const sqlContent = lines.join("\n");

    // 1. Save backup permanently to PostgreSQL public.user_backups table
    try {
      await supabase.from("user_backups").insert({
        user_id: resolvedUserId,
        email: targetEmail,
        filename: fileName,
        reports_count: userReports.length,
        standards_count: userStandards.length,
        backup_sql: sqlContent,
      });
    } catch (dbErr) {
      console.warn("[User Backup] Could not save to DB table:", dbErr);
    }

    // 2. Optional disk write (non-blocking for serverless/read-only environments like Vercel)
    try {
      const targetDir = process.env.VERCEL ? "/tmp" : BACKUP_USER_DIR;
      if (!fs.existsSync(targetDir)) {
        fs.mkdirSync(targetDir, { recursive: true });
      }
      fs.writeFileSync(path.join(targetDir, fileName), sqlContent, "utf8");
    } catch (diskErr) {
      console.warn("[User Backup] Could not write to disk (safe to ignore on serverless):", diskErr);
    }

    return NextResponse.json({
      ok: true,
      filename: fileName,
      reportsCount: userReports.length,
      standardsCount: userStandards.length,
      userEmail: targetEmail,
      sqlContent,
      message: `הגיבוי נוצר בהצלחה! נשמרו ${userReports.length.toLocaleString("he-IL")} דיווחים.`,
    });
  } catch (e) {
    console.error("[User Backup Error]", e);
    return NextResponse.json(
      { error: e instanceof Error ? e.message : "שגיאה ביצירת גיבוי למשתמש" },
      { status: 500 }
    );
  }
}
```

## `app/api/admin/backups/route.ts`

```ts
import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from "next/server";
import { createSupabaseServerClient } from "@/lib/supabase/server";

export const dynamic = "force-dynamic";

const ADMIN_EMAILS = new Set(["michael.liarzi@gmail.com"]);

/**
 * GET /api/admin/backups - List system backups and current retention setting stored on the NAS
 */
export async function GET() {
  try {
    const supabase = createSupabaseServerClient();
    const {
      data: { user },
      error: authError,
    } = await supabase.auth.getUser();

    if (authError || !user || !user.email || !ADMIN_EMAILS.has(user.email.toLowerCase())) {
      return NextResponse.json({ error: "גישה נדחתה. דרושות הרשאות מנהל." }, { status: 403 });
    }

    const { data: backups, error: dbErr } = await supabase
      .from("system_backups")
      .select("id, filename, backup_type, reports_count, profiles_count, standards_count, terms_count, file_size_kb, created_at")
      .order("created_at", { ascending: false });

    if (dbErr) {
      throw new Error("שגיאה בשליפת רשימת הגיבויים מה-NAS: " + dbErr.message);
    }

    // Fetch retention setting
    const { data: configData } = await supabase
      .from("system_config")
      .select("value")
      .eq("key", "backup_retention_days")
      .maybeSingle();

    let retentionDays = 30;
    if (configData?.value !== undefined && configData?.value !== null) {
      const parsed = parseInt(String(configData.value), 10);
      if (!isNaN(parsed) && parsed >= 1) {
        retentionDays = parsed;
      }
    }

    return NextResponse.json({
      ok: true,
      backups: backups || [],
      retentionDays,
    });
  } catch (err: any) {
    console.error("[Backups API GET Error]", err);
    return NextResponse.json(
      { error: err.message || "שגיאה בטעינת גיבויים" },
      { status: 500 }
    );
  }
}

/**
 * POST /api/admin/backups - Create, restore, download NAS backups, or set retention days
 */
export async function POST(req: NextRequest) {
  try {
    const supabase = createSupabaseServerClient();
    const {
      data: { user },
      error: authError,
    } = await supabase.auth.getUser();

    if (authError || !user || !user.email || !ADMIN_EMAILS.has(user.email.toLowerCase())) {
      return NextResponse.json({ error: "גישה נדחתה. דרושות הרשאות מנהל." }, { status: 403 });
    }

    const body = await req.json().catch(() => ({}));
    const action = body.action;

    // 1. Action: SET RETENTION DAYS
    if (action === "set_retention") {
      const days = parseInt(body.retentionDays, 10);
      if (isNaN(days) || days < 1 || days > 365) {
        return NextResponse.json({ error: "מספר הימים חייב להיות מספר חיובי בין 1 ל-365." }, { status: 400 });
      }

      const { error: upsertErr } = await supabase
        .from("system_config")
        .upsert({
          key: "backup_retention_days",
          value: days,
          updated_at: new Date().toISOString(),
        });

      if (upsertErr) {
        throw new Error("שגיאה בעדכון ימי הגיבוי: " + upsertErr.message);
      }

      // Enforce immediate cleanup of backups beyond the new limit
      try {
        const { data: allBackups } = await supabase
          .from("system_backups")
          .select("id")
          .order("created_at", { ascending: false });

        if (allBackups && allBackups.length > days) {
          const toDeleteIds = allBackups.slice(days).map((b) => b.id);
          await supabase.from("system_backups").delete().in("id", toDeleteIds);
        }
      } catch (cleanErr) {
        console.warn("Immediate cleanup note:", cleanErr);
      }

      return NextResponse.json({
        ok: true,
        retentionDays: days,
        message: `מדיניות השמירה עודכנה בהצלחה: יישמרו ${days} ימי גיבוי אחרונים ב-NAS.`,
      });
    }

    // 2. Action: CREATE MANUAL BACKUP ON NAS
    if (action === "create") {
      const { data, error } = await supabase.rpc("generate_system_backup", {
        p_backup_type: "manual_admin",
      });

      if (error) {
        throw new Error("שגיאה ביצירת הגיבוי ב-NAS: " + error.message);
      }

      return NextResponse.json({
        ok: true,
        backupId: data,
        message: "הגיבוי נוצר ונשמר בהצלחה ב-NAS!",
      });
    }

    // 3. Action: RESTORE BACKUP FROM NAS
    if (action === "restore") {
      const backupId = body.backupId;
      if (!backupId) {
        return NextResponse.json({ error: "מזהה גיבוי חסר לשחזור." }, { status: 400 });
      }

      const { data, error } = await supabase.rpc("restore_system_backup_by_id", {
        p_backup_id: backupId,
      });

      if (error) {
        throw new Error("שגיאה בביצוע השחזור ב-NAS: " + error.message);
      }

      return NextResponse.json({
        ok: true,
        result: data,
        message: "המערכת שוחזרה בהצלחה לנקודת הגיבוי שנבחרה! (נוצר גיבוי ביטחון מקדים באופן אוטומטי)",
      });
    }

    // 4. Action: DOWNLOAD SQL CONTENT OF A BACKUP
    if (action === "download") {
      const backupId = body.backupId;
      if (!backupId) {
        return NextResponse.json({ error: "מזהה גיבוי חסר להורדה." }, { status: 400 });
      }

      const { data, error } = await supabase
        .from("system_backups")
        .select("filename, backup_sql")
        .eq("id", backupId)
        .single();

      if (error || !data) {
        throw new Error("קובץ הגיבוי לא נמצא ב-NAS: " + (error?.message || ""));
      }

      return NextResponse.json({
        ok: true,
        filename: data.filename,
        sql: data.backup_sql,
      });
    }

    return NextResponse.json({ error: "פעולה לא חוקית." }, { status: 400 });
  } catch (err: any) {
    console.error("[Backups API POST Error]", err);
    return NextResponse.json(
      { error: err.message || "שגיאה בביצוע הפעולה" },
      { status: 500 }
    );
  }
}

/**
 * DELETE /api/admin/backups - Delete a system backup by ID (admin only)
 */
export async function DELETE(req: NextRequest) {
  try {
    const supabase = createSupabaseServerClient();
    const {
      data: { user },
      error: authError,
    } = await supabase.auth.getUser();

    if (authError || !user || !user.email || !ADMIN_EMAILS.has(user.email.toLowerCase())) {
      return NextResponse.json({ error: "גישה נדחתה. דרושות הרשאות מנהל." }, { status: 403 });
    }

    const { searchParams } = new URL(req.url);
    const body = await req.json().catch(() => ({}));
    const backupId = searchParams.get("id") || body.backupId || body.id;

    if (!backupId) {
      return NextResponse.json({ error: "מזהה גיבוי חסר למחיקה." }, { status: 400 });
    }

    const { error: delErr } = await supabase
      .from("system_backups")
      .delete()
      .eq("id", backupId);

    if (delErr) {
      throw new Error("שגיאה במחיקת גיבוי המערכת: " + delErr.message);
    }

    return NextResponse.json({
      ok: true,
      message: "גיבוי המערכת נמחק בהצלחה מה-NAS.",
    });
  } catch (err: any) {
    console.error("[System Backup DELETE Error]", err);
    return NextResponse.json(
      { error: err.message || "שגיאה במחיקת הגיבוי" },
      { status: 500 }
    );
  }
}
```

## `app/api/admin/users/route.ts`

```ts
import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from "next/server";
import { createSupabaseServerClient } from "@/lib/supabase/server";
import fs from "fs";
import path from "path";

export const dynamic = "force-dynamic";

const ADMIN_EMAILS = new Set(["michael.liarzi@gmail.com"]);
const DELETED_BACKUPS_DIR = path.join(process.cwd(), "backups", "deleted-users");
const SIX_MONTHS_MS = 180 * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000;

function escapeSqlValue(val: any): string {
  if (val === null || val === undefined) return "NULL";
  if (typeof val === "number") return val.toString();
  if (typeof val === "boolean") return val ? "TRUE" : "FALSE";
  if (val instanceof Date) return `'${val.toISOString()}'`;
  if (typeof val === "object") return `'${JSON.stringify(val).replace(/'/g, "''")}'`;
  return `'${String(val).replace(/'/g, "''")}'`;
}

async function fetchAllUserReports(supabase: any, userId: string): Promise<any[]> {
  const allReports: any[] = [];
  const pageSize = 1000;
  let from = 0;
  while (true) {
    const { data, error } = await supabase
      .from("reports")
      .select("*")
      .eq("user_id", userId)
      .order("date", { ascending: true })
      .range(from, from + pageSize - 1);

    if (error) {
      throw new Error("שגיאה בשליפת דיווחי השעות: " + error.message);
    }
    if (!data || data.length === 0) break;
    allReports.push(...data);
    if (data.length < pageSize) break;
    from += pageSize;
  }
  return allReports;
}

/** Clean up physical files in deleted-users older than 6 months */
function cleanupExpiredDeletedFiles() {
  try {
    if (!fs.existsSync(DELETED_BACKUPS_DIR)) return;
    const now = Date.now();
    const files = fs.readdirSync(DELETED_BACKUPS_DIR);
    for (const f of files) {
      const fullPath = path.join(DELETED_BACKUPS_DIR, f);
      const stat = fs.statSync(fullPath);
      if (now - stat.mtimeMs > SIX_MONTHS_MS) {
        console.log(`[Backup Retention] Removing 6-month expired deleted-user backup: ${f}`);
        fs.unlinkSync(fullPath);
      }
    }
  } catch (e) {
    console.error("[Backup Retention] Error cleaning up expired files:", e);
  }
}

/** GET /api/admin/users - List all users (admin only) */
export async function GET() {
  try {
    const supabase = createSupabaseServerClient();
    const {
      data: { user },
      error: authError,
    } = await supabase.auth.getUser();

    if (authError || !user || !user.email || !ADMIN_EMAILS.has(user.email.toLowerCase())) {
      return NextResponse.json({ error: "גישה נדחתה. דרושות הרשאות מנהל." }, { status: 403 });
    }

    const { data: profiles, error: dbError } = await supabase
      .from("profiles")
      .select("id, email, annual_vacation_quota, created_at, reports(count)")
      .order("created_at", { ascending: false });

    // Fetch all backups for each user from user_backups table
    const userBackupsMap = new Map<string, any[]>();
    try {
      const { data: allBackups } = await supabase
        .from("user_backups")
        .select("id, user_id, filename, reports_count, standards_count, terms_count, backup_type, file_size_kb, created_at")
        .order("created_at", { ascending: false });

      if (allBackups) {
        for (const b of allBackups) {
          const list = userBackupsMap.get(b.user_id) || [];
          list.push(b);
          userBackupsMap.set(b.user_id, list);
        }
      }
    } catch (e) {
      console.warn("Could not fetch user_backups:", e);
    }

    if (dbError) {
      // Fallback without reports join if relation issue occurs
      const { data: fallbackProfiles, error: fallbackError } = await supabase
        .from("profiles")
        .select("id, email, annual_vacation_quota, created_at")
        .order("created_at", { ascending: false });

      if (fallbackError) {
        return NextResponse.json({ error: fallbackError.message }, { status: 500 });
      }

      const mapped = (fallbackProfiles || []).map((p: any) => {
        const uBackups = userBackupsMap.get(p.id) || [];
        return {
          id: p.id,
          email: p.email,
          annual_vacation_quota: p.annual_vacation_quota,
          created_at: p.created_at,
          reports_count: null,
          last_backup_at: uBackups[0]?.created_at || null,
          backups: uBackups,
          is_current_auth_user: p.id === user.id,
        };
      });
      return NextResponse.json({ users: mapped });
    }

    const mapped = (profiles || []).map((p: any) => {
      const uBackups = userBackupsMap.get(p.id) || [];
      return {
        id: p.id,
        email: p.email,
        annual_vacation_quota: p.annual_vacation_quota,
        created_at: p.created_at,
        reports_count: p.reports?.[0]?.count ?? 0,
        last_backup_at: uBackups[0]?.created_at || null,
        backups: uBackups,
        is_current_auth_user: p.id === user.id,
      };
    });

    return NextResponse.json({ users: mapped });
  } catch (e) {
    return NextResponse.json(
      { error: e instanceof Error ? e.message : "שגיאה בטעינת משתמשים" },
      { status: 500 }
    );
  }
}

/** DELETE /api/admin/users - Pre-backup (6-month retention) and delete user */
export async function DELETE(req: NextRequest) {
  try {
    const supabase = createSupabaseServerClient();
    const {
      data: { user },
      error: authError,
    } = await supabase.auth.getUser();

    if (authError || !user || !user.email || !ADMIN_EMAILS.has(user.email.toLowerCase())) {
      return NextResponse.json({ error: "גישה נדחתה. דרושות הרשאות מנהל." }, { status: 403 });
    }

    const body = await req.json().catch(() => ({}));
    let email = typeof body.email === "string" ? body.email.trim() : null;
    let userId = typeof body.userId === "string" ? body.userId.trim() : null;

    if (!email && !userId) {
      return NextResponse.json({ error: "נא לספק מזהה משתמש או כתובת אימייל למחיקה." }, { status: 400 });
    }

    // Safety guard: Never allow deleting the currently logged-in active user!
    if (userId && userId === user.id) {
      return NextResponse.json({ error: "פעולה חסומה: לא ניתן למחוק את החשבון הפעיל המחובר כעת." }, { status: 400 });
    }
    if (email && email.toLowerCase() === user.email.toLowerCase()) {
      return NextResponse.json({ error: "פעולה חסומה: לא ניתן למחוק את כתובת האימייל של החשבון המחובר." }, { status: 400 });
    }

    // 1. Resolve user profile and details for pre-deletion backup
    let profileQuery = supabase.from("profiles").select("*");
    if (userId) {
      profileQuery = profileQuery.eq("id", userId);
    } else if (email) {
      profileQuery = profileQuery.eq("email", email);
    }
    const { data: profiles } = await profileQuery;
    const targetProfile = profiles?.[0];

    const resolvedUserId = targetProfile?.id || userId;
    const resolvedEmail = targetProfile?.email || email || resolvedUserId;

    // Additional safety guard
    if (resolvedUserId === user.id || resolvedEmail.toLowerCase() === user.email.toLowerCase()) {
      return NextResponse.json({ error: "פעולה חסומה: לא ניתן למחוק את החשבון הפעיל." }, { status: 400 });
    }

    // 2. Fetch all reports and workday_standards for backup before deletion
    let userReports: any[] = [];
    let userStandards: any[] = [];
    if (resolvedUserId) {
      userReports = await fetchAllUserReports(supabase, resolvedUserId);

      const { data: stds } = await supabase
        .from("workday_standards")
        .select("*")
        .eq("user_id", resolvedUserId);
      userStandards = stds || [];
    }

    // 3. Generate SQL & JSON pre-deletion backup
    const now = new Date();
    const expiresAt = new Date(now.getTime() + SIX_MONTHS_MS);
    const dateStr = now.toISOString().slice(0, 10);
    const timeStr = now.toTimeString().slice(0, 8).replace(/:/g, "");
    const safeEmail = resolvedEmail.replace(/[^a-zA-Z0-9_-]/g, "_");

    const backupFileName = `deleted-user-${safeEmail}-${dateStr}-${timeStr}.sql`;

    const sqlLines = [
      `-- ============================================================`,
      `-- PRE-DELETION USER BACKUP (Retention: 6 Months until ${expiresAt.toISOString().slice(0, 10)})`,
      `-- User: ${resolvedEmail}`,
      `-- User ID: ${resolvedUserId}`,
      `-- Deleted At: ${now.toISOString()}`,
      `-- Expires At: ${expiresAt.toISOString()}`,
      `-- Total Reports: ${userReports.length}`,
      `-- Total Standards: ${userStandards.length}`,
      `-- ============================================================`,
      `BEGIN;`,
      ``,
    ];

    if (targetProfile) {
      sqlLines.push(`-- Profile`);
      sqlLines.push(`INSERT INTO public.profiles ("id", "email", "annual_vacation_quota") VALUES (${escapeSqlValue(targetProfile.id)}, ${escapeSqlValue(targetProfile.email)}, ${escapeSqlValue(targetProfile.annual_vacation_quota)}) ON CONFLICT (id) DO NOTHING;`);
      sqlLines.push(``);
    }

    if (userStandards.length > 0) {
      sqlLines.push(`-- Workday Standards (${userStandards.length} rows)`);
      for (const std of userStandards) {
        const keys = Object.keys(std);
        const cols = keys.map((k) => `"${k}"`).join(", ");
        const vals = keys.map((k) => escapeSqlValue(std[k])).join(", ");
        sqlLines.push(`INSERT INTO public.workday_standards (${cols}) VALUES (${vals});`);
      }
      sqlLines.push(``);
    }

    if (userReports.length > 0) {
      sqlLines.push(`-- Reports (${userReports.length} rows)`);
      for (const rep of userReports) {
        const keys = Object.keys(rep);
        const cols = keys.map((k) => `"${k}"`).join(", ");
        const vals = keys.map((k) => escapeSqlValue(rep[k])).join(", ");
        sqlLines.push(`INSERT INTO public.reports (${cols}) VALUES (${vals});`);
      }
      sqlLines.push(``);
    }

    sqlLines.push(`COMMIT;`);
    const backupSql = sqlLines.join("\n");

    // Optional write file to disk (non-blocking for serverless/read-only environments)
    try {
      const targetDir = process.env.VERCEL ? "/tmp" : DELETED_BACKUPS_DIR;
      if (!fs.existsSync(targetDir)) {
        fs.mkdirSync(targetDir, { recursive: true });
      }
      fs.writeFileSync(path.join(targetDir, backupFileName), backupSql, "utf8");
    } catch (diskErr) {
      console.warn("[Pre-deletion Backup] Could not write to disk (safe to ignore on serverless):", diskErr);
    }

    // Save to PostgreSQL deleted_user_backups table for 6 months
    try {
      await supabase.from("deleted_user_backups").insert({
        user_id: resolvedUserId,
        email: resolvedEmail,
        reports_count: userReports.length,
        backup_data: {
          profile: targetProfile,
          standards: userStandards,
          reports: userReports,
        },
        backup_sql: backupSql,
        deleted_at: now.toISOString(),
        expires_at: expiresAt.toISOString(),
      });

      // Run automated DB cleanup for expired backups older than 6 months
      await supabase.rpc("cleanup_expired_deleted_user_backups");
    } catch (dbSaveErr) {
      console.warn("[Pre-deletion Backup] Could not save to DB table (saved to disk):", dbSaveErr);
    }

    // Clean up physical disk files older than 6 months
    cleanupExpiredDeletedFiles();

    // 4. Perform actual user deletion via RPC
    let rpcResponse: any;
    if (resolvedUserId) {
      rpcResponse = await supabase.rpc("delete_user_by_id", { target_user_id: resolvedUserId });
    } else {
      rpcResponse = await supabase.rpc("delete_user_by_email", { target_email: resolvedEmail });
    }

    if (rpcResponse.error) {
      return NextResponse.json({ error: rpcResponse.error.message }, { status: 500 });
    }

    const result = rpcResponse.data;
    if (!result?.success) {
      return NextResponse.json({ error: result?.error || "המחיקה נכשלה" }, { status: 400 });
    }

    return NextResponse.json({
      ok: true,
      message: `המשתמש ${resolvedEmail} נמחק בהצלחה. בוצע גיבוי מקדים של ${userReports.length} דיווחים שיישמר למשך 6 חודשים.`,
    });
  } catch (e) {
    return NextResponse.json(
      { error: e instanceof Error ? e.message : "שגיאה במחיקת המשתמש" },
      { status: 500 }
    );
  }
}
```

## `app/api/employment-terms/route.ts`

```ts
import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from "next/server";
import { getEmploymentTerms, saveEmploymentTerm, deleteEmploymentTerm } from "@/lib/data";
import { EmploymentTerm, EmploymentType } from "@/lib/types";

export const dynamic = "force-dynamic";

const VALID_TYPES: EmploymentType[] = ["monthly_overtime", "global", "hourly", "custom"];

export async function GET() {
  try {
    const terms = await getEmploymentTerms();
    return NextResponse.json({ terms });
  } catch (e) {
    return NextResponse.json(
      { error: e instanceof Error ? e.message : "שגיאה באחזור תנאי העסקה" },
      { status: 500 }
    );
  }
}

export async function POST(req: NextRequest) {
  try {
    const body = await req.json();
    const {
      id,
      name,
      employmentType,
      startDate,
      endDate,
      jobScopePct,
      dailyStandardSunWed,
      dailyStandardThu,
      overtimeEligible,
      hourlyRate,
      notes,
    } = body;

    // Validation
    if (!startDate || !/^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(startDate)) {
      return NextResponse.json({ error: "תאריך התחלה לא תקין" }, { status: 400 });
    }

    if (endDate && !/^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(endDate)) {
      return NextResponse.json({ error: "תאריך סיום לא תקין" }, { status: 400 });
    }

    if (endDate && endDate < startDate) {
      return NextResponse.json({ error: "תאריך הסיום אינו יכול להיות לפני תאריך ההתחלה" }, { status: 400 });
    }

    if (!VALID_TYPES.includes(employmentType)) {
      return NextResponse.json({ error: "סוג העסקה אינו תקין" }, { status: 400 });
    }

    const parsedScope = Number(jobScopePct ?? 100);
    if (isNaN(parsedScope) || parsedScope <= 0 || parsedScope > 200) {
      return NextResponse.json({ error: "היקף משרה אינו תקין (1%-200%)" }, { status: 400 });
    }

    const parsedSunWed = Number(dailyStandardSunWed ?? 9.0);
    const parsedThu = Number(dailyStandardThu ?? 8.5);
    if (isNaN(parsedSunWed) || parsedSunWed < 0 || parsedSunWed > 24 || isNaN(parsedThu) || parsedThu < 0 || parsedThu > 24) {
      return NextResponse.json({ error: "שעות תקן יומיות אינן תקינות (0-24)" }, { status: 400 });
    }

    const parsedHourlyRate =
      hourlyRate !== undefined && hourlyRate !== null && String(hourlyRate).trim() !== ""
        ? Number(hourlyRate)
        : null;
    if (parsedHourlyRate !== null && (isNaN(parsedHourlyRate) || parsedHourlyRate < 0)) {
      return NextResponse.json({ error: "ערך שעה אינו תקין" }, { status: 400 });
    }

    const termInput: EmploymentTerm = {
      id: id || undefined,
      name: String(name || "תנאי העסקה").trim().slice(0, 100),
      employmentType,
      startDate,
      endDate: endDate || null,
      jobScopePct: Math.round(parsedScope * 100) / 100,
      dailyStandardSunWed: Math.round(parsedSunWed * 100) / 100,
      dailyStandardThu: Math.round(parsedThu * 100) / 100,
      overtimeEligible: Boolean(overtimeEligible),
      hourlyRate: parsedHourlyRate !== null ? Math.round(parsedHourlyRate * 100) / 100 : null,
      notes: notes ? String(notes).slice(0, 500) : "",
    };

    const saved = await saveEmploymentTerm(termInput);
    return NextResponse.json({ ok: true, term: saved });
  } catch (e) {
    return NextResponse.json(
      { error: e instanceof Error ? e.message : "שגיאה בשמירת תנאי העסקה" },
      { status: 500 }
    );
  }
}

export async function DELETE(req: NextRequest) {
  try {
    const { searchParams } = new URL(req.url);
    const id = searchParams.get("id");
    if (!id) {
      return NextResponse.json({ error: "מזהה תנאי העסקה חסר" }, { status: 400 });
    }

    await deleteEmploymentTerm(id);
    return NextResponse.json({ ok: true });
  } catch (e) {
    return NextResponse.json(
      { error: e instanceof Error ? e.message : "שגיאה במחיקת תנאי העסקה" },
      { status: 500 }
    );
  }
}
```

## `app/api/holidays/route.ts`

```ts
import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from "next/server";
import { HolidaySetting } from "@/lib/settingsStore";

export const dynamic = "force-dynamic";

// In-memory cache for fetched years to prevent redundant external calls
const holidayCache = new Map<string, HolidaySetting[]>();

function cleanHolidayName(rawName: string): string {
  let name = rawName.trim();
  // Remove Hebrew year number e.g. " 5787" or " 5786"
  name = name.replace(/\s+5\d{3}$/, "");
  // Replace special Unicode Hebrew marks with standard quotes
  name = name.replace(/״/g, '"').replace(/׳/g, "'");
  return name;
}

function determineCategory(name: string, titleOrig: string, yomtov: boolean, subcat?: string): string {
  if (name.startsWith("ערב ") || titleOrig.toLowerCase().startsWith("erev ")) {
    return "ערב חג";
  }
  if (name.includes("חוה\"מ") || name.includes("חול המועד")) {
    return "חול המועד";
  }
  if (yomtov || name.includes("יום העצמאות") || name.includes("פורים") || name.includes("שמחת תורה")) {
    return "חג";
  }
  // Default to חג for other holidays (e.g. minor holidays / fasts)
  return "חג";
}

/**
 * Fetch holidays from Hebcal for a given Gregorian year (in Israel schedule)
 */
async function fetchHebcalYear(year: number, mode: "all" | "work" = "work"): Promise<HolidaySetting[]> {
  const cacheKey = `${year}_${mode}`;
  if (holidayCache.has(cacheKey)) {
    return holidayCache.get(cacheKey)!;
  }

  // Hebcal API with Israel schedule (i=on) and Hebrew titles (lg=he)
  const url = `https://www.hebcal.com/hebcal?v=1&cfg=json&maj=on&min=on&mod=on&nx=on&year=${year}&i=on&lg=he`;
  const res = await fetch(url, { next: { revalidate: 86400 } });
  if (!res.ok) {
    throw new Error(`שגיאה בפנייה ל-Hebcal: ${res.statusText}`);
  }

  const data = await res.json();
  const items: any[] = data.items || [];
  const results: HolidaySetting[] = [];
  const seenDates = new Set<string>();

  for (const item of items) {
    // We only care about holiday events (skip rosh chodesh, candle lighting, etc.)
    if (item.category !== "holiday") continue;

    const rawHebrew = item.hebrew || item.title || "";
    const name = cleanHolidayName(rawHebrew);
    const titleOrig = item.title_orig || item.title || "";
    const isYomtov = Boolean(item.yomtov);
    const category = determineCategory(name, titleOrig, isYomtov, item.subcat);

    // If mode is 'work', filter out daily candle counts like "חנוכה: ב' נרות"
    if (mode === "work") {
      if (name.includes("נרות") || name.includes("נר") || name.includes("חג הבנות")) {
        continue;
      }
      // Skip fast days that aren't national holidays if work mode
      if (item.subcat === "fast" && !name.includes("כיפור")) {
        continue;
      }
    }

    // In Hebcal, some multi-day events or items share dates. Keep the most significant one per date.
    const dateKey = `${item.date}_${category}`;
    if (!seenDates.has(dateKey)) {
      seenDates.add(dateKey);
      results.push({
        date: item.date,
        name,
        category,
      });
    }
  }

  // Sort chronologically
  results.sort((a, b) => a.date.localeCompare(b.date));

  holidayCache.set(cacheKey, results);
  return results;
}

export async function GET(req: NextRequest) {
  try {
    const { searchParams } = new URL(req.url);
    const yearParam = searchParams.get("year");
    const startYearParam = searchParams.get("startYear");
    const endYearParam = searchParams.get("endYear");
    const mode = (searchParams.get("mode") === "all" ? "all" : "work") as "all" | "work";

    if (startYearParam && endYearParam) {
      const start = parseInt(startYearParam, 10);
      const end = parseInt(endYearParam, 10);
      if (isNaN(start) || isNaN(end) || start < 2000 || end > 2100 || start > end) {
        return NextResponse.json({ error: "טווח שנים לא תקין" }, { status: 400 });
      }

      let combined: HolidaySetting[] = [];
      for (let y = start; y <= end; y++) {
        const list = await fetchHebcalYear(y, mode);
        combined = combined.concat(list);
      }

      return NextResponse.json({
        ok: true,
        count: combined.length,
        holidays: combined,
      });
    }

    const currentYear = new Date().getFullYear();
    const year = yearParam ? parseInt(yearParam, 10) : currentYear;

    if (isNaN(year) || year < 2000 || year > 2100) {
      return NextResponse.json({ error: "שנה לא תקינה" }, { status: 400 });
    }

    const holidays = await fetchHebcalYear(year, mode);

    return NextResponse.json({
      ok: true,
      year,
      count: holidays.length,
      holidays,
    });
  } catch (e) {
    return NextResponse.json(
      { error: e instanceof Error ? e.message : "שגיאה באחזור חגים מ-Hebcal" },
      { status: 500 }
    );
  }
}
```

## `app/api/import/route.ts`

```ts
import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from "next/server";
import { createSupabaseServerClient } from "@/lib/supabase/server";
import { ddmmyyyyToIso } from "@/lib/date";

export const dynamic = "force-dynamic";

function parseCsvLine(line: string): string[] {
  const result: string[] = [];
  let current = "";
  let inQuotes = false;
  for (let i = 0; i < line.length; i++) {
    const char = line[i];
    if (char === '"') {
      inQuotes = !inQuotes;
    } else if (char === "," && !inQuotes) {
      result.push(current.trim());
      current = "";
    } else {
      current += char;
    }
  }
  result.push(current.trim());
  return result;
}

function isValidIsoDate(s: string): boolean {
  return /^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(s);
}

function padTime(t: string): string {
  const m = /^(\d{1,2}):(\d{2})(?::\d{2})?$/.exec(t.trim());
  return m ? `${m[1].padStart(2, "0")}:${m[2]}` : "";
}

function parseCellTime(val: any): string {
  if (val === null || val === undefined || val === "") return "";
  if (typeof val === "number" && val >= 0 && val <= 1) {
    // Excel time fraction of day
    const totalMinutes = Math.round(val * 24 * 60);
    const h = String(Math.floor(totalMinutes / 60)).padStart(2, "0");
    const m = String(totalMinutes % 60).padStart(2, "0");
    return `${h}:${m}`;
  }
  if (val instanceof Date) {
    const h = String(val.getHours()).padStart(2, "0");
    const m = String(val.getMinutes()).padStart(2, "0");
    return `${h}:${m}`;
  }
  return padTime(String(val));
}

function cleanQuotes(val: any): string {
  if (val === null || val === undefined) return "";
  let s = String(val).trim();
  s = s.replace(/""/g, '"');
  s = s.replace(/^["']|["']$/g, "").trim();
  return s;
}

function parseCellDate(val: any): string {
  if (val === null || val === undefined || val === "") return "";
  if (typeof val === "number" && val > 1000) {
    // Excel serial date (days since 1899-12-30) - compute using UTC day slots to prevent timezone slippage
    const utcDays = Math.floor(val - 25569);
    const d = new Date(utcDays * 86400 * 1000);
    const y = d.getUTCFullYear();
    const m = String(d.getUTCMonth() + 1).padStart(2, "0");
    const day = String(d.getUTCDate()).padStart(2, "0");
    return `${y}-${m}-${day}`;
  }
  if (val instanceof Date && !isNaN(val.getTime())) {
    const y = val.getFullYear();
    const m = String(val.getMonth() + 1).padStart(2, "0");
    const d = String(val.getDate()).padStart(2, "0");
    return `${y}-${m}-${d}`;
  }
  const s = String(val).trim();
  if (isValidIsoDate(s)) return s;
  const iso = ddmmyyyyToIso(s);
  if (iso) return iso;
  return "";
}

function sanitizeCsvField(val: string): string {
  let s = cleanQuotes(val);
  if (/^[=+\-@\t\r]/.test(s)) {
    s = "'" + s;
  }
  return s;
}

const norm = (s: any) => String(s || "").trim().toLowerCase().replace(/["'_ \t\r\n\ufeff]/g, "");

/**
 * POST /api/import — upload historic CSV or Excel data
 */
export async function POST(req: NextRequest) {
  try {
    const supabase = createSupabaseServerClient();
    const {
      data: { user },
      error: authError,
    } = await supabase.auth.getUser();

    if (authError || !user) {
      return NextResponse.json({ error: "משתמש לא מחובר" }, { status: 401 });
    }

    const userId = user.id;

    // Enforce 10MB maximum payload size
    const MAX_PAYLOAD_BYTES = 10 * 1024 * 1024;
    const contentLength = Number(req.headers.get("content-length") || "0");
    if (contentLength > MAX_PAYLOAD_BYTES) {
      return NextResponse.json(
        { error: "גודל הקובץ חורג מהמגבלה המותרת (עד 10MB)" },
        { status: 413 }
      );
    }

    const rawBody = await req.text();
    if (!rawBody || rawBody.trim().length === 0) {
      return NextResponse.json({ error: "קובץ ריק או לא תקין" }, { status: 400 });
    }

    let rawRows: any[][] = [];

    // Check if JSON payload (from Excel sheet_to_json or frontend JSON upload)
    const contentType = req.headers.get("content-type") || "";
    if (contentType.includes("application/json") || rawBody.trim().startsWith("{")) {
      try {
        const json = JSON.parse(rawBody);
        if (Array.isArray(json.rows)) {
          rawRows = json.rows;
        }
      } catch (jsonErr) {
        console.warn("Could not parse as JSON, falling back to CSV line parser:", jsonErr);
      }
    }

    // Fallback: CSV text splitting with XLSX
    if (rawRows.length === 0) {
      try {
        const XLSX = require("xlsx");
        const workbook = XLSX.read(rawBody, { type: "string", raw: true });
        const sheet = workbook.Sheets[workbook.SheetNames[0]];
        rawRows = XLSX.utils.sheet_to_json(sheet, { header: 1, raw: true });
      } catch {
        const lines = rawBody.split(/\r?\n/);
        rawRows = lines.map((l) => parseCsvLine(l));
      }
    }

    // Filter out completely blank lines
    rawRows = rawRows.filter((r) => Array.isArray(r) && r.some((c) => c !== null && c !== undefined && String(c).trim() !== ""));

    if (rawRows.length < 2) {
      return NextResponse.json({ error: "קובץ ריק או חסר שורות נתונים" }, { status: 400 });
    }

    // 1. Detect dynamic column positions by analyzing header row
    const firstRow = rawRows[0];
    let dateIdx = -1;
    let entryIdx = -1;
    let exitIdx = -1;
    let classIdx = -1;
    let dayTypeIdx = -1;
    let hoursIdx = -1;
    let vacationIdx = -1;
    let sickIdx = -1;
    let reserveIdx = -1;
    let notesIdx = -1;
    let uniqueNotesIdx = -1;
    let orderTypeIdx = -1;

    firstRow.forEach((col, idx) => {
      const n = norm(col);
      if (n === "תאריך" || n === "date") dateIdx = idx;
      else if (
        n === "כניסה" ||
        n === "entry" ||
        n === "start" ||
        n.includes("שעתהתחלה") ||
        n.includes("התחלה") ||
        n.includes("שעתכניסה")
      ) entryIdx = idx;
      else if (
        n === "יציאה" ||
        n === "exit" ||
        n === "end" ||
        n.includes("שעתסיום") ||
        n.includes("סיום") ||
        n.includes("שעתיציאה")
      ) exitIdx = idx;
      else if (n.includes("סוגיום")) dayTypeIdx = idx;
      else if (
        n === "סיווג" ||
        n === "classification" ||
        n === "type" ||
        n.includes("תאורנוכחות") ||
        n.includes("תיאורנוכחות") ||
        n.includes("תאורהעדרות")
      ) classIdx = idx;
      else if (n.includes("סךשעות") || n.includes("סהכשעות") || n === "hours") hoursIdx = idx;
      else if (n.includes("חופש") || n.includes("חופשה") || n === "vacation" || n === "vacationdays") vacationIdx = idx;
      else if (n.includes("מחלה") || n === "sick" || n === "sickdays") sickIdx = idx;
      else if (n.includes("מילואים") || n === "reserve" || n === "reservedays") reserveIdx = idx;
      else if (n.includes("הערותייחודיות") || n.includes("הערהייחודית") || n.includes("הערותיחודיות") || n === "uniquenotes" || n === "uniquenote") uniqueNotesIdx = idx;
      else if (n.includes("הערות") || n.includes("הערה") || n === "notes" || n === "note") notesIdx = idx;
      else if (n.includes("סוגצו") || n === "צו" || n === "ordertype") orderTypeIdx = idx;
    });

    const hasHeaderMapping = dateIdx !== -1;
    const isLegacy = !hasHeaderMapping && firstRow.length >= 16;
    const startIndex = hasHeaderMapping ? 1 : (parseCellDate(firstRow[0]) ? 0 : 1);

    // Group rows by parsed date to handle multi-shift / split punch days
    const dateGroups = new Map<string, any[]>();
    let skippedCount = 0;

    for (let i = startIndex; i < rawRows.length; i++) {
      const cols = rawRows[i];
      if (!cols || cols.length < 2) {
        skippedCount++;
        continue;
      }

      let dateRaw: any = "";
      let entryRaw: any = "";
      let exitRaw: any = "";
      let classificationRaw: any = "";
      let dayTypeRaw: any = "";
      let totalHoursRaw: any = 0;
      let vacationDays = 0;
      let sickDays = 0;
      let reserveDays = 0;
      let notesRaw: any = "";
      let uniqueNotesRaw: any = "";
      let orderTypeRaw: any = "";

      if (hasHeaderMapping) {
        dateRaw = dateIdx !== -1 ? cols[dateIdx] : "";
        entryRaw = entryIdx !== -1 ? cols[entryIdx] : "";
        exitRaw = exitIdx !== -1 ? cols[exitIdx] : "";
        classificationRaw = classIdx !== -1 ? cols[classIdx] : "";
        dayTypeRaw = dayTypeIdx !== -1 ? cols[dayTypeIdx] : "";
        totalHoursRaw = hoursIdx !== -1 ? parseFloat(cols[hoursIdx]) || 0 : 0;
        vacationDays = vacationIdx !== -1 ? parseFloat(cols[vacationIdx]) || 0 : 0;
        sickDays = sickIdx !== -1 ? parseFloat(cols[sickIdx]) || 0 : 0;
        reserveDays = reserveIdx !== -1 ? parseFloat(cols[reserveIdx]) || 0 : 0;
        notesRaw = notesIdx !== -1 ? cols[notesIdx] : "";
        uniqueNotesRaw = uniqueNotesIdx !== -1 ? cols[uniqueNotesIdx] : "";
        orderTypeRaw = orderTypeIdx !== -1 ? cols[orderTypeIdx] : "";
      } else if (isLegacy) {
        dateRaw = cols[0];
        entryRaw = cols[1];
        exitRaw = cols[2];
        vacationDays = parseFloat(cols[9]) || 0;
        reserveDays = parseFloat(cols[10]) || 0;
        sickDays = parseFloat(cols[11]) || 0;
        classificationRaw = cols[12] || "";
        notesRaw = cols[13] || "";
        uniqueNotesRaw = cols[14] || "";
        orderTypeRaw = cols[15] || "";
      } else {
        dateRaw = cols[0];
        entryRaw = cols[1];
        exitRaw = cols[2];
        classificationRaw = cols[3] || "";
        vacationDays = parseFloat(cols[4]) || 0;
        sickDays = parseFloat(cols[5]) || 0;
        reserveDays = parseFloat(cols[6]) || 0;
        notesRaw = cols[7] || "";
        uniqueNotesRaw = cols[8] || "";
        orderTypeRaw = cols[9] || "";
      }

      if (String(classificationRaw).trim() === "הגדרת תקן") {
        continue;
      }

      const date = parseCellDate(dateRaw);
      if (!date) {
        skippedCount++;
        continue;
      }

      if (!dateGroups.has(date)) {
        dateGroups.set(date, []);
      }
      dateGroups.get(date)!.push({
        entryRaw,
        exitRaw,
        classificationRaw,
        dayTypeRaw,
        totalHoursRaw,
        vacationDays,
        sickDays,
        reserveDays,
        notesRaw,
        uniqueNotesRaw,
        orderTypeRaw,
      });
    }

    const parsedRows: any[] = [];

    for (const [date, items] of Array.from(dateGroups.entries())) {
      const dateObj = new Date(`${date}T00:00:00`);
      const dow = dateObj.getDay();

      // Check if completely empty placeholder row
      const hasAnyHours = items.some((it) => it.entryRaw || it.exitRaw || it.totalHoursRaw > 0);
      const hasAnyNotes = items.some((it) => it.notesRaw || it.uniqueNotesRaw);
      const hasAbsenceDays = items.some((it) => it.vacationDays > 0 || it.sickDays > 0 || it.reserveDays > 0);
      const hasSpecialDesc = items.some(
        (it) => it.dayTypeRaw || (it.classificationRaw && it.classificationRaw !== "עבודה")
      );

      if (!hasAnyHours && !hasAnyNotes && !hasAbsenceDays && !hasSpecialDesc) {
        // Skip empty future / placeholder lines
        continue;
      }

      // Extract valid shifts for this day
      const validShifts: Array<{ entry: string; exit: string; description?: string }> = [];
      for (const it of items) {
        const ent = parseCellTime(it.entryRaw);
        const ext = parseCellTime(it.exitRaw);
        if (ent || ext) {
          validShifts.push({
            entry: ent,
            exit: ext,
            description: cleanQuotes(it.classificationRaw || it.dayTypeRaw) || undefined,
          });
        }
      }

      // Classify the day
      const firstDesc = items.map((it) => cleanQuotes(it.classificationRaw)).find(Boolean) || "";
      const firstDayType = items.map((it) => cleanQuotes(it.dayTypeRaw)).find(Boolean) || "";
      let classificationClean = "עבודה";

      if (
        firstDayType.includes("חוה\"מ") ||
        firstDayType.includes("חוהמ") ||
        firstDayType.includes("חול המועד") ||
        firstDesc.includes("חול המועד")
      ) {
        classificationClean = "חול המועד";
      } else if (
        firstDayType.includes("ערב חג") ||
        firstDayType.includes("הושע\"ר") ||
        firstDayType.includes("הושער")
      ) {
        classificationClean = "ערב חג";
      } else if (
        firstDayType.includes("חג") ||
        firstDayType.includes("ש' תורה") ||
        firstDayType.includes("שמחת תורה") ||
        firstDayType.includes("סוכות") ||
        firstDayType.includes("פסח") ||
        firstDayType.includes("ראש השנה") ||
        firstDayType.includes("כיפור")
      ) {
        classificationClean = "חג";
      } else if (
        firstDesc.includes("עבודה במילואים") ||
        firstDayType.includes("עבודה במילואים")
      ) {
        classificationClean = "עבודה במילואים";
      } else if (
        firstDesc.includes("מילואים") ||
        firstDayType.includes("מילואים")
      ) {
        classificationClean = "מילואים";
      } else if (
        firstDesc.includes("מחלה") ||
        firstDayType.includes("מחלה")
      ) {
        classificationClean = "מחלה";
      } else if (
        firstDesc.includes("חופש") ||
        firstDesc.includes("חופשה") ||
        firstDesc.includes("היעדרות כללית")
      ) {
        classificationClean = "חופש";
      } else if (
        firstDesc.includes("נוכחות") ||
        firstDesc.includes("עבודה") ||
        firstDesc.includes("שעון")
      ) {
        classificationClean = "עבודה";
      } else if (firstDesc) {
        classificationClean = firstDesc;
      } else if (dow === 5 || dow === 6) {
        classificationClean = 'סופ"ש';
      }

      classificationClean = sanitizeCsvField(classificationClean);

      // Absence days computation
      let vacationDays = items.reduce((acc, it) => acc + (it.vacationDays || 0), 0);
      let sickDays = items.reduce((acc, it) => acc + (it.sickDays || 0), 0);
      let reserveDays = items.reduce((acc, it) => acc + (it.reserveDays || 0), 0);

      if (classificationClean === "חופש" && vacationDays === 0 && validShifts.length === 0) {
        vacationDays = 1;
      }
      if (classificationClean === "מחלה" && sickDays === 0) {
        sickDays = 1;
      }
      if (classificationClean === "מילואים" && reserveDays === 0) {
        reserveDays = 1;
      }

      const isAbsence =
        classificationClean !== "עבודה" && classificationClean !== "עבודה במילואים";

      let entryClean: string | null = null;
      let exitClean: string | null = null;
      let shiftsPayload: Array<{ entry: string; exit: string; description?: string }> = [];

      if (!isAbsence && validShifts.length > 0) {
        entryClean = validShifts[0].entry || null;
        exitClean = validShifts[validShifts.length - 1].exit || null;
        if (validShifts.length > 1) {
          shiftsPayload = validShifts;
        }
      }

      // Combine notes
      const notesClean = items
        .map((it) => it.notesRaw)
        .filter(Boolean)
        .map((n) => cleanQuotes(n))
        .join(" ; ");
      const uniqueNotesClean = items
        .map((it) => it.uniqueNotesRaw)
        .filter(Boolean)
        .map((n) => cleanQuotes(n))
        .join(" ; ");

      const isReserveImport =
        classificationClean === "מילואים" ||
        classificationClean === "עבודה במילואים" ||
        reserveDays > 0;

      const orderTypeClean =
        items.map((it) => cleanQuotes(it.orderTypeRaw)).find(Boolean) ||
        (isReserveImport
          ? /צו\s*8/.test(notesClean)
            ? "צו 8"
            : "מילואים רגילים"
          : null);

      parsedRows.push({
        user_id: userId,
        date,
        entry: entryClean,
        exit: exitClean,
        shifts: shiftsPayload,
        vacation_days: vacationDays,
        sick_days: sickDays,
        reserve_days: reserveDays,
        classification: classificationClean,
        notes: notesClean ? sanitizeCsvField(notesClean.substring(0, 500)) : null,
        unique_notes: uniqueNotesClean
          ? sanitizeCsvField(uniqueNotesClean.substring(0, 500))
          : null,
        order_type: orderTypeClean ? sanitizeCsvField(orderTypeClean) : null,
      });
    }

    if (parsedRows.length === 0) {
      return NextResponse.json({ error: "לא נמצאו שורות תקינות לייבוא" }, { status: 400 });
    }

    // Batch upsert to database (up to 1000 rows at once)
    const chunkSize = 1000;
    for (let i = 0; i < parsedRows.length; i += chunkSize) {
      const chunk = parsedRows.slice(i, i + chunkSize);
      const { error: upsertError } = await supabase
        .from("reports")
        .upsert(chunk, { onConflict: "user_id, date" });

      if (upsertError) {
        throw new Error(upsertError.message || "שגיאה בכתיבה למסד הנתונים");
      }
    }

    return NextResponse.json({
      success: true,
      count: parsedRows.length,
      imported: parsedRows.length,
      skipped: skippedCount,
      format: hasHeaderMapping ? "header-detected" : isLegacy ? "legacy" : "standard",
    });
  } catch (err: any) {
    console.error("[Import Route Error]", err);
    return NextResponse.json(
      { error: err.message || "שגיאה לא צפויה במהלך הייבוא" },
      { status: 500 }
    );
  }
}
```

## `app/api/reports/batch/route.ts`

```ts
import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from "next/server";
import { saveReportsBatch } from "@/lib/data";
import { ReportInput } from "@/lib/types";

const ALLOWED_CLASSIFICATIONS = new Set<string>([
  "עבודה",
  "חופש",
  "מחלה",
  "מילואים",
  "חג",
  "ערב חג",
  "סופ\"ש",
  "סופש",
  "סופשבוע",
  "שבת",
  "חול המועד",
  "עבודה במילואים",
]);

function toMinutes(t: string): number {
  const [h, m] = t.split(":").map(Number);
  return h * 60 + m;
}

function isValidAbsence(v: unknown): boolean {
  return (
    v === undefined ||
    (typeof v === "number" && Number.isFinite(v) && v >= 0 && v <= 1)
  );
}

export const dynamic = "force-dynamic";

export async function POST(req: NextRequest) {
  let body: {
    dates: string[];
    updates: Partial<ReportInput>;
    fieldsToUpdate: string[];
  };

  try {
    body = await req.json();
  } catch {
    return NextResponse.json({ error: "גוף בקשה לא תקין" }, { status: 400 });
  }

  const { dates, updates, fieldsToUpdate } = body;

  if (!dates || !Array.isArray(dates) || dates.length === 0) {
    return NextResponse.json({ error: "רשימת תאריכים חסרה או לא תקינה" }, { status: 400 });
  }

  if (!fieldsToUpdate || !Array.isArray(fieldsToUpdate) || fieldsToUpdate.length === 0) {
    return NextResponse.json({ error: "לא נבחרו שדות לעדכון" }, { status: 400 });
  }

  const fieldsSet = new Set(fieldsToUpdate);

  // Validate updates
  if (fieldsSet.has("entry") && updates.entry) {
    const hhmm = /^([01]?\d|2[0-3]):[0-5]\d$/;
    if (!hhmm.test(updates.entry)) {
      return NextResponse.json({ error: "שעת כניסה לא תקינה (HH:mm)" }, { status: 400 });
    }
  }

  if (fieldsSet.has("exit") && updates.exit) {
    const hhmm = /^([01]?\d|2[0-3]):[0-5]\d$/;
    if (!hhmm.test(updates.exit)) {
      return NextResponse.json({ error: "שעת יציאה לא תקינה (HH:mm)" }, { status: 400 });
    }
  }

  if (fieldsSet.has("entry") || fieldsSet.has("exit")) {
    const entryVal = updates.entry;
    const exitVal = updates.exit;
    if (entryVal && exitVal && toMinutes(exitVal) <= toMinutes(entryVal)) {
      return NextResponse.json(
        { error: "שעת היציאה חייבת להיות אחרי שעת הכניסה" },
        { status: 400 }
      );
    }
  }

  if (fieldsSet.has("classification") && updates.classification) {
    if (!ALLOWED_CLASSIFICATIONS.has(updates.classification)) {
      return NextResponse.json({ error: `סיווג לא מוכר: "${updates.classification}"` }, { status: 400 });
    }
  }

  if (
    (fieldsSet.has("vacationDays") && !isValidAbsence(updates.vacationDays)) ||
    (fieldsSet.has("sickDays") && !isValidAbsence(updates.sickDays)) ||
    (fieldsSet.has("reserveDays") && !isValidAbsence(updates.reserveDays))
  ) {
    return NextResponse.json(
      { error: "ערך היעדרות חייב להיות בין 0 ל-1" },
      { status: 400 }
    );
  }

  if (fieldsSet.has("notes") && typeof updates.notes === "string" && updates.notes.length > 500) {
    return NextResponse.json({ error: "הערה ארוכה מדי (עד 500 תווים)" }, { status: 400 });
  }

  if (fieldsSet.has("uniqueNotes") && typeof updates.uniqueNotes === "string" && updates.uniqueNotes.length > 500) {
    return NextResponse.json({ error: "הערה ייחודית ארוכה מדי (עד 500 תווים)" }, { status: 400 });
  }

  try {
    await saveReportsBatch(dates, updates, fieldsToUpdate);
    return NextResponse.json({ success: true });
  } catch (e) {
    return NextResponse.json(
      { error: e instanceof Error ? e.message : "שגיאה בשמירת הדיווחים" },
      { status: 500 }
    );
  }
}
```

```ts
import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from "next/server";
import { getMissingReportsList } from "@/lib/data";

export const dynamic = "force-dynamic";

export async function GET(req: NextRequest) {
  try {
    const { searchParams } = new URL(req.url);
    const from = searchParams.get("from") || undefined;
    const to = searchParams.get("to") || undefined;
    const holidaysParam = searchParams.get("holidays");
    const customHolidayDates = holidaysParam ? holidaysParam.split(",").filter(Boolean) : undefined;

    const data = await getMissingReportsList({ from, to, customHolidayDates });
    return NextResponse.json(data);
  } catch (e) {
    return NextResponse.json(
      { error: e instanceof Error ? e.message : "שגיאה בטעינת דיווחים חסרים" },
      { status: 500 }
    );
  }
}
```

## `app/api/reports/route.ts`

```ts
import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from "next/server";
import { getMonthData, saveReport } from "@/lib/data";
import { CLASSIFICATIONS, ReportInput } from "@/lib/types";

/** Values that may legally be written to the classification column. */
const ALLOWED_CLASSIFICATIONS = new Set<string>([
  "עבודה",
  "חופש",
  "מחלה",
  "מילואים",
  "חג",
  "ערב חג",
  "סופ\"ש",
  "סופש",
  "סופשבוע",
  "שבת",
  "חול המועד",
  "עבודה במילואים",
]);

function toMinutes(t: string): number {
  const [h, m] = t.split(":").map(Number);
  return h * 60 + m;
}

function isValidAbsence(v: unknown): boolean {
  return (
    v === undefined ||
    (typeof v === "number" && Number.isFinite(v) && v >= 0 && v <= 1)
  );
}

export const dynamic = "force-dynamic";

/** GET /api/reports?year=2026&month=7 */
export async function GET(req: NextRequest) {
  const { searchParams } = new URL(req.url);
  const now = new Date();
  const year = parseInt(searchParams.get("year") ?? "", 10) || now.getFullYear();
  const month = parseInt(searchParams.get("month") ?? "", 10) || now.getMonth() + 1;
  const force = searchParams.get("force") === "1";
  try {
    const data = await getMonthData(year, month, force);
    return NextResponse.json(data);
  } catch (e) {
    return NextResponse.json(
      { error: e instanceof Error ? e.message : "שגיאה בקריאת הנתונים" },
      { status: 500 }
    );
  }
}

/** POST /api/reports — upsert a single day report (raw fields only). */
export async function POST(req: NextRequest) {
  let body: ReportInput;
  try {
    body = (await req.json()) as ReportInput;
  } catch {
    return NextResponse.json({ error: "גוף בקשה לא תקין" }, { status: 400 });
  }

  if (!body.date || !/^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(body.date)) {
    return NextResponse.json({ error: "תאריך חסר או לא תקין" }, { status: 400 });
  }
  const hhmm = /^([01]?\d|2[0-3]):[0-5]\d$/;
  if (body.entry && !hhmm.test(body.entry)) {
    return NextResponse.json({ error: "שעת כניסה לא תקינה (HH:mm)" }, { status: 400 });
  }
  if (body.exit && !hhmm.test(body.exit)) {
    return NextResponse.json({ error: "שעת יציאה לא תקינה (HH:mm)" }, { status: 400 });
  }
  if (body.entry && body.exit && toMinutes(body.exit) <= toMinutes(body.entry)) {
    return NextResponse.json(
      { error: "שעת היציאה חייבת להיות אחרי שעת הכניסה" },
      { status: 400 }
    );
  }
  if (body.classification && !ALLOWED_CLASSIFICATIONS.has(body.classification)) {
    return NextResponse.json({ error: `סיווג לא מוכר: "${body.classification}"` }, { status: 400 });
  }
  if (
    !isValidAbsence(body.vacationDays) ||
    !isValidAbsence(body.sickDays) ||
    !isValidAbsence(body.reserveDays)
  ) {
    return NextResponse.json(
      { error: "ערך היעדרות חייב להיות בין 0 ל-1" },
      { status: 400 }
    );
  }
  if (typeof body.notes === "string" && body.notes.length > 500) {
    return NextResponse.json({ error: "הערה ארוכה מדי (עד 500 תווים)" }, { status: 400 });
  }
  if (typeof body.uniqueNotes === "string" && body.uniqueNotes.length > 500) {
    return NextResponse.json({ error: "הערה ייחודית ארוכה מדי (עד 500 תווים)" }, { status: 400 });
  }

  try {
    const record = await saveReport(body);
    return NextResponse.json({ record });
  } catch (e) {
    return NextResponse.json(
      { error: e instanceof Error ? e.message : "שגיאה בשמירת הדיווח" },
      { status: 500 }
    );
  }
}
```

## `app/api/settings/route.ts`

```ts
import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from "next/server";
import { getSettings, saveSettings } from "@/lib/data";
import { StandardSettings } from "@/lib/types";

export const dynamic = "force-dynamic";

export async function GET() {
  try {
    const settings = await getSettings();
    return NextResponse.json(settings);
  } catch (e) {
    return NextResponse.json(
      { error: e instanceof Error ? e.message : "שגיאה בקריאת ההגדרות" },
      { status: 500 }
    );
  }
}

export async function POST(req: NextRequest) {
  let body: StandardSettings;
  try {
    body = (await req.json()) as StandardSettings;
  } catch {
    return NextResponse.json({ error: "גוף בקשה לא תקין" }, { status: 400 });
  }
  if (
    typeof body.annualVacationQuota !== "number" ||
    !Number.isFinite(body.annualVacationQuota) ||
    body.annualVacationQuota < 0 ||
    body.annualVacationQuota > 366 ||
    typeof body.years !== "object" ||
    body.years === null
  ) {
    return NextResponse.json({ error: "הגדרות לא תקינות" }, { status: 400 });
  }
  for (const [year, months] of Object.entries(body.years)) {
    if (
      !/^\d{4}$/.test(year) ||
      !Array.isArray(months) ||
      months.length !== 12 ||
      months.some((m) => typeof m !== "number" || !Number.isFinite(m) || m < 0 || m > 31)
    ) {
      return NextResponse.json(
        { error: `נתוני תקן לא תקינים לשנת ${year}` },
        { status: 400 }
      );
    }
  }
  try {
    await saveSettings(body);
    return NextResponse.json({ ok: true });
  } catch (e) {
    return NextResponse.json(
      { error: e instanceof Error ? e.message : "שגיאה בשמירת ההגדרות" },
      { status: 500 }
    );
  }
}
```

## `app/api/user/backups/route.ts`

```ts
import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from "next/server";
import { createSupabaseServerClient } from "@/lib/supabase/server";

export const dynamic = "force-dynamic";

const ADMIN_EMAILS = new Set(["michael.liarzi@gmail.com"]);

/**
 * GET /api/user/backups - List personal backups or download backup by ID
 */
export async function GET(req: NextRequest) {
  try {
    const supabase = createSupabaseServerClient();
    const {
      data: { user },
      error: authError,
    } = await supabase.auth.getUser();

    if (authError || !user) {
      return NextResponse.json({ error: "משתמש לא מחובר" }, { status: 401 });
    }

    const { searchParams } = new URL(req.url);
    const backupId = searchParams.get("id");
    const isAdmin = user.email ? ADMIN_EMAILS.has(user.email.toLowerCase()) : false;

    // Single backup download
    if (backupId) {
      let query = supabase
        .from("user_backups")
        .select("id, filename, backup_sql, user_id")
        .eq("id", backupId);

      if (!isAdmin) {
        query = query.eq("user_id", user.id);
      }

      const { data, error: dbErr } = await query.single();
      if (dbErr || !data) {
        return NextResponse.json({ error: "קובץ גיבוי לא נמצא או שאינך מורשה לגשת אליו" }, { status: 404 });
      }

      return NextResponse.json({
        ok: true,
        filename: data.filename,
        sql_dump: data.backup_sql,
      });
    }

    const { data: backups, error: dbErr } = await supabase
      .from("user_backups")
      .select("id, filename, reports_count, standards_count, terms_count, backup_type, file_size_kb, created_at")
      .eq("user_id", user.id)
      .order("created_at", { ascending: false });

    if (dbErr) {
      throw new Error("שגיאה בשליפת הגיבויים האישיים: " + dbErr.message);
    }

    return NextResponse.json({
      ok: true,
      backups: backups || [],
    });
  } catch (err: any) {
    console.error("[User Backups GET Error]", err);
    return NextResponse.json(
      { error: err.message || "שגיאה בטעינת גיבויים אישיים" },
      { status: 500 }
    );
  }
}

/**
 * POST /api/user/backups - Create personal backup, restore personal backup, or download SQL
 */
export async function POST(req: NextRequest) {
  try {
    const supabase = createSupabaseServerClient();
    const {
      data: { user },
      error: authError,
    } = await supabase.auth.getUser();

    if (authError || !user) {
      return NextResponse.json({ error: "משתמש לא מחובר" }, { status: 401 });
    }

    const body = await req.json().catch(() => ({}));
    const action = body.action;

    // 1. Action: CREATE PERSONAL BACKUP
    if (action === "create") {
      const { data, error } = await supabase.rpc("generate_user_backup", {
        p_user_id: user.id,
        p_backup_type: "manual_user",
      });

      if (error) {
        throw new Error("שגיאה ביצירת הגיבוי האישי ב-NAS: " + error.message);
      }

      return NextResponse.json({
        ok: true,
        backupId: data,
        message: "הגיבוי האישי שלך נשמר בהצלחה ב-NAS!",
      });
    }

    // 2. Action: RESTORE PERSONAL BACKUP
    if (action === "restore") {
      const backupId = body.backupId;
      if (!backupId) {
        return NextResponse.json({ error: "מזהה גיבוי חסר לשחזור." }, { status: 400 });
      }

      const isAdmin = user.email ? ADMIN_EMAILS.has(user.email.toLowerCase()) : false;
      const targetUserId = (isAdmin && body.targetUserId) ? body.targetUserId : user.id;

      const { data, error } = await supabase.rpc("restore_user_backup_by_id", {
        p_backup_id: backupId,
        p_user_id: targetUserId,
      });

      if (error) {
        throw new Error("שגיאה בשחזור הגיבוי: " + error.message);
      }

      return NextResponse.json({
        ok: true,
        result: data,
        message: "הנתונים שוחזרו בהצלחה מנקודת הגיבוי! (נוצר גיבוי ביטחון מקדים)",
      });
    }

    // 3. Action: DOWNLOAD PERSONAL SQL
    if (action === "download") {
      const backupId = body.backupId;
      if (!backupId) {
        return NextResponse.json({ error: "מזהה גיבוי חסר להורדה." }, { status: 400 });
      }

      const isAdmin = user.email ? ADMIN_EMAILS.has(user.email.toLowerCase()) : false;
      let query = supabase
        .from("user_backups")
        .select("filename, backup_sql, user_id")
        .eq("id", backupId);

      if (!isAdmin) {
        query = query.eq("user_id", user.id);
      }

      const { data, error } = await query.single();

      if (error || !data) {
        throw new Error("קובץ הגיבוי לא נמצא או שאינך מורשה לגשת אליו: " + (error?.message || ""));
      }

      return NextResponse.json({
        ok: true,
        filename: data.filename,
        sql: data.backup_sql,
      });
    }

    return NextResponse.json({ error: "פעולה לא חוקית." }, { status: 400 });
  } catch (err: any) {
    console.error("[User Backups POST Error]", err);
    return NextResponse.json(
      { error: err.message || "שגיאה בביצוע הפעולה" },
      { status: 500 }
    );
  }
}

/**
 * DELETE /api/user/backups - Delete a user backup by ID (owner or admin)
 */
export async function DELETE(req: NextRequest) {
  try {
    const supabase = createSupabaseServerClient();
    const {
      data: { user },
      error: authError,
    } = await supabase.auth.getUser();

    if (authError || !user) {
      return NextResponse.json({ error: "משתמש לא מחובר" }, { status: 401 });
    }

    const { searchParams } = new URL(req.url);
    const body = await req.json().catch(() => ({}));
    const backupId = searchParams.get("id") || body.backupId || body.id;

    if (!backupId) {
      return NextResponse.json({ error: "מזהה גיבוי חסר למחיקה." }, { status: 400 });
    }

    const isAdmin = user.email ? ADMIN_EMAILS.has(user.email.toLowerCase()) : false;

    let query = supabase.from("user_backups").delete().eq("id", backupId);
    if (!isAdmin) {
      query = query.eq("user_id", user.id);
    }

    const { error: delErr } = await query;
    if (delErr) {
      throw new Error("שגיאה במחיקת הגיבוי: " + delErr.message);
    }

    return NextResponse.json({
      ok: true,
      message: "הגיבוי נמחק בהצלחה מה-NAS.",
    });
  } catch (err: any) {
    console.error("[User Backup DELETE Error]", err);
    return NextResponse.json(
      { error: err.message || "שגיאה במחיקת הגיבוי" },
      { status: 500 }
    );
  }
}
```

## `app/auth/callback/route.ts`

```ts
import { NextResponse } from "next/server";
import { createSupabaseServerClient } from "@/lib/supabase/server";

export const dynamic = "force-dynamic";

/**
 * GET /auth/callback — completes the Supabase OAuth / email verification login flow.
 * Exchanges the code parameter for a session, setting cookies, and redirects to home.
 */
export async function GET(request: Request) {
  const { searchParams, origin } = new URL(request.url);
  const code = searchParams.get("code");
  const rawNext = searchParams.get("next") ?? "/";
  // Validate that next is a relative path starting with a single '/' to prevent open redirects (e.g. //evil.com or /\evil.com)
  const safeNext =
    typeof rawNext === "string" &&
    rawNext.startsWith("/") &&
    !rawNext.startsWith("//") &&
    !rawNext.startsWith("/\\")
      ? rawNext
      : "/";

  if (code) {
    try {
      const supabase = createSupabaseServerClient();
      const { error } = await supabase.auth.exchangeCodeForSession(code);
      if (!error) {
        return NextResponse.redirect(`${origin}${safeNext}`);
      }
      console.error("Auth exchange error:", error);
    } catch (err) {
      console.error("Auth exchange exception:", err);
    }
  }

  // Redirect to login page on authentication failure
  return NextResponse.redirect(`${origin}/login?auth=failed`);
}
```

```tsx
import type { Metadata, Viewport } from "next";
import "./globals.css";

export const metadata: Metadata = {
  title: "ניהול דיווח שעות - 24H WORK DASHBOARD",
  description: "לוח בקרה לניהול דיווח שעות עבודה, חופשות והיעדרויות",
  manifest: "/manifest.json",
  appleWebApp: {
    capable: true,
    statusBarStyle: "default",
    title: "24H Work",
  },
};

export const viewport: Viewport = {
  themeColor: "#2563eb",
  width: "device-width",
  initialScale: 1,
  maximumScale: 1,
  userScalable: false,
};

export default function RootLayout({
  children,
}: Readonly<{ children: React.ReactNode }>) {
  return (
    <html lang="he" dir="rtl">
      <head>
        <link rel="apple-touch-icon" href="/icon-192.png" />
        {/* Suppress third-party browser extension errors (e.g. MetaMask) from popping up in Next.js dev overlay */}
        <script
          dangerouslySetInnerHTML={{
            __html: `
              (function() {
                function isExtensionError(msg, src) {
                  var str = (msg || '') + ' ' + (src || '');
                  return str.indexOf('chrome-extension://') !== -1 ||
                         str.indexOf('moz-extension://') !== -1 ||
                         str.toLowerCase().indexOf('metamask') !== -1;
                }
                window.addEventListener('error', function(e) {
                  if (isExtensionError(e.message, e.filename)) {
                    e.stopImmediatePropagation();
                    e.preventDefault();
                  }
                }, true);
                window.addEventListener('unhandledrejection', function(e) {
                  var reason = e.reason ? (e.reason.message || String(e.reason)) : '';
                  if (isExtensionError(reason, '')) {
                    e.stopImmediatePropagation();
                    e.preventDefault();
                  }
                }, true);
              })();
            `,
          }}
        />
      </head>
      <body className="min-h-screen bg-slate-50 text-slate-800 antialiased">
        {children}
        
        {/* Register PWA Service Worker */}
        <script
          dangerouslySetInnerHTML={{
            __html: `
              if ('serviceWorker' in navigator) {
                window.addEventListener('load', function() {
                  navigator.serviceWorker.register('/sw.js').then(
                    function(registration) {
                      console.log('ServiceWorker registration successful with scope: ', registration.scope);
                    },
                    function(err) {
                      console.log('ServiceWorker registration failed: ', err);
                    }
                  );
                });
              }
            `,
          }}
        />
      </body>
    </html>
  );
}
```
