## `components/MonthPicker.tsx`

```tsx
"use client";

import { useEffect, useRef, useState } from "react";
import { HEBREW_MONTHS } from "@/lib/types";
import { ChevronRight } from "./Icons";

const FIRST_YEAR = 2019;

interface Props {
  year: number;
  month: number; // 1-12
  onSelect: (year: number, month: number) => void;
}

/**
 * Drill-down month selector: click the label -> year grid -> month grid ->
 * selection applied. Chevron navigation in the header stays untouched.
 */
export default function MonthPicker({ year, month, onSelect }: Props) {
  const [open, setOpen] = useState(false);
  const [stage, setStage] = useState<"years" | "months">("years");
  const [pickedYear, setPickedYear] = useState(year);
  const ref = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);

  // Close on outside click / Escape.
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!open) return;
    const onDown = (e: MouseEvent) => {
      if (ref.current && !ref.current.contains(e.target as Node)) setOpen(false);
    };
    const onKey = (e: KeyboardEvent) => {
      if (e.key === "Escape") setOpen(false);
    };
    document.addEventListener("mousedown", onDown);
    document.addEventListener("keydown", onKey);
    return () => {
      document.removeEventListener("mousedown", onDown);
      document.removeEventListener("keydown", onKey);
    };
  }, [open]);

  const toggle = () => {
    setStage("years");
    setPickedYear(year);
    setOpen((v) => !v);
  };

  const years: number[] = [];
  for (let y = FIRST_YEAR; y <= new Date().getFullYear() + 1; y++) years.push(y);

  return (
    <div className="relative" ref={ref}>
      <button
        onClick={toggle}
        aria-haspopup="dialog"
        aria-expanded={open}
        className="min-w-[90px] rounded-lg px-2 py-1 text-center text-sm font-semibold text-slate-700 transition hover:bg-slate-100"
      >
        {HEBREW_MONTHS[month - 1]} {year}
      </button>

      {open && (
        <div className="absolute start-1/2 top-full z-40 mt-2 w-64 -translate-x-1/2 rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 bg-white p-3 shadow-xl rtl:translate-x-1/2">
          {stage === "years" ? (
            <>
              <p className="mb-2 px-1 text-xs font-medium text-slate-400">בחר שנה</p>
              <div className="grid max-h-56 grid-cols-4 gap-1.5 overflow-y-auto">
                {years.map((y) => (
                  <button
                    key={y}
                    onClick={() => {
                      setPickedYear(y);
                      setStage("months");
                    }}
                    className={`rounded-lg py-2 text-sm font-medium transition ${
                      y === year
                        ? "bg-brand-600 text-white"
                        : "text-slate-600 hover:bg-brand-50 hover:text-brand-700"
                    }`}
                  >
                    {y}
                  </button>
                ))}
              </div>
            </>
          ) : (
            <>
              <div className="mb-2 flex items-center gap-1 px-1">
                <button
                  onClick={() => setStage("years")}
                  aria-label="חזרה לבחירת שנה"
                  className="flex items-center gap-1 rounded-md px-1.5 py-0.5 text-xs font-medium text-slate-500 transition hover:bg-slate-100"
                >
                  <ChevronRight className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
                  {pickedYear}
                </button>
                <span className="text-xs text-slate-400">בחר חודש</span>
              </div>
              <div className="grid grid-cols-3 gap-1.5">
                {HEBREW_MONTHS.map((name, i) => (
                  <button
                    key={name}
                    onClick={() => {
                      onSelect(pickedYear, i + 1);
                      setOpen(false);
                    }}
                    className={`rounded-lg py-2 text-sm font-medium transition ${
                      pickedYear === year && i + 1 === month
                        ? "bg-brand-600 text-white"
                        : "text-slate-600 hover:bg-brand-50 hover:text-brand-700"
                    }`}
                  >
                    {name}
                  </button>
                ))}
              </div>
            </>
          )}
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}
```

## `components/MonthlySummary.tsx`

```tsx
"use client";

import { MonthSummary } from "@/lib/types";

function Row({
  label,
  value,
  sub,
  onClick,
  isClickable,
}: {
  label: string;
  value: React.ReactNode;
  sub?: React.ReactNode;
  onClick?: () => void;
  isClickable?: boolean;
}) {
  return (
    <div
      onClick={onClick}
      className={`flex items-center justify-between rounded-xl bg-slate-50 px-4 py-3 transition ${
        isClickable
          ? "cursor-pointer hover:bg-indigo-50/80 hover:ring-1 hover:ring-indigo-200"
          : ""
      }`}
    >
      <div>
        <span className="text-sm text-slate-600 block">{label}</span>
        {sub && <span className="text-[11px] text-slate-400 block">{sub}</span>}
      </div>
      <div className="text-left font-bold text-brand-700 text-sm">{value}</div>
    </div>
  );
}

interface Props {
  summary: MonthSummary;
  onOpenOvertimeModal?: () => void;
}

export default function MonthlySummary({ summary, onOpenOvertimeModal }: Props) {
  const term = summary.activeEmploymentTerm;
  const isHourly = term?.employmentType === "hourly";
  const rate = term?.hourlyRate || 0;
  const otHours = summary.overtimeHoursThisMonth || 0;

  return (
    <section className="rounded-2xl border border-slate-100 bg-white p-5 shadow-card space-y-3">
      <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-1">
        <h2 className="text-sm font-bold text-slate-800">סיכום סטטוס חודשי</h2>
        {otHours > 0 && onOpenOvertimeModal && (
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={onOpenOvertimeModal}
            className="text-[11px] font-semibold text-indigo-600 hover:text-indigo-800 transition flex items-center gap-1 cursor-pointer"
          >
            <span>פירוט ש"נ ⏱️</span>
          </button>
        )}
      </div>

      <Row label="יעד שעות" value={`${summary.targetHours.toFixed(1)} ש'`} />
      <Row label="דווח בפועל" value={`${summary.reportedHours.toFixed(1)} ש'`} />
      <Row label="יתרה להשלמה" value={`${summary.remainingHours.toFixed(1)} ש'`} />

      {/* Overtime Row */}
      <Row
        label="שעות נוספות"
        sub={
          otHours > 0 ? (
            <span className="text-indigo-600">
              {summary.ot125HoursThisMonth?.toFixed(1) || 0}ש' (125%) · {summary.ot150HoursThisMonth?.toFixed(1) || 0}ש' (150%)
            </span>
          ) : (
            "לא בוצעו החודש"
          )
        }
        value={
          <span className={otHours > 0 ? "text-indigo-700 font-extrabold" : "text-slate-400 font-normal"}>
            {otHours.toFixed(1)} ש'
          </span>
        }
        onClick={otHours > 0 ? onOpenOvertimeModal : undefined}
        isClickable={otHours > 0}
      />

      {/* Overtime / Earnings Pay Estimate */}
      <Row
        label={isHourly ? "שכר משוער כולל" : "תוספת ש\"נ לתלוש"}
        sub={
          rate > 0 ? (
            <span>לפי ערך שעה ₪{rate}</span>
          ) : (
            <span className="text-amber-600 font-medium">לא הוגדר ערך שעה בחוזה</span>
          )
        }
        value={
          rate > 0 ? (
            <span className="text-emerald-700 font-extrabold">
              ₪{(isHourly ? summary.totalEstimatedPay : summary.overtimeAdditionPay || 0)?.toLocaleString()}
            </span>
          ) : (
            <span className="text-slate-300 font-normal">—</span>
          )
        }
        onClick={otHours > 0 ? onOpenOvertimeModal : undefined}
        isClickable={otHours > 0}
      />

      <div className="rounded-xl bg-brand-600 px-4 py-4 text-white mt-2">
        <p className="text-xs font-medium text-brand-100">תחזית המשך עבודה:</p>
        <p className="mt-1 text-2xl font-bold">
          {summary.forecastHoursPerDay.toFixed(1)}{" "}
          <span className="text-base font-semibold">שעות/יום</span>
        </p>
        <p className="mt-1 text-xs text-brand-100">
          נותרו {summary.remainingWorkdays} ימי עבודה פוטנציאליים
        </p>
      </div>
    </section>
  );
}
```

## `components/OvertimeModal.tsx`

```tsx
"use client";

import { useMemo } from "react";
import { DayRecord, MonthSummary } from "@/lib/types";
import { HEBREW_MONTHS } from "@/lib/types";

interface Props {
  isOpen: boolean;
  onClose: () => void;
  summary: MonthSummary;
  records: DayRecord[];
  year: number;
  month: number;
}

export default function OvertimeModal({
  isOpen,
  onClose,
  summary,
  records,
  year,
  month,
}: Props) {
  const term = summary.activeEmploymentTerm;
  const rate = term?.hourlyRate || 0;
  const isHourly = term?.employmentType === "hourly";
  const isGlobal = term?.employmentType === "global";

  // Filter records that have overtime (> 0)
  const overtimeDays = useMemo(() => {
    return records
      .filter((r) => (r.overtimeDecimal || 0) > 0)
      .map((r) => {
        const ot125 = r.overtime125Decimal || 0;
        const ot150 = r.overtime150Decimal || 0;
        let dayCost = 0;
        if (rate > 0) {
          if (isHourly) {
            dayCost = ot125 * (rate * 1.25) + ot150 * (rate * 1.5);
          } else if (!isGlobal) {
            // monthly with overtime: net addition 25% and 50%
            dayCost = ot125 * (rate * 0.25) + ot150 * (rate * 0.5);
          }
        }
        return {
          ...r,
          ot125,
          ot150,
          dayCost: Math.round(dayCost * 100) / 100,
        };
      });
  }, [records, rate, isHourly, isGlobal]);

  if (!isOpen) return null;

  return (
    <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex items-center justify-center bg-slate-900/50 p-4 backdrop-blur-xs">
      <div className="flex max-h-[90vh] w-full max-w-3xl flex-col rounded-2xl bg-white shadow-2xl animate-in fade-in zoom-in-95 duration-150">
        {/* Header */}
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between border-b border-slate-100 px-6 py-4">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
            <span className="flex h-9 w-9 items-center justify-center rounded-xl bg-indigo-50 text-lg">
              ⏱️
            </span>
            <div>
              <h2 className="text-base font-bold text-slate-800">
                פירוט שעות נוספות — {HEBREW_MONTHS[month - 1]} {year}
              </h2>
              <p className="text-xs text-slate-400">
                סוג חוזה: {term?.name || "ברירת מחדל"} ({isHourly ? "שעתי" : isGlobal ? "גלובלי" : "חודשי עם שעות נוספות"})
                {rate > 0 ? ` • ערך שעה: ₪${rate}` : " • לא הוגדר ערך שעה"}
              </p>
            </div>
          </div>
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={onClose}
            className="rounded-lg p-1.5 text-slate-400 hover:bg-slate-100 hover:text-slate-600 transition cursor-pointer"
          >
            ✕
          </button>
        </div>

        {/* KPI Strip */}
        <div className="grid grid-cols-2 sm:grid-cols-4 gap-3 p-4 bg-slate-50 border-b border-slate-100 text-center">
          <div className="rounded-xl bg-white p-3 border border-slate-200/80 shadow-xs">
            <span className="block text-[11px] font-medium text-slate-500">סה"כ שעות נוספות</span>
            <span className="text-lg font-bold text-slate-800">
              {(summary.overtimeHoursThisMonth || 0).toFixed(1)} ש'
            </span>
          </div>
          <div className="rounded-xl bg-white p-3 border border-slate-200/80 shadow-xs">
            <span className="block text-[11px] font-medium text-slate-500">מדרגת 125% (שעתיים ראשונות)</span>
            <span className="text-lg font-bold text-indigo-600">
              {(summary.ot125HoursThisMonth || 0).toFixed(1)} ש'
            </span>
          </div>
          <div className="rounded-xl bg-white p-3 border border-slate-200/80 shadow-xs">
            <span className="block text-[11px] font-medium text-slate-500">מדרגת 150% (משעה 3 ומעלה)</span>
            <span className="text-lg font-bold text-purple-600">
              {(summary.ot150HoursThisMonth || 0).toFixed(1)} ש'
            </span>
          </div>
          <div className="rounded-xl bg-white p-3 border border-slate-200/80 shadow-xs">
            <span className="block text-[11px] font-medium text-slate-500">
              {isHourly ? "עלות ש\"נ מלאה" : isGlobal ? "תוספת גלובלית" : "תוספת ש\"נ לתלוש"}
            </span>
            <span className="text-lg font-extrabold text-emerald-600">
              {rate > 0 ? (
                `₪${(isHourly ? summary.overtimeFullPay : summary.overtimeAdditionPay || 0)?.toLocaleString()}`
              ) : (
                <span className="text-xs text-slate-400 font-normal">הגדר ערך שעה</span>
              )}
            </span>
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Days Table */}
        <div className="flex-1 overflow-y-auto p-4">
          {overtimeDays.length === 0 ? (
            <div className="py-12 text-center text-slate-400">
              <span className="text-3xl block mb-2">🎉</span>
              <p className="font-semibold text-slate-600 text-sm">אין שעות נוספות בחודש זה</p>
              <p className="text-xs mt-1">כל שעות העבודה שדווחו היו במסגרת התקן היומי.</p>
            </div>
          ) : (
            <div className="overflow-hidden rounded-xl border border-slate-200 bg-white">
              <table className="w-full text-right text-xs">
                <thead>
                  <tr className="bg-slate-50 text-slate-500 font-semibold border-b border-slate-200">
                    <th className="px-3 py-2.5">תאריך ויום</th>
                    <th className="px-3 py-2.5 text-center">כניסה - יציאה</th>
                    <th className="px-3 py-2.5 text-center">סה"כ שעות</th>
                    <th className="px-3 py-2.5 text-center">תקן יומי</th>
                    <th className="px-3 py-2.5 text-center text-indigo-700">ש"נ 125%</th>
                    <th className="px-3 py-2.5 text-center text-purple-700">ש"נ 150%</th>
                    {rate > 0 && <th className="px-3 py-2.5 text-center text-emerald-700">עלות יומית</th>}
                  </tr>
                </thead>
                <tbody className="divide-y divide-slate-100">
                  {overtimeDays.map((d) => (
                    <tr key={d.date} className="hover:bg-slate-50/70 transition">
                      <td className="px-3 py-2.5 font-medium text-slate-800">
                        {d.dateDisplay} <span className="text-slate-400 font-normal">({d.dayName})</span>
                      </td>
                      <td className="px-3 py-2.5 text-center font-mono text-slate-600">
                        {d.entry} - {d.exit}
                      </td>
                      <td className="px-3 py-2.5 text-center font-bold text-slate-700">
                        {d.totalHoursDecimal.toFixed(1)}
                      </td>
                      <td className="px-3 py-2.5 text-center text-slate-500">
                        {d.dailyStandard.toFixed(1)}
                      </td>
                      <td className="px-3 py-2.5 text-center font-bold text-indigo-600">
                        {d.ot125 > 0 ? `${d.ot125.toFixed(1)}ש'` : "—"}
                      </td>
                      <td className="px-3 py-2.5 text-center font-bold text-purple-600">
                        {d.ot150 > 0 ? `${d.ot150.toFixed(1)}ש'` : "—"}
                      </td>
                      {rate > 0 && (
                        <td className="px-3 py-2.5 text-center font-bold text-emerald-600">
                          ₪{d.dayCost.toFixed(2)}
                        </td>
                      )}
                    </tr>
                  ))}
                </tbody>
              </table>
            </div>
          )}

          {/* Legal Explanatory Note */}
          <div className="mt-4 rounded-xl border border-slate-100 bg-slate-50 p-3 text-[11px] text-slate-500 space-y-1">
            <span className="font-bold text-slate-700 block">ℹ️ חישוב לפי חוק שעות עבודה ומנוחה בישראל:</span>
            <p>
              • <strong>2 השעות הנוספות הראשונות ביום:</strong> תעריף 125% (לעובד חודשי: תוספת 25% מעבר למשכורת הבסיס).
            </p>
            <p>
              • <strong>החל מהשעה הנוספת השלישית:</strong> תעריף 150% (לעובד חודשי: תוספת 50% מעבר למשכורת הבסיס).
            </p>
            {rate > 0 && !isHourly && (
              <p className="text-emerald-700 font-medium">
                • שווי רכיב שעות נוספות מלא (125%/150%): ₪{(summary.overtimeFullPay || 0).toLocaleString()}
              </p>
            )}
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Footer */}
        <div className="border-t border-slate-100 px-6 py-3 bg-slate-50/50 flex justify-end">
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={onClose}
            className="rounded-xl bg-slate-200 px-4 py-2 text-xs font-semibold text-slate-700 hover:bg-slate-300 transition cursor-pointer"
          >
            סגור
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}
```

## `components/ReportModal.tsx`

```tsx
"use client";

import { FormEvent, useEffect, useState, useMemo } from "react";
import { DayRecord, ReportInput, ReportShift } from "@/lib/types";
import { SaveIcon, XIcon, PlusIcon, TrashIcon } from "./Icons";
import { getClassifications, getOrderTypes, getHolidays, getHolidayForDate, HolidaySetting } from "@/lib/settingsStore";
import { isoToDdmmyyyy } from "@/lib/date";

interface Props {
  /** Existing record when editing, null for a fresh report. */
  record: DayRecord | null;
  defaultDate: string; // ISO
  onClose: () => void;
  onSave: (input: ReportInput) => Promise<void>;
}

/** Sheet stores "7:05"; <input type="time"> requires "07:05". */
function padTime(t: string): string {
  const m = /^(\d{1,2}):(\d{2})$/.exec((t ?? "").trim());
  return m ? `${m[1].padStart(2, "0")}:${m[2]}` : "";
}

function formatTimeInput(value: string, prevValue: string = ""): string {
  if (!value) return "";

  // Allow backspacing over colon or deleting
  if (prevValue.endsWith(":") && value === prevValue.slice(0, -1)) {
    return value;
  }
  if (value.endsWith(":") && value.length < prevValue.length) {
    return value;
  }

  // Extract only digits
  const digits = value.replace(/\D/g, "");
  if (!digits) return "";

  // 1 digit: if 3..9 or manual colon, auto-pad with 0 and add colon (e.g. '8' -> '08:')
  if (digits.length === 1) {
    const first = parseInt(digits[0], 10);
    if ((first >= 3 && first <= 9) || value.includes(":")) {
      return `0${first}:`;
    }
    return digits;
  }

  // 2 digits: hours (00 - 23)
  let hh = digits.slice(0, 2);
  let hhInt = parseInt(hh, 10);
  if (isNaN(hhInt) || hhInt > 23) hh = "23";

  if (digits.length === 2) {
    return `${hh}:`;
  }

  // 3 digits: minutes tens (0 - 5)
  if (digits.length === 3) {
    let m1 = parseInt(digits[2], 10);
    if (isNaN(m1)) m1 = 0;
    if (m1 > 5) m1 = 5;
    return `${hh}:${m1}`;
  }

  // 4+ digits: minutes (00 - 59)
  let mm = digits.slice(2, 4);
  let mmInt = parseInt(mm, 10);
  if (isNaN(mmInt) || mmInt > 59) mm = "59";
  return `${hh}:${mm}`;
}


function AbsenceInput({
  label,
  value,
  onChange,
}: {
  label: string;
  value: string;
  onChange: (v: string) => void;
}) {
  return (
    <div className="flex-1">
      <label className="mb-1.5 block text-xs font-medium text-slate-500">{label}</label>
      <input
        type="number"
        min={0}
        max={1}
        step={0.5}
        inputMode="decimal"
        value={value}
        onChange={(e) => onChange(e.target.value)}
        className="ltr-field w-full rounded-xl border border-slate-200 bg-slate-100/70 px-3 py-2.5 text-sm outline-none transition focus:border-brand-500 focus:bg-white focus:ring-2 focus:ring-brand-100"
      />
    </div>
  );
}

export default function ReportModal({ record, defaultDate, onClose, onSave }: Props) {
  const [date, setDate] = useState(record?.date ?? defaultDate);
  const initialClassification = useMemo(() => {
    const raw = record?.classification || "";
    const clean = raw.replace(/""/g, '"').replace(/^["']|["']$/g, "").trim();
    if (clean === "סופש" || clean === "סופשבוע" || clean === 'סופ"ש') {
      return 'סופ"ש';
    }
    return raw || "עבודה";
  }, [record?.classification]);
  const [classification, setClassification] = useState(initialClassification);
  const [entry, setEntry] = useState(padTime(record?.entry ?? ""));
  const [exit, setExit] = useState(padTime(record?.exit ?? ""));
  const [vacation, setVacation] = useState(record?.vacationDays ? String(record.vacationDays) : "");
  const [sick, setSick] = useState(record?.sickDays ? String(record.sickDays) : "");
  const [reserve, setReserve] = useState(record?.reserveDays ? String(record.reserveDays) : "");
  const [notes, setNotes] = useState(record?.notes ?? "");
  const [uniqueNotes, setUniqueNotes] = useState(record?.uniqueNotes ?? "");
  const [orderType, setOrderType] = useState(record?.orderType || "מילואים רגילים");
  const initialShifts = useMemo(() => {
    if (record?.shifts && record.shifts.length > 0) {
      return record.shifts.map((s) => ({
        entry: padTime(s.entry),
        exit: padTime(s.exit),
        description: s.description || "",
      }));
    }
    return [];
  }, [record]);

  const [shifts, setShifts] = useState<ReportShift[]>(initialShifts);

  const addShift = () => {
    setShifts((prev) => [
      ...prev,
      { entry: "08:00", exit: "12:00", description: `מקטע ${prev.length + 1}` },
    ]);
  };

  const removeShift = (idx: number) => {
    setShifts((prev) => prev.filter((_, i) => i !== idx));
  };

  const updateShift = (idx: number, field: keyof ReportShift, val: string) => {
    setShifts((prev) => {
      const next = [...prev];
      next[idx] = { ...next[idx], [field]: val };
      return next;
    });
  };

  // Convert HH:mm to minutes
  const toMin = (t: string) => {
    if (!t || !t.includes(":")) return 0;
    const [h, m] = t.split(":").map(Number);
    return h * 60 + m;
  };

  // Live total hours calculation
  const computedTotalHours = useMemo(() => {
    if (shifts.length > 0) {
      let totalMins = 0;
      for (const s of shifts) {
        if (s.entry && s.exit) {
          const diff = toMin(s.exit) - toMin(s.entry);
          if (diff > 0) totalMins += diff;
        }
      }
      const hrs = totalMins / 60;
      const h = Math.floor(hrs);
      const m = Math.round((hrs - h) * 60);
      return { decimal: Math.round(hrs * 100) / 100, text: `${h}:${String(m).padStart(2, "0")}` };
    }
    if (entry && exit) {
      const diff = toMin(exit) - toMin(entry);
      if (diff > 0) {
        const hrs = diff / 60;
        const h = Math.floor(hrs);
        const m = Math.round((hrs - h) * 60);
        return { decimal: Math.round(hrs * 100) / 100, text: `${h}:${String(m).padStart(2, "0")}` };
      }
    }
    return { decimal: 0, text: "0:00" };
  }, [shifts, entry, exit]);

  const [saving, setSaving] = useState(false);
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);

  const classificationsList = useMemo(() => {
    const list = [...getClassifications()];
    if (classification && !list.includes(classification)) {
      list.push(classification);
    }
    return list;
  }, [classification]);
  const orderTypesList = useMemo(() => getOrderTypes(), []);

  // Auto-detect holiday for current date
  const holidayInfo = useMemo(() => (date ? getHolidayForDate(date) : undefined), [date]);

  // Auto-detect holiday dates when date changes (for new reports only)
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!record && holidayInfo) {
      setClassification(holidayInfo.category);
      setNotes((prev) => (prev ? prev : holidayInfo.name));
    }
  }, [date, record, holidayInfo]);

  // Close on Escape.
  useEffect(() => {
    const onKey = (e: KeyboardEvent) => {
      if (e.key === "Escape") onClose();
    };
    window.addEventListener("keydown", onKey);
    return () => window.removeEventListener("keydown", onKey);
  }, [onClose]);

  async function handleSubmit(e: FormEvent) {
    e.preventDefault();
    setError(null);
    if (!date) {
      setError("יש לבחור תאריך");
      return;
    }
    setSaving(true);
    try {
      const effectiveEntry = shifts.length > 0 ? (shifts[0]?.entry || entry || undefined) : (entry || undefined);
      const effectiveExit = shifts.length > 0 ? (shifts[shifts.length - 1]?.exit || exit || undefined) : (exit || undefined);
      const validShifts = shifts.filter((s) => s.entry && s.exit);

      await onSave({
        date,
        entry: effectiveEntry,
        exit: effectiveExit,
        shifts: validShifts.length > 0 ? validShifts : undefined,
        classification,
        vacationDays: vacation ? parseFloat(vacation) : 0,
        sickDays: sick ? parseFloat(sick) : 0,
        reserveDays: reserve ? parseFloat(reserve) : 0,
        notes: notes || undefined,
        uniqueNotes: uniqueNotes || undefined,
        orderType: (classification === "מילואים" || (reserve && parseFloat(reserve) > 0)) ? orderType : undefined,
      });
    } catch (err) {
      setError(err instanceof Error ? err.message : "שגיאה בשמירת הדיווח");
      setSaving(false);
    }
  }

  return (
    <div
      role="dialog"
      aria-modal="true"
      aria-label={record ? "עריכת דיווח" : "דיווח חדש"}
      className="fixed inset-0 z-[60] flex items-center justify-center bg-slate-900/50 p-4 backdrop-blur-sm"
      onMouseDown={(e) => {
        if (e.target === e.currentTarget) onClose();
      }}
    >
      <div className="w-full max-w-lg overflow-hidden rounded-2xl bg-white shadow-2xl max-h-[90vh] flex flex-col">
        {/* Header */}
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between border-b border-slate-100 bg-slate-50/60 px-4 sm:px-6 py-4 shrink-0">
          <h2 className="text-base font-bold text-slate-900">
            {record ? "עריכת דיווח" : "דיווח חדש"}
          </h2>
          <button onClick={onClose} aria-label="סגירה" className="text-slate-400 hover:text-slate-600">
            <XIcon />
          </button>
        </div>

        <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className="space-y-5 px-4 sm:px-6 py-5 overflow-y-auto flex-1">
          {holidayInfo && (
            <div className="flex items-center gap-2 rounded-xl bg-purple-50 border border-purple-200 px-3.5 py-2.5 text-xs text-purple-900">
              <span className="flex h-5 w-5 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-full bg-purple-200/70 text-[11px]">
                🗓️
              </span>
              <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 flex-wrap">
                <span className="font-bold">מועד מוגדר:</span>
                <span className="font-semibold text-purple-800">{holidayInfo.name}</span>
                <span className="rounded-md bg-purple-200/60 px-1.5 py-0.5 text-[10px] font-bold text-purple-800">
                  {holidayInfo.category}
                </span>
              </div>
            </div>
          )}

          {/* Date + classification */}
          <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-4 sm:grid-cols-2">
            <div>
              <label className="mb-1.5 block text-xs font-medium text-slate-500">תאריך</label>
              <input
                type={record ? "text" : "date"}
                value={record ? isoToDdmmyyyy(date) : date}
                onChange={(e) => {
                  if (!record) setDate(e.target.value);
                }}
                disabled={Boolean(record)}
                className="w-full rounded-xl border border-slate-200 bg-slate-100/70 px-3 py-2.5 text-sm outline-none transition focus:border-brand-500 focus:bg-white focus:ring-2 focus:ring-brand-100 disabled:opacity-60"
              />
            </div>
            <div>
              <label className="mb-1.5 block text-xs font-medium text-slate-500">סיווג</label>
              <select
                value={classification}
                onChange={(e) => setClassification(e.target.value)}
                className="w-full appearance-none rounded-xl border border-slate-200 bg-slate-100/70 px-3 py-2.5 text-sm outline-none transition focus:border-brand-500 focus:bg-white focus:ring-2 focus:ring-brand-100"
              >
                {classificationsList.map((c) => (
                  <option key={c} value={c}>
                    {c === 'סופ"ש' ? "סופשבוע" : c}
                  </option>
                ))}
              </select>
            </div>
          </div>

          {(classification === "מילואים" || (reserve && parseFloat(reserve) > 0)) && (
            <div>
              <label className="mb-1.5 block text-xs font-medium text-slate-500">סוג צו מילואים</label>
              <select
                value={orderType}
                onChange={(e) => setOrderType(e.target.value)}
                className="w-full appearance-none rounded-xl border border-slate-200 bg-slate-100/70 px-3 py-2.5 text-sm outline-none transition focus:border-brand-500 focus:bg-white focus:ring-2 focus:ring-brand-100"
              >
                {orderTypesList.map((ot) => (
                  <option key={ot} value={ot}>{ot}</option>
                ))}
              </select>
            </div>
          )}

          {/* Working hours block */}
          <div className="rounded-2xl bg-brand-50/60 p-4 border border-brand-100/80">
            <div className="mb-3 flex items-center justify-between">
              <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                <p className="text-xs font-bold text-brand-700">שעות עבודה (פורמט 24H)</p>
                {computedTotalHours.decimal > 0 && (
                  <span className="rounded-md bg-brand-600 px-2 py-0.5 text-[11px] font-bold text-white shadow-xs">
                    סה״כ: {computedTotalHours.text} ({computedTotalHours.decimal} שע׳)
                  </span>
                )}
              </div>
              {shifts.length > 0 ? (
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => {
                    const firstEntry = shifts[0]?.entry || entry;
                    const lastExit = shifts[shifts.length - 1]?.exit || exit;
                    setEntry(firstEntry);
                    setExit(lastExit);
                    setShifts([]);
                  }}
                  className="text-xs font-semibold text-brand-600 hover:text-brand-800 underline transition"
                >
                  חזרה למשמרת רציפה
                </button>
              ) : (
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => {
                    setShifts([
                      { entry: entry || "08:00", exit: exit ? (toMin(exit) > toMin(entry || "08:00") + 240 ? "12:00" : exit) : "12:00", description: "מקטע 1" },
                      { entry: "12:30", exit: exit || "17:00", description: "מקטע 2" },
                    ]);
                  }}
                  className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 text-xs font-semibold text-brand-600 hover:text-brand-800 transition bg-white/80 border border-brand-200 rounded-lg px-2 py-1 shadow-xs"
                >
                  <PlusIcon className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                  <span>פיצול למקטעים</span>
                </button>
              )}
            </div>

            {shifts.length > 0 ? (
              <div className="space-y-3">
                {shifts.map((s, idx) => (
                  <div key={idx} className="relative rounded-xl border border-brand-200/90 bg-white p-3 shadow-xs transition hover:border-brand-300">
                    <div className="mb-2 flex items-center justify-between">
                      <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                        <span className="inline-flex items-center justify-center w-5 h-5 rounded-full bg-brand-100 text-[11px] font-extrabold text-brand-700">
                          {idx + 1}
                        </span>
                        <input
                          type="text"
                          value={s.description || `מקטע ${idx + 1}`}
                          placeholder={`מקטע ${idx + 1}`}
                          onChange={(e) => updateShift(idx, "description", e.target.value)}
                          className="text-xs font-bold text-slate-700 bg-transparent border-none outline-none focus:text-brand-700"
                        />
                      </div>
                      {shifts.length > 1 && (
                        <button
                          type="button"
                          onClick={() => removeShift(idx)}
                          className="rounded-lg p-1 text-slate-400 hover:bg-rose-50 hover:text-rose-600 transition"
                          title="מחיקת מקטע"
                        >
                          <TrashIcon className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                        </button>
                      )}
                    </div>
                    <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3">
                      <div>
                        <label className="mb-1 block text-[11px] font-medium text-slate-500">כניסה</label>
                        <input
                          type="text"
                          value={s.entry}
                          placeholder="08:00"
                          inputMode="numeric"
                          onChange={(e) => updateShift(idx, "entry", formatTimeInput(e.target.value, s.entry))}
                          className="ltr-field w-full rounded-lg border border-slate-200 bg-slate-50 px-2.5 py-1.5 text-xs font-bold text-brand-700 outline-none transition focus:border-brand-500 focus:bg-white focus:ring-1 focus:ring-brand-200"
                        />
                      </div>
                      <div>
                        <label className="mb-1 block text-[11px] font-medium text-slate-500">יציאה</label>
                        <input
                          type="text"
                          value={s.exit}
                          placeholder="12:00"
                          inputMode="numeric"
                          onChange={(e) => updateShift(idx, "exit", formatTimeInput(e.target.value, s.exit))}
                          className="ltr-field w-full rounded-lg border border-slate-200 bg-slate-50 px-2.5 py-1.5 text-xs font-bold text-brand-700 outline-none transition focus:border-brand-500 focus:bg-white focus:ring-1 focus:ring-brand-200"
                        />
                      </div>
                    </div>
                  </div>
                ))}

                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={addShift}
                  className="flex w-full items-center justify-center gap-1.5 rounded-xl border border-dashed border-brand-300 bg-white/70 py-2 text-xs font-bold text-brand-600 hover:bg-brand-50/50 hover:border-brand-400 transition"
                >
                  <PlusIcon className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                  <span>הוסף מקטע עבודה נוסף</span>
                </button>
              </div>
            ) : (
              <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-4">
                <div>
                  <label className="mb-1.5 block text-xs font-medium text-slate-500">כניסה</label>
                  <input
                    type="text"
                    value={entry}
                    placeholder="08:00"
                    inputMode="numeric"
                    onChange={(e) => setEntry(formatTimeInput(e.target.value, entry))}
                    className="ltr-field w-full rounded-xl border border-transparent bg-white px-3 py-2.5 text-sm font-bold text-brand-700 shadow-sm outline-none transition focus:border-brand-500 focus:ring-2 focus:ring-brand-100"
                  />
                </div>
                <div>
                  <label className="mb-1.5 block text-xs font-medium text-slate-500">יציאה</label>
                  <input
                    type="text"
                    value={exit}
                    placeholder="17:00"
                    inputMode="numeric"
                    onChange={(e) => setExit(formatTimeInput(e.target.value, exit))}
                    className="ltr-field w-full rounded-xl border border-transparent bg-white px-3 py-2.5 text-sm font-bold text-brand-700 shadow-sm outline-none transition focus:border-brand-500 focus:ring-2 focus:ring-brand-100"
                  />
                </div>
              </div>
            )}

            {((entry && !exit) || (!entry && exit)) && shifts.length === 0 && (
              <div className="mt-3 flex items-center gap-2 rounded-xl bg-amber-50/90 border border-amber-200 px-3 py-2 text-xs text-amber-900">
                <span className="text-sm">⚠️</span>
                <span>
                  {entry && !exit
                    ? 'הוזנה שעת כניסה בלבד ללא שעת יציאה. הדיווח יישמר כ"דיווח חסר חלקי - יציאה".'
                    : 'הוזנה שעת יציאה בלבד ללא שעת כניסה. הדיווח יישמר כ"דיווח חסר חלקי - כניסה".'}
                </span>
              </div>
            )}
          </div>

          {/* Full-day absences */}
          <div className="flex gap-4">
            <AbsenceInput label="חופש" value={vacation} onChange={setVacation} />
            <AbsenceInput label="מחלה" value={sick} onChange={setSick} />
            <AbsenceInput label="מילואים" value={reserve} onChange={setReserve} />
          </div>

          {/* Notes area */}
          <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-4 sm:grid-cols-2">
            <div>
              <label className="mb-1.5 block text-xs font-medium text-slate-500">הערות</label>
              <textarea
                value={notes}
                onChange={(e) => setNotes(e.target.value)}
                placeholder="רישום הערות נוספות..."
                rows={3}
                maxLength={500}
                className="w-full rounded-xl border border-slate-200 bg-slate-100/70 px-3 py-2 text-sm outline-none transition focus:border-brand-500 focus:bg-white focus:ring-2 focus:ring-brand-100"
              />
            </div>
            <div>
              <label className="mb-1.5 block text-xs font-medium text-slate-500">הערות ייחודיות</label>
              <textarea
                value={uniqueNotes}
                onChange={(e) => setUniqueNotes(e.target.value)}
                placeholder="רישום הערות ייחודיות..."
                rows={3}
                maxLength={500}
                className="w-full rounded-xl border border-slate-200 bg-slate-100/70 px-3 py-2 text-sm outline-none transition focus:border-brand-500 focus:bg-white focus:ring-2 focus:ring-brand-100"
              />
            </div>
          </div>

          {error && (
            <p className="rounded-xl bg-red-50 px-3 py-2 text-xs text-red-600">{error}</p>
          )}

          {/* Actions */}
          <div className="flex items-center gap-3 border-t border-slate-100 pt-4">
            <button
              type="submit"
              disabled={saving}
              className="flex items-center gap-2 rounded-xl bg-brand-600 px-5 py-2.5 text-sm font-semibold text-white shadow-sm transition hover:bg-brand-700 disabled:opacity-60"
            >
              <SaveIcon />
              {saving ? "שומר..." : "שמירה"}
            </button>
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={onClose}
              className="rounded-xl px-4 py-2.5 text-sm font-medium text-slate-500 hover:bg-slate-100"
            >
              ביטול
            </button>
          </div>
        </form>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}
```

## `components/ReportsScreen.tsx`

```tsx
"use client";

import { useMemo, useState, useEffect, useRef } from "react";
import { DayRecord, HEBREW_MONTHS, MonthData } from "@/lib/types";
import { PencilIcon } from "./Icons";
import { getHolidays, getNonWorkingDays, HolidaySetting } from "@/lib/settingsStore";
import { getDayOfWeekFromIso } from "@/lib/date";

interface Props {
  data?: MonthData;
  year: number;
  month: number;
  onYear: (y: number) => void;
  onMonth: (m: number) => void;
  onEdit: (rec: DayRecord) => void;
  onBulkUpdate: (dates: string[]) => void;
}

const CLASS_STYLES: Record<string, string> = {
  עבודה: "bg-emerald-50 text-emerald-700",
  'סופ"ש': "bg-slate-100 text-slate-500",
  סופשבוע: "bg-slate-100 text-slate-500",
  חופש: "bg-amber-50 text-amber-700",
  מחלה: "bg-rose-50 text-rose-700",
  מילואים: "bg-indigo-50 text-indigo-700",
  חג: "bg-purple-50 text-purple-700",
  "ערב חג": "bg-purple-50 text-purple-600",
  "חול המועד": "bg-purple-50 text-purple-600",
  "עבודה במילואים": "bg-cyan-50 text-cyan-750 border border-cyan-200",
};

function classChip(c: string) {
  const normC = (c || "").replace(/""/g, '"').replace(/^["']|["']$/g, "").trim();
  if (normC === "חופשה") return CLASS_STYLES["חופש"];
  if (normC === 'סופ"ש' || normC === "סופש" || normC.includes("סופ")) return CLASS_STYLES['סופ"ש'];
  return CLASS_STYLES[normC] ?? "bg-slate-100 text-slate-600";
}

export default function ReportsScreen({ data, year, month, onYear, onMonth, onEdit, onBulkUpdate }: Props) {
  const [classFilter, setClassFilter] = useState("הכל");
  const [showMissingOnly, setShowMissingOnly] = useState(false);
  const [search, setSearch] = useState("");
  const [selectedDates, setSelectedDates] = useState<string[]>([]);

  // Holidays & Non-working days settings
  const [holidays, setHolidays] = useState<HolidaySetting[]>([]);
  const [nonWorkingDays, setNonWorkingDays] = useState<number[]>([5, 6]);

  useEffect(() => {
    setHolidays(getHolidays());
    setNonWorkingDays(getNonWorkingDays());

    const handleStorage = () => {
      setHolidays(getHolidays());
      setNonWorkingDays(getNonWorkingDays());
    };
    window.addEventListener("storage", handleStorage);
    return () => window.removeEventListener("storage", handleStorage);
  }, []);

  const holidayDatesSet = useMemo(() => new Set(holidays.map((h) => h.date)), [holidays]);
  const holidayMap = useMemo(() => new Map(holidays.map((h) => [h.date, h])), [holidays]);
  const nonWorkingDaysSet = useMemo(() => new Set(nonWorkingDays), [nonWorkingDays]);

  const todayIso = useMemo(() => {
    const now = new Date();
    const y = now.getFullYear();
    const m = String(now.getMonth() + 1).padStart(2, "0");
    const d = String(now.getDate()).padStart(2, "0");
    return `${y}-${m}-${d}`;
  }, []);

  const todayRowRef = useRef<HTMLTableRowElement | null>(null);

  const records = data?.records ?? [];

  // Automatically scroll to today's row when viewing current month/year
  useEffect(() => {
    const now = new Date();
    if (year === now.getFullYear() && month === now.getMonth() + 1) {
      const timer = setTimeout(() => {
        if (todayRowRef.current) {
          todayRowRef.current.scrollIntoView({
            behavior: "smooth",
            block: "center",
            inline: "nearest",
          });
        }
      }, 250);
      return () => clearTimeout(timer);
    }
  }, [year, month, records.length]);

  // Reset selection when year/month or filters change
  useEffect(() => {
    setSelectedDates([]);
  }, [year, month, showMissingOnly, classFilter]);

  const getEffectiveClassification = (r: DayRecord): string => {
    if (r.classification) {
      return r.classification.replace(/""/g, '"').replace(/^["']|["']$/g, "").trim();
    }
    if (r.date > todayIso) {
      const hol = holidayMap.get(r.date);
      if (hol) return hol.category;
  
      const dow = getDayOfWeekFromIso(r.date);
      if (nonWorkingDaysSet.has(dow)) return 'סופ"ש';
    }
    return "";
  };

  const getEffectiveNotes = (r: DayRecord): string => {
    if (r.notes) return r.notes;
    if (r.date > todayIso) {
      const hol = holidayMap.get(r.date);
      if (hol) return hol.name;
    }
    return "";
  };

  // Check if a day record is a missing report and its type
  const getMissingReportType = (r: DayRecord): "full" | "missing_entry" | "missing_exit" | null => {
    // 1. Must not be a future date
    if (r.date > todayIso) return null;

    // 2. Must not be a defined non-working day (e.g. weekend)


    const dow = getDayOfWeekFromIso(r.date);
    if (nonWorkingDaysSet.has(dow)) return null;

    // 3. Must not be a holiday from the holidays settings table
    if (holidayDatesSet.has(r.date)) return null;

    // 4. Must not have an excused absence / weekend / holiday classification
    const normC = (r.classification || "").replace(/""/g, '"').replace(/^["']|["']$/g, "").trim();
    if (
      normC === "חופש" ||
      normC === "חופשה" ||
      normC === "מחלה" ||
      normC === "מילואים" ||
      normC === "עבודה במילואים" ||
      normC === "חג" ||
      normC === "ערב חג" ||
      normC === "חול המועד" ||
      normC === 'סופ"ש' ||
      normC === "סופש" ||
      normC === "סופשבוע"
    ) {
      return null;
    }

    // 5. Must not have any absence days recorded
    if ((r.vacationDays ?? 0) > 0 || (r.sickDays ?? 0) > 0 || (r.reserveDays ?? 0) > 0) {
      return null;
    }

    // 6. Check entry and exit
    const hasEntry = Boolean(r.entry && r.entry.trim() !== "");
    const hasExit = Boolean(r.exit && r.exit.trim() !== "");

    if (hasEntry && hasExit) {
      return null; // Complete report!
    }

    // If it's today and neither entry nor exit has been reported yet, it's not missing yet
    if (r.date === todayIso && !hasEntry && !hasExit) {
      return null;
    }

    if (!hasEntry && !hasExit) {
      return "full";
    }
    if (!hasEntry && hasExit) {
      return "missing_entry";
    }
    return "missing_exit";
  };

  // Map of dates to their missing type
  const missingMap = useMemo(() => {
    const map = new Map<string, "full" | "missing_entry" | "missing_exit">();
    for (const r of records) {
      const type = getMissingReportType(r);
      if (type) map.set(r.date, type);
    }
    return map;
  }, [records, todayIso, nonWorkingDaysSet, holidayDatesSet]);

  const missingDatesSet = useMemo(() => new Set(missingMap.keys()), [missingMap]);

  const missingBreakdown = useMemo(() => {
    let full = 0;
    let missingEntry = 0;
    let missingExit = 0;
    missingMap.forEach((type) => {
      if (type === "full") full++;
      else if (type === "missing_entry") missingEntry++;
      else if (type === "missing_exit") missingExit++;
    });
    return { full, missingEntry, missingExit };
  }, [missingMap]);

  const classifications = useMemo(() => {
    const s = new Set<string>();
    records.forEach((r) => {
      const c = getEffectiveClassification(r);
      if (c) s.add(c);
    });
    const list = ["הכל", ...Array.from(s)];
    if (missingDatesSet.size > 0) {
      list.push("דיווחים חסרים");
      if (missingBreakdown.full > 0) list.push("דיווח חסר מלא");
      if (missingBreakdown.missingEntry > 0) list.push("דיווח חסר חלקי - כניסה");
      if (missingBreakdown.missingExit > 0) list.push("דיווח חסר חלקי - יציאה");
    }
    return list;
  }, [records, missingDatesSet, missingBreakdown, holidayMap, nonWorkingDaysSet, todayIso]);

  const filtered = useMemo(() => {
    return records.filter((r) => {
      const missingType = missingMap.get(r.date);
      const isMissing = Boolean(missingType);
      const effClass = getEffectiveClassification(r);
      const effNotes = getEffectiveNotes(r);

      if (showMissingOnly && !isMissing) return false;
      if (classFilter === "דיווחים חסרים") {
        if (!isMissing) return false;
      } else if (classFilter === "דיווח חסר מלא") {
        if (missingType !== "full") return false;
      } else if (classFilter === "דיווח חסר חלקי - כניסה") {
        if (missingType !== "missing_entry") return false;
      } else if (classFilter === "דיווח חסר חלקי - יציאה") {
        if (missingType !== "missing_exit") return false;
      } else if (classFilter !== "הכל" && effClass !== classFilter) {
        return false;
      }
      if (search) {
        const hay = `${r.dateDisplay} ${effNotes} ${r.uniqueNotes ?? ""} ${effClass} ${r.dayName ?? ""} ${isMissing ? "חסר דיווח חסרים חלקי כניסה יציאה" : ""}`;
        if (!hay.includes(search)) return false;
      }
      return true;
    });
  }, [records, classFilter, showMissingOnly, search, missingMap, holidayMap, nonWorkingDaysSet, todayIso]);

  const yearOptions = useMemo(() => {
    const current = new Date().getFullYear();
    const ys: number[] = [];
    for (let y = 2019; y <= current + 1; y++) ys.push(y);
    if (!ys.includes(year)) ys.push(year);
    return ys.sort();
  }, [year]);

  const toggleSelectAll = (checked: boolean) => {
    if (checked) {
      setSelectedDates(filtered.map((r) => r.date));
    } else {
      setSelectedDates([]);
    }
  };

  const handleRowCheck = (date: string, checked: boolean) => {
    if (checked) {
      setSelectedDates((prev) => [...prev, date]);
    } else {
      setSelectedDates((prev) => prev.filter((d) => d !== date));
    }
  };

  const selectCls =
    "rounded-xl border border-slate-200 bg-white px-3 py-2 text-sm outline-none transition focus:border-brand-500 focus:ring-2 focus:ring-brand-100";

  return (
    <div className="space-y-4">
      {/* Filters bar */}
      <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2 sm:gap-3 rounded-2xl border border-slate-100 bg-white p-3.5 sm:p-4 shadow-card">
        <select value={year} onChange={(e) => onYear(parseInt(e.target.value, 10))} className={`${selectCls} flex-1 sm:flex-initial min-w-[85px]`} aria-label="שנה">
          {yearOptions.map((y) => (
            <option key={y} value={y}>{y}</option>
          ))}
        </select>
        <select value={month} onChange={(e) => onMonth(parseInt(e.target.value, 10))} className={`${selectCls} flex-1 sm:flex-initial min-w-[95px]`} aria-label="חודש">
          {HEBREW_MONTHS.map((name, i) => (
            <option key={name} value={i + 1}>{name}</option>
          ))}
        </select>
        <select value={classFilter} onChange={(e) => setClassFilter(e.target.value)} className={`${selectCls} flex-1 sm:flex-initial min-w-[95px]`} aria-label="סיווג">
          {classifications.map((c) => (
            <option key={c} value={c}>{c}</option>
          ))}
        </select>
        <button
          type="button"
          onClick={() => setShowMissingOnly((prev) => !prev)}
          className={`flex-1 sm:flex-initial inline-flex items-center justify-center gap-1.5 rounded-xl px-3 py-2 text-xs font-bold transition border cursor-pointer ${
            showMissingOnly
              ? "bg-amber-500 text-white border-amber-600 shadow-sm ring-2 ring-amber-300"
              : missingDatesSet.size > 0
              ? "bg-amber-50 text-amber-900 border-amber-300 hover:bg-amber-100"
              : "bg-slate-50 text-slate-400 border-slate-200 hover:bg-slate-100"
          }`}
          title={
            missingDatesSet.size > 0
              ? `סה"כ דיווחים חסרים: ${missingDatesSet.size} (מלא: ${missingBreakdown.full}, חסר כניסה: ${missingBreakdown.missingEntry}, חסר יציאה: ${missingBreakdown.missingExit})`
              : "אין דיווחים חסרים"
          }
        >
          <span>⚠️</span>
          <span>דיווחים חסרים</span>
          <span
            className={`inline-flex h-5 min-w-[20px] items-center justify-center rounded-full px-1.5 text-[11px] font-extrabold ${
              showMissingOnly
                ? "bg-white text-amber-800"
                : missingDatesSet.size > 0
                ? "bg-amber-200 text-amber-950"
                : "bg-slate-200 text-slate-500"
            }`}
          >
            {missingDatesSet.size}
          </span>
        </button>
        <button
          type="button"
          onClick={() => {
            const now = new Date();
            const curY = now.getFullYear();
            const curM = now.getMonth() + 1;
            if (year !== curY || month !== curM) {
              onYear(curY);
              onMonth(curM);
            }
            setTimeout(() => {
              todayRowRef.current?.scrollIntoView({ behavior: "smooth", block: "center", inline: "nearest" });
            }, 250);
          }}
          className="flex-1 sm:flex-initial inline-flex items-center justify-center gap-1 rounded-xl border border-brand-200 bg-brand-50 px-3 py-2 text-xs font-bold text-brand-700 hover:bg-brand-100 transition cursor-pointer"
          title="קפיצה מהירה לתאריך של היום ומיקוד"
        >
          <span>📍</span>
          <span>היום</span>
        </button>
        <input
          value={search}
          onChange={(e) => setSearch(e.target.value)}
          placeholder="חיפוש בהערות / תאריך..."
          className="w-full sm:w-auto sm:max-w-xs sm:ms-auto rounded-xl border border-slate-200 bg-white px-3 py-2 text-sm outline-none transition focus:border-brand-500 focus:ring-2 focus:ring-brand-100"
        />
      </div>

      {/* Bulk actions bar */}
      {selectedDates.length > 0 && (
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between rounded-2xl border border-brand-200 bg-brand-50/70 px-5 py-3 shadow-sm animate-in fade-in slide-in-from-top-2 duration-200">
          <div className="text-sm font-semibold text-brand-800">
            {selectedDates.length} רשומות נבחרו לעדכון
          </div>
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
            <button
              onClick={() => onBulkUpdate(selectedDates)}
              className="rounded-xl bg-brand-600 px-4 py-1.5 text-xs font-bold text-white shadow-sm transition hover:bg-brand-700"
            >
              עדכון מרוכז
            </button>
            <button
              onClick={() => setSelectedDates([])}
              className="rounded-xl border border-slate-200 bg-white px-3 py-1.5 text-xs font-semibold text-slate-600 transition hover:bg-slate-50"
            >
              ביטול בחירה
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

      {/* Table */}
      <div className="overflow-hidden rounded-2xl border border-slate-100 bg-white shadow-card">
        <div className="overflow-x-auto">
          <table className="w-full min-w-[720px] text-sm">
            <thead>
              <tr className="border-b border-slate-100 bg-slate-50/70 text-xs text-slate-500">
                <th className="px-4 py-3 text-center" style={{ width: "40px" }}>
                  <input
                    type="checkbox"
                    checked={filtered.length > 0 && selectedDates.length === filtered.length}
                    onChange={(e) => toggleSelectAll(e.target.checked)}
                    className="rounded border-slate-300 text-brand-600 focus:ring-brand-500 cursor-pointer"
                  />
                </th>
                <th className="px-4 py-3 text-right font-medium">תאריך</th>
                <th className="px-4 py-3 text-center font-medium">כניסה - יציאה</th>
                <th className="px-4 py-3 text-center font-medium">סה"כ</th>
                <th className="px-4 py-3 text-center font-medium">סיווג</th>
                <th className="px-4 py-3 text-center font-medium">סוג צו</th>
                <th className="px-4 py-3 text-center font-medium">היעדרויות</th>
                <th className="px-4 py-3 text-right font-medium">הערות</th>
                <th className="px-4 py-3 text-center font-medium">פעולה</th>
              </tr>
            </thead>
            <tbody>
              {filtered.map((r) => {
                const absences: string[] = [];
                if (r.vacationDays) absences.push(`חופש ${r.vacationDays}`);
                if (r.sickDays) absences.push(`מחלה ${r.sickDays}`);
                if (r.reserveDays) absences.push(`מילואים ${r.reserveDays}`);
                const missingType = missingMap.get(r.date);
                const isMissing = Boolean(missingType) || Boolean((r.entry && !r.exit) || (!r.entry && r.exit));
                const isToday = r.date === todayIso;
                return (
                  <tr
                    key={r.date}
                    ref={isToday ? todayRowRef : undefined}
                    className={`border-b transition ${
                      isToday
                        ? "bg-blue-50/70 hover:bg-blue-100/70 border-y-2 border-brand-400"
                        : isMissing
                        ? "bg-amber-50/90 hover:bg-amber-100/80 border-s-4 border-s-amber-400 border-b-amber-200/60"
                        : "border-slate-50 hover:bg-slate-50/60"
                    }`}
                  >
                    <td className="px-4 py-2.5 text-center">
                      <input
                        type="checkbox"
                        checked={selectedDates.includes(r.date)}
                        onChange={(e) => handleRowCheck(r.date, e.target.checked)}
                        className="rounded border-slate-300 text-brand-600 focus:ring-brand-500 cursor-pointer"
                      />
                    </td>
                    <td className="px-4 py-2.5">
                      {isToday ? (
                        <div className="relative inline-flex flex-col rounded-xl border-2 border-brand-600 bg-white px-3 py-1.5 shadow-sm animate-today-blink">
                          <span className="absolute -top-2.5 right-2 inline-flex items-center gap-0.5 rounded bg-brand-600 text-white text-[10px] font-black px-1.5 py-0.2 shadow-xs whitespace-nowrap">
                            היום 📍
                          </span>
                          <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 mt-0.5">
                            <span className="font-extrabold text-brand-900 text-sm">
                              {r.dateDisplay}
                            </span>
                          </div>
                          <div className="text-[11px] font-bold text-brand-700">{r.dayName}</div>
                        </div>
                      ) : (
                        <>
                          <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5">
                            {isMissing && (
                              <span
                                className={`h-2 w-2 rounded-full shrink-0 ${
                                  missingType === "missing_exit"
                                    ? "bg-rose-500"
                                    : missingType === "missing_entry"
                                    ? "bg-amber-500"
                                    : "bg-amber-500"
                                }`}
                                title={
                                  missingType === "missing_exit"
                                    ? "דיווח חסר חלקי - יציאה"
                                    : missingType === "missing_entry"
                                    ? "דיווח חסר חלקי - כניסה"
                                    : "דיווח חסר מלא"
                                }
                              />
                            )}
                            <span className={`font-semibold ${isMissing ? "text-amber-950 font-bold" : "text-slate-800"}`}>
                              {r.dateDisplay}
                            </span>
                          </div>
                          <div className="text-[11px] text-slate-400">{r.dayName}</div>
                        </>
                      )}
                    </td>
                    <td className="px-4 py-2.5 text-center text-brand-700">
                      {r.shifts && r.shifts.length > 1 ? (
                        <div className="flex flex-col items-center gap-0.5">
                          <span dir="ltr" className="font-semibold text-xs text-brand-900">
                            {r.entry} — {r.exit}
                          </span>
                          <span
                            className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 rounded-full bg-brand-50 px-2 py-0.5 text-[10px] font-bold text-brand-700 border border-brand-200 cursor-help"
                            title={r.shifts.map((s, i) => `${s.description || `מקטע ${i + 1}`}: ${s.entry} - ${s.exit}`).join("\n")}
                          >
                            <span>{r.shifts.length} מקטעים</span>
                          </span>
                        </div>
                      ) : missingType === "missing_exit" || (r.entry && !r.exit) ? (
                        <div className="flex flex-col items-center gap-0.5">
                          <span dir="ltr" className="font-semibold text-xs text-amber-950">
                            {r.entry} — <span className="text-rose-500 font-extrabold">--:--</span>
                          </span>
                          <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-0.5 rounded-full bg-rose-50 px-2 py-0.2 text-[10px] font-bold text-rose-700 border border-rose-200">
                            חסרה יציאה
                          </span>
                        </div>
                      ) : missingType === "missing_entry" || (!r.entry && r.exit) ? (
                        <div className="flex flex-col items-center gap-0.5">
                          <span dir="ltr" className="font-semibold text-xs text-amber-950">
                            <span className="text-rose-500 font-extrabold">--:--</span> — {r.exit}
                          </span>
                          <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-0.5 rounded-full bg-amber-100/90 px-2 py-0.2 text-[10px] font-bold text-amber-900 border border-amber-300">
                            חסרה כניסה
                          </span>
                        </div>
                      ) : missingType === "full" ? (
                        <span className="inline-block rounded-md bg-amber-100/90 px-2 py-0.5 text-xs font-bold text-amber-800 border border-amber-200">
                          לא מולא
                        </span>
                      ) : r.entry && r.exit ? (
                        <span dir="ltr" className="font-medium">
                          {r.entry} — {r.exit}
                        </span>
                      ) : (
                        <span className="text-slate-300">—</span>
                      )}
                    </td>
                    <td className="px-4 py-2.5 text-center font-bold text-slate-800">
                      {isMissing ? (
                        <span className="font-semibold text-amber-600 text-xs">0.0</span>
                      ) : (
                        <div>
                          <div>{(r.totalHoursDecimal || 0).toFixed(1)}</div>
                          {(r.overtimeDecimal || 0) > 0 && (
                            <div className="text-[10px] font-extrabold text-indigo-600 bg-indigo-50 border border-indigo-100 rounded-full px-1.5 py-0.2 mt-0.5 inline-block">
                              +{(r.overtimeDecimal || 0).toFixed(1)} ש"נ
                            </div>
                          )}
                        </div>
                      )}
                    </td>
                    <td className="px-4 py-2.5 text-center">
                      {missingType === "full" ? (
                        <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 rounded-full border border-amber-300 bg-amber-200/80 px-2.5 py-0.5 text-xs font-bold text-amber-900 shadow-2xs whitespace-nowrap">
                          <span>⚠️</span>
                          <span>דיווח חסר מלא</span>
                        </span>
                      ) : missingType === "missing_entry" || (!r.entry && r.exit) ? (
                        <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 rounded-full border border-amber-300 bg-amber-100 px-2.5 py-0.5 text-xs font-bold text-amber-900 shadow-2xs whitespace-nowrap">
                          <span>⚠️</span>
                          <span>חסר חלקי - כניסה</span>
                        </span>
                      ) : missingType === "missing_exit" || (r.entry && !r.exit) ? (
                        <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 rounded-full border border-rose-200 bg-rose-50 px-2.5 py-0.5 text-xs font-bold text-rose-800 shadow-2xs whitespace-nowrap">
                          <span>⚠️</span>
                          <span>חסר חלקי - יציאה</span>
                        </span>
                      ) : (() => {
                        const effC = getEffectiveClassification(r);
                        return effC ? (
                          <span className={`inline-block rounded-full px-2.5 py-0.5 text-xs font-medium ${classChip(effC)}`}>
                            {effC}
                          </span>
                        ) : null;
                      })()}
                    </td>
                    <td className="px-4 py-2.5 text-center text-xs font-bold text-indigo-600">
                      {r.orderType || <span className="text-slate-300">—</span>}
                    </td>
                    <td className="px-4 py-2.5 text-center text-xs text-amber-700">
                      {absences.length ? absences.join(" · ") : <span className="text-slate-300">—</span>}
                    </td>
                    <td className="max-w-[220px] px-4 py-2.5 text-xs text-slate-500">
                      {(() => {
                        const effNotes = getEffectiveNotes(r);
                        return (
                          <>
                            {effNotes && <div className="truncate text-slate-700 font-medium" title={effNotes}>{effNotes}</div>}
                            {r.uniqueNotes && <div className="truncate text-brand-600 font-bold mt-0.5" title={r.uniqueNotes}>{r.uniqueNotes}</div>}
                            {!effNotes && !r.uniqueNotes && <span className="text-slate-300">—</span>}
                          </>
                        );
                      })()}
                    </td>
                    <td className="px-4 py-2.5 text-center">
                      <button
                        onClick={() => onEdit(r)}
                        aria-label={`עריכת ${r.dateDisplay}`}
                        className={`rounded-lg p-2 transition ${
                          isMissing
                            ? "bg-amber-100 text-amber-800 hover:bg-amber-200"
                            : "text-slate-400 hover:bg-brand-50 hover:text-brand-600"
                        }`}
                      >
                        <PencilIcon />
                      </button>
                    </td>
                  </tr>
                );
              })}
              {!filtered.length && (
                <tr>
                  <td colSpan={9} className="px-4 py-12 text-center text-sm">
                    {showMissingOnly || classFilter === "דיווחים חסרים" ? (
                      <div className="flex flex-col items-center justify-center gap-2 text-slate-500">
                        <span className="text-3xl">🎉</span>
                        <span className="font-bold text-slate-700">כל הכבוד! אין דיווחים חסרים לחודש זה</span>
                        <span className="text-xs text-slate-400">כל ימי העבודה בעבר מולאו או הוגדרו כחופשה/חג/סופ"ש כראוי.</span>
                        <button
                          type="button"
                          onClick={() => {
                            setShowMissingOnly(false);
                            if (classFilter === "דיווחים חסרים") setClassFilter("הכל");
                          }}
                          className="mt-2 rounded-lg bg-slate-100 px-3 py-1.5 text-xs font-semibold text-slate-600 hover:bg-slate-200 transition cursor-pointer"
                        >
                          הצג את כל הדיווחים
                        </button>
                      </div>
                    ) : (
                      <span className="text-slate-400">אין רשומות להצגה בחודש זה</span>
                    )}
                  </td>
                </tr>
              )}
            </tbody>
          </table>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}
```
