## `components/guide/DashboardDemo.tsx`

```tsx
"use client";

import { useState, useEffect } from "react";
import {
  ClockIcon,
  PlusIcon,
  ChevronRight,
  ChevronLeft,
  GridIcon,
  ListIcon,
  ShieldIcon,
  BookOpenIcon,
  RefreshIcon,
  LogOutIcon,
  TrendIcon,
  UmbrellaIcon,
  BriefcaseIcon,
  AlertTriangleIcon,
} from "../Icons";

export default function DashboardDemo() {
  const [step, setStep] = useState(0);
  const [isPlaying, setIsPlaying] = useState(true);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!isPlaying) return;
    const timer = setInterval(() => {
      setStep((prev) => (prev + 1) % 4);
    }, 2800);
    return () => clearInterval(timer);
  }, [isPlaying]);

  return (
    <div className="overflow-hidden rounded-2xl border border-slate-700/30 bg-slate-950 shadow-2xl">
      {/* Simulation Bar */}
      <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between border-b border-slate-800 bg-slate-900/90 px-4 py-3 text-slate-300 gap-2">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
          <span className="h-3 w-3 rounded-full bg-rose-500/80 inline-block" />
          <span className="h-3 w-3 rounded-full bg-amber-500/80 inline-block" />
          <span className="h-3 w-3 rounded-full bg-emerald-500/80 inline-block" />
          <span className="mr-2 text-xs font-bold text-white flex items-center gap-1.5">
            <span className="inline-block h-2 w-2 rounded-full bg-emerald-400 animate-ping" />
            הדמיה מונפשת
          </span>
        </div>
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => setIsPlaying(!isPlaying)}
            className="flex items-center gap-1 rounded-lg bg-slate-800 px-3 py-1 text-xs font-bold text-slate-200 hover:bg-slate-700 transition"
          >
            <span>{isPlaying ? "⏸️ השהה" : "▶️ נגן"}</span>
          </button>
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => setStep(0)}
            className="rounded-lg bg-slate-800 px-2 py-1 text-xs font-bold text-slate-300 hover:bg-slate-700 transition"
            title="הפעל מחדש"
          >
            🔄
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Screen Container (Exact replica of real application layout) */}
      <div className="relative bg-[#f8fafc] p-3 sm:p-5 select-none overflow-hidden min-h-[520px]">
        {/* 1. REAL HEADER REPLICA */}
        <header className="rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 bg-white p-3 shadow-xs mb-4">
          <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-3">
            {/* Title (Right in RTL) */}
            <div className="flex items-center gap-2.5">
              <span className="flex h-9 w-9 items-center justify-center rounded-xl bg-brand-600 text-white shadow-xs">
                <ClockIcon className="h-5 w-5" />
              </span>
              <div className="leading-tight">
                <div className="text-sm font-bold text-slate-900">ניהול דיווח שעות</div>
                <div className="text-[10px] font-medium tracking-wide text-slate-400">
                  24H WORK DASHBOARD
                </div>
              </div>
            </div>

            {/* Navigation Tabs (Center) */}
            <nav className="flex items-center rounded-xl border border-slate-200 bg-slate-50 p-1 text-xs">
              <span className="flex items-center gap-1.5 rounded-lg bg-white px-3 py-1.5 font-bold text-brand-700 shadow-xs border border-slate-200/80">
                <GridIcon className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
                <span>לוח בקרה</span>
              </span>
              <span className="flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-1.5 font-medium text-slate-600">
                <ListIcon className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
                <span>דיווחים</span>
              </span>
              <span className="flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-1.5 font-medium text-slate-600">
                <BookOpenIcon className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
                <span>הדרכה</span>
              </span>
              <span className="flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-1.5 font-medium text-slate-600">
                <ShieldIcon className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
                <span>הגדרות</span>
              </span>
            </nav>

            {/* Controls (Left in RTL) */}
            <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
              <div
                className={`relative flex items-center rounded-xl border bg-slate-50 px-2.5 py-1 text-xs font-bold gap-1.5 transition-all duration-300 ${
                  step === 3
                    ? "border-brand-500 ring-2 ring-brand-300 text-brand-700 bg-brand-50/70 shadow-sm scale-105"
                    : "border-slate-200 text-slate-700"
                }`}
              >
                {step === 3 && (
                  <div className="absolute -bottom-8 left-1/2 -translate-x-1/2 z-30 pointer-events-none drop-shadow-xl animate-pulse">
                    <div className="relative">
                      <svg
                        className="w-6 h-6 text-slate-950 fill-slate-950 drop-shadow-md -rotate-45"
                        viewBox="0 0 24 24"
                      >
                        <path d="M4 0l16 12.279-6.951 1.17 4.325 8.817-3.596 1.734-4.35-8.879-5.428 5.43z" />
                      </svg>
                      <span className="absolute -top-1 -right-1 h-6 w-6 rounded-full bg-brand-500/50 animate-ping pointer-events-none" />
                    </div>
                  </div>
                )}
                <ChevronRight className="h-3.5 w-3.5 text-slate-400" />
                <span>אוקטובר 2026</span>
                <ChevronLeft className="h-3.5 w-3.5 text-slate-400" />
              </div>
              <span className="rounded-xl border border-slate-200 bg-slate-50 p-1.5 text-slate-500">
                <RefreshIcon className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
              </span>
              <span className="rounded-xl border border-slate-200 bg-slate-50 p-1.5 text-slate-500">
                <LogOutIcon className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
              </span>
              <span className="flex items-center gap-1 rounded-xl bg-brand-600 px-3 py-1.5 text-xs font-bold text-white shadow-xs">
                <PlusIcon className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
                <span>דיווח חדש</span>
              </span>
            </div>
          </div>
        </header>

        {/* 2. REAL STATUS BANNER REPLICA */}
        <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-2 rounded-xl border border-emerald-200/90 bg-emerald-50/70 px-4 py-2 text-xs text-emerald-900 shadow-2xs mb-4">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5">
            <span className="font-semibold text-emerald-950">מחובר למסד הנתונים Supabase</span>
            <span className="text-emerald-800 text-[11px]">
              · נטענו 3,013 דיווחים בסך הכל (01/10/2019 — 30/12/2027)
            </span>
          </div>
          <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5">
            <span className="text-slate-500 font-normal">תנאי העסקה לחודש זה:</span>
            <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 rounded-md bg-emerald-100/90 px-2 py-0.5 font-bold text-emerald-800 border border-emerald-200 text-[11px]">
              חודשי עם שעות נוספות (ברירת מחדל)
              <span className="text-emerald-700">(100%)</span>
            </span>
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* 3. REAL 5 KPI CARDS REPLICA */}
        <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-3 sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-5 mb-4">
          {/* Card 1: שעות שדווחו */}
          <div
            className={`relative rounded-2xl bg-white p-4 shadow-card transition-all duration-300 ${
              step === 0
                ? "border-2 border-brand-500 ring-2 ring-brand-100 shadow-md scale-[1.02]"
                : "border border-slate-100"
            }`}
          >
            {step === 0 && (
              <div className="absolute -top-3 -right-3 z-30 pointer-events-none drop-shadow-xl animate-pulse">
                <div className="relative">
                  <svg
                    className="w-6 h-6 text-slate-950 fill-slate-950 drop-shadow-md"
                    viewBox="0 0 24 24"
                  >
                    <path d="M4 0l16 12.279-6.951 1.17 4.325 8.817-3.596 1.734-4.35-8.879-5.428 5.43z" />
                  </svg>
                  <span className="absolute -top-1 -right-1 h-6 w-6 rounded-full bg-brand-500/50 animate-ping pointer-events-none" />
                </div>
              </div>
            )}
            <div className="flex items-start justify-between">
              <div className="space-y-0.5">
                <p className="text-xs text-slate-500 font-medium">שעות שדווחו</p>
                <p className="text-xl font-black text-slate-900">84.8 <span className="text-sm font-normal text-slate-500">שעות</span></p>
              </div>
              <span className="flex h-10 w-10 items-center justify-center rounded-xl bg-brand-50 text-brand-600">
                <ClockIcon className="h-5 w-5" />
              </span>
            </div>
            <div className="mt-2.5 text-[11px] text-slate-400">
              <div>מתוך יעד של 189.0</div>
              <div className="font-bold text-indigo-600 mt-0.5 flex items-center gap-1">
                <span>⏱️ 7.2 שעות נוספות</span>
                <span className="text-emerald-600 font-semibold">(תוספת 202.5 ₪)</span>
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>

          {/* Card 2: שעות שנותרו */}
          <div className="rounded-2xl border border-slate-100 bg-white p-4 shadow-card">
            <div className="flex items-start justify-between">
              <div className="space-y-0.5">
                <p className="text-xs text-slate-500 font-medium">שעות שנותרו</p>
                <p className="text-xl font-black text-slate-900">104.2 <span className="text-sm font-normal text-slate-500">שעות</span></p>
              </div>
              <span className="flex h-10 w-10 items-center justify-center rounded-xl bg-emerald-50 text-emerald-600">
                <TrendIcon className="h-5 w-5" />
              </span>
            </div>
            <div className="mt-2.5 text-[11px] text-slate-400">
              <div>כ- 11.6 ליום</div>
            </div>
          </div>

          {/* Card 3: יתרת חופשה לניצול */}
          <div className="rounded-2xl border border-slate-100 bg-white p-4 shadow-card">
            <div className="flex items-start justify-between">
              <div className="space-y-0.5">
                <p className="text-xs text-slate-500 font-medium">יתרת חופשה לניצול</p>
                <p className="text-xl font-black text-rose-600">45.3 <span className="text-sm font-normal text-rose-500">ימים</span></p>
              </div>
              <span className="flex h-10 w-10 items-center justify-center rounded-xl bg-amber-50 text-amber-600">
                <UmbrellaIcon className="h-5 w-5" />
              </span>
            </div>
            <div className="mt-2.5 text-[11px] text-slate-400">
              <div>1 נוצלו החודש</div>
              <div className="text-[10px] text-amber-700 font-medium mt-0.5">
                הערכה לסוף השנה (כולל עתידיים): 49.0 ימים
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>

          {/* Card 4: מילואים / מחלה */}
          <div className="rounded-2xl border border-slate-100 bg-white p-4 shadow-card">
            <div className="flex items-start justify-between">
              <div className="space-y-0.5">
                <p className="text-xs text-slate-500 font-medium">מילואים / מחלה</p>
                <p className="text-xl font-black text-slate-900">0 / 3</p>
              </div>
              <span className="flex h-10 w-10 items-center justify-center rounded-xl bg-blue-50 text-blue-600">
                <BriefcaseIcon className="h-5 w-5" />
              </span>
            </div>
            <div className="mt-2.5 text-[11px] text-slate-400">
              <div>ימי היעדרות החודש</div>
            </div>
          </div>

          {/* Card 5: דיווחים חסרים */}
          <div className={`rounded-2xl bg-white p-4 shadow-card transition-all duration-300 ${
            step === 0
              ? "border-2 border-amber-300 ring-2 ring-amber-100"
              : "border border-slate-100"
          }`}>
            <div className="flex items-start justify-between">
              <div className="space-y-0.5">
                <p className="text-xs text-slate-500 font-medium">דיווחים חסרים</p>
                <p className="text-xl font-black text-emerald-600">0 ימים</p>
              </div>
              <span className="flex h-10 w-10 items-center justify-center rounded-xl bg-emerald-50 text-emerald-600">
                <AlertTriangleIcon className="h-5 w-5" />
              </span>
            </div>
            <div className="mt-2.5 text-[11px] font-bold text-emerald-700">
              מעולה! אין דיווחים חסרים
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* 4. REAL DAILY CHART & MONTHLY SUMMARY ROW */}
        <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-4 lg:grid-cols-3">
          {/* Monthly Summary */}
          <div
            className={`relative rounded-2xl bg-white p-4 shadow-card space-y-2.5 text-xs transition-all duration-300 ${
              step === 2
                ? "border-2 border-brand-500 ring-2 ring-brand-100 shadow-md scale-[1.01]"
                : "border border-slate-100"
            }`}
          >
            {step === 2 && (
              <div className="absolute -top-3 -right-3 z-30 pointer-events-none drop-shadow-xl animate-pulse">
                <div className="relative">
                  <svg
                    className="w-6 h-6 text-slate-950 fill-slate-950 drop-shadow-md"
                    viewBox="0 0 24 24"
                  >
                    <path d="M4 0l16 12.279-6.951 1.17 4.325 8.817-3.596 1.734-4.35-8.879-5.428 5.43z" />
                  </svg>
                  <span className="absolute -top-1 -right-1 h-6 w-6 rounded-full bg-brand-500/50 animate-ping pointer-events-none" />
                </div>
              </div>
            )}
            <div className="flex items-center justify-between border-b border-slate-100 pb-2">
              <h3 className="font-bold text-slate-800">סיכום סטטוס חודשי</h3>
              <span className="text-[11px] font-semibold text-indigo-600">פירוט ש"נ ⏱️</span>
            </div>
            <div className="flex items-center justify-between rounded-xl bg-slate-50 p-2.5">
              <span className="text-slate-600">יעד חודשי:</span>
              <span className="font-bold text-brand-700">189.0 שעות</span>
            </div>
            <div className="flex items-center justify-between rounded-xl bg-slate-50 p-2.5">
              <span className="text-slate-600">דווח עד כה:</span>
              <span className="font-bold text-brand-700">84.8 שעות</span>
            </div>
            <div className="flex items-center justify-between rounded-xl bg-slate-50 p-2.5">
              <span className="text-slate-600">נותרו להשלמה:</span>
              <span className="font-bold text-brand-700">104.2 שעות</span>
            </div>
          </div>

          {/* Daily Bar Chart */}
          <div
            className={`lg:col-span-2 rounded-2xl bg-white p-4 shadow-card transition-all duration-300 ${
              step === 1 ? "border-2 border-brand-500 ring-2 ring-brand-100" : "border border-slate-100"
            }`}
          >
            <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-2">
              <h3 className="text-xs font-bold text-slate-800">פעילות שעות יומית</h3>
              {step === 1 && (
                <span className="rounded-full bg-brand-600 text-white text-[10px] font-bold px-2 py-0.5 animate-pulse">
                  ריחוף: יום 14/10 נבחר
                </span>
              )}
            </div>
            <div className="relative flex items-end justify-between gap-1 h-36 pt-10 px-2 border-b border-slate-100">
              {[
                { d: 1, h: 9 }, { d: 2, h: 9 }, { d: 3, h: 0 }, { d: 4, h: 11.75 }, { d: 5, h: 9 },
                { d: 6, h: 9 }, { d: 7, h: 9 }, { d: 8, h: 9 }, { d: 9, h: 0 }, { d: 10, h: 0 },
                { d: 11, h: 9 }, { d: 12, h: 9 }, { d: 13, h: 9 }, { d: 14, h: 10.6 }, { d: 15, h: 8.5 },
                { d: 16, h: 9 }, { d: 17, h: 0 }, { d: 18, h: 0 }, { d: 19, h: 9 }, { d: 20, h: 9 },
              ].map((bar, i) => {
                const isTarget = bar.d === 14;
                const heightPct = bar.h === 0 ? "4%" : `${(bar.h / 12) * 100}%`;
                return (
                  <div key={i} className="flex-1 flex flex-col items-center h-full justify-end relative">
                    {/* Tooltip & Cursor directly on Day 14 bar */}
                    {isTarget && step === 1 && (
                      <>
                        {/* Hover Tooltip Popup */}
                        <div className="absolute -top-12 left-1/2 -translate-x-1/2 z-40 whitespace-nowrap rounded-lg bg-slate-900 px-2.5 py-1 text-[11px] font-bold text-white shadow-xl flex items-center gap-1.5 border border-slate-700 pointer-events-none animate-bounce">
                          <span>14/10:</span>
                          <span className="text-amber-400 font-black">10.6 ש'</span>
                          <span className="text-[10px] text-emerald-400 font-semibold">(+1.6 ש"נ)</span>
                          <div className="absolute -bottom-1 left-1/2 -translate-x-1/2 w-2 h-2 bg-slate-900 rotate-45 border-r border-b border-slate-700" />
                        </div>

                        {/* Virtual Cursor directly on the bar */}
                        <div className="absolute -top-5 -right-2 z-50 pointer-events-none drop-shadow-xl animate-pulse">
                          <div className="relative">
                            <svg
                              className="w-6 h-6 text-slate-950 fill-slate-950 drop-shadow-md"
                              viewBox="0 0 24 24"
                            >
                              <path d="M4 0l16 12.279-6.951 1.17 4.325 8.817-3.596 1.734-4.35-8.879-5.428 5.43z" />
                            </svg>
                            <span className="absolute -top-1 -right-1 h-6 w-6 rounded-full bg-amber-500/50 animate-ping pointer-events-none" />
                          </div>
                        </div>
                      </>
                    )}
                    <div
                      style={{ height: heightPct }}
                      className={`w-full max-w-[14px] rounded-t-sm transition-all duration-300 ${
                        bar.h === 0
                          ? "bg-slate-200"
                          : isTarget && step === 1
                          ? "bg-amber-500 ring-2 ring-amber-300 shadow-md scale-110"
                          : bar.h > 9
                          ? "bg-indigo-600"
                          : "bg-brand-600"
                      }`}
                    />
                  </div>
                );
              })}
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Progress Timeline Tabs */}
      <div className="border-t border-slate-800 bg-slate-950 p-4">
        <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-2 sm:grid-cols-4 text-center text-xs">
          {[
            { num: 1, label: "5 כרטיסי KPI ומדדים" },
            { num: 2, label: "גרף שעות וריחוף יומי" },
            { num: 3, label: "סיכום סטטוס ותחזית" },
            { num: 4, label: "תפריט וסרגל ניווט" },
          ].map((st, i) => (
            <button
              key={st.num}
              type="button"
              onClick={() => setStep(i)}
              className={`rounded-xl p-2.5 transition text-center min-h-[58px] flex flex-col justify-center ${
                step === i
                  ? "bg-brand-600 text-white font-black shadow-md ring-2 ring-brand-400/50"
                  : "bg-slate-900 text-slate-400 hover:bg-slate-800 hover:text-slate-200"
              }`}
            >
              <div className="text-[10px] opacity-75 font-semibold">שלב {st.num}</div>
              <div className="text-xs font-bold mt-0.5 leading-snug break-words">{st.label}</div>
            </button>
          ))}
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}
```

## `components/guide/ImportDemo.tsx`

```tsx
"use client";

import { useState, useEffect } from "react";
import { UploadIcon, CheckCircleIcon, SparklesIcon } from "../Icons";

export default function ImportDemo() {
  const [step, setStep] = useState(0);
  const [isPlaying, setIsPlaying] = useState(true);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!isPlaying) return;
    const timer = setInterval(() => {
      setStep((prev) => (prev + 1) % 4);
    }, 2800);
    return () => clearInterval(timer);
  }, [isPlaying]);

  return (
    <div className="overflow-hidden rounded-2xl border border-slate-700/30 bg-slate-950 shadow-2xl">
      {/* Header */}
      <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between border-b border-slate-800 bg-slate-900/90 px-4 py-3 text-slate-300 gap-2">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
          <span className="h-3 w-3 rounded-full bg-rose-500/80 inline-block" />
          <span className="h-3 w-3 rounded-full bg-amber-500/80 inline-block" />
          <span className="h-3 w-3 rounded-full bg-emerald-500/80 inline-block" />
          <span className="mr-2 text-xs font-bold text-white flex items-center gap-1.5">
            <span className="inline-block h-2 w-2 rounded-full bg-emerald-400 animate-ping" />
            הדמיה מונפשת
          </span>
        </div>
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => setIsPlaying(!isPlaying)}
            className="flex items-center gap-1 rounded-lg bg-slate-800 px-3 py-1 text-xs font-bold text-slate-200 hover:bg-slate-700 transition"
          >
            <span>{isPlaying ? "⏸️ השהה" : "▶️ נגן"}</span>
          </button>
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => setStep(0)}
            className="rounded-lg bg-slate-800 px-2 py-1 text-xs font-bold text-slate-300 hover:bg-slate-700 transition"
            title="הפעל מחדש"
          >
            🔄
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Screen Area */}
      <div className="relative bg-slate-100 p-4 sm:p-6 select-none overflow-hidden min-h-[400px]">
        {/* Dropzone & Preview Mockup */}
        <div className="max-w-lg mx-auto space-y-3">
          {step === 0 ? (
            /* Step 0: Empty Dropzone */
            <div className="rounded-2xl border-2 border-dashed border-slate-300 bg-white p-8 text-center shadow-xs transition hover:border-brand-400">
              <div className="mx-auto flex h-12 w-12 items-center justify-center rounded-2xl bg-brand-50 text-brand-600 mb-3">
                <UploadIcon className="w-6 h-6" />
              </div>
              <div className="text-sm font-black text-slate-800">בחר קובץ CSV או Excel להעלאה</div>
              <p className="text-xs text-slate-500 mt-1">תומך בפלט שעון נוכחות: חילן, מלם, הר-גל או קובץ מקומי</p>
              <div className="mt-4 relative inline-block">
                <div className="rounded-xl bg-brand-50 border border-brand-200 px-4 py-2 text-xs font-bold text-brand-700 shadow-2xs">
                  📄 גרור קובץ לכאן
                </div>
                {/* Step 0 Virtual Cursor sitting on the dropzone */}
                <div className="absolute -bottom-6 left-1/2 -translate-x-1/2 z-40 pointer-events-none drop-shadow-xl animate-pulse">
                  <div className="relative">
                    <svg className="w-6 h-6 text-slate-950 fill-slate-950 drop-shadow-md -rotate-45" viewBox="0 0 24 24">
                      <path d="M4 0l16 12.279-6.951 1.17 4.325 8.817-3.596 1.734-4.35-8.879-5.428 5.43z" />
                    </svg>
                    <span className="absolute -top-1 -right-1 h-6 w-6 rounded-full bg-brand-500/50 animate-ping pointer-events-none" />
                  </div>
                </div>
              </div>
            </div>
          ) : step === 1 ? (
            /* Step 1: File Dropped & Parsing */
            <div className="rounded-2xl border-2 border-brand-500 bg-brand-50/50 p-6 text-center shadow-md animate-fadeIn">
              <span className="inline-block p-3 rounded-full bg-brand-600 text-white mb-2 shadow-xs">
                📄
              </span>
              <div className="text-sm font-black text-brand-900">אוקטובר 2026.csv נטען בהצלחה!</div>
              <p className="text-xs text-brand-700 mt-1">סורק נתונים, מזהה כותרות ומאחד מקטעי עבודה...</p>
              <div className="mt-3 h-2 w-48 mx-auto rounded-full bg-brand-200 overflow-hidden">
                <div className="h-full bg-brand-600 rounded-full animate-pulse w-3/4" />
              </div>
            </div>
          ) : (
            /* Step 2 & 3: Preview Table & Success */
            <div className="rounded-2xl bg-white p-4 shadow-xl border border-slate-200 animate-fadeIn space-y-3">
              <div className="flex items-center justify-between border-b border-slate-100 pb-2.5">
                <div>
                  <h5 className="text-xs font-black text-slate-900">סיכום נתוני הקובץ שזוהו</h5>
                  <p className="text-[11px] text-slate-500">22 ימי עבודה • 195.5 שעות עבודה</p>
                </div>
                <span className="rounded-full bg-emerald-100 text-emerald-800 font-bold text-[11px] px-2.5 py-0.5 border border-emerald-200">
                  זיהוי תקין 100%
                </span>
              </div>

              <div className="space-y-1.5 text-xs">
                <div className="flex items-center justify-between bg-slate-50 p-2 rounded-lg">
                  <span className="text-slate-600 font-bold">איחוד מקטעים מרובים:</span>
                  <span className="font-extrabold text-blue-700">04/10 (2 מקטעים), 14/10 (2 מקטעים)</span>
                </div>
                <div className="flex items-center justify-between bg-slate-50 p-2 rounded-lg">
                  <span className="text-slate-600 font-bold">סיווגי חגים אוטומטיים:</span>
                  <span className="font-extrabold text-purple-700">חוה"מ, הושע"ר, שמחת תורה</span>
                </div>
              </div>

              {step === 2 ? (
                <div className="relative">
                  <button
                    type="button"
                    className="w-full rounded-xl bg-brand-600 py-2.5 text-center text-xs font-black text-white shadow-md ring-2 ring-brand-300 scale-102 transition"
                  >
                    ✓ אשר ושמור 22 דיווחים במערכת
                  </button>
                  {/* Step 2 Cursor sitting directly on the confirm button */}
                  <div className="absolute -bottom-6 left-1/2 -translate-x-1/2 z-40 pointer-events-none drop-shadow-xl animate-pulse">
                    <div className="relative">
                      <svg className="w-6 h-6 text-slate-950 fill-slate-950 drop-shadow-md -rotate-45" viewBox="0 0 24 24">
                        <path d="M4 0l16 12.279-6.951 1.17 4.325 8.817-3.596 1.734-4.35-8.879-5.428 5.43z" />
                      </svg>
                      <span className="absolute -top-1 -right-1 h-6 w-6 rounded-full bg-brand-500/50 animate-ping pointer-events-none" />
                    </div>
                  </div>
                </div>
              ) : (
                <div className="rounded-xl bg-emerald-600 py-2.5 text-center text-xs font-black text-white shadow-lg animate-bounce flex items-center justify-center gap-1.5">
                  <CheckCircleIcon className="w-4 h-4 text-emerald-200" />
                  <span>כל הדיווחים נשמרו בהצלחה במסד הנתונים!</span>
                </div>
              )}
            </div>
          )}
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Progress Timeline Tabs */}
      <div className="border-t border-slate-800 bg-slate-950 p-4">
        <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-2 sm:grid-cols-4 text-center text-xs">
          {[
            { num: 1, label: "בחירת קובץ CSV" },
            { num: 2, label: "זיהוי ואיחוד מקטעים" },
            { num: 3, label: "תצוגה מקדימה" },
            { num: 4, label: "שמירה אוטומטית!" },
          ].map((st, i) => (
            <button
              key={st.num}
              type="button"
              onClick={() => setStep(i)}
              className={`rounded-xl p-2.5 transition text-center min-h-[58px] flex flex-col justify-center ${
                step === i
                  ? "bg-brand-600 text-white font-black shadow-md ring-2 ring-brand-400/50"
                  : "bg-slate-900 text-slate-400 hover:bg-slate-800 hover:text-slate-200"
              }`}
            >
              <div className="text-[10px] opacity-75 font-semibold">שלב {st.num}</div>
              <div className="text-xs font-bold mt-0.5 leading-snug break-words">{st.label}</div>
            </button>
          ))}
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}
```

```tsx
"use client";

import { useState, useEffect } from "react";
import { AlertTriangleIcon, PencilIcon, XIcon } from "../Icons";

export default function MissingReportsDemo() {
  const [step, setStep] = useState(0);
  const [isPlaying, setIsPlaying] = useState(true);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!isPlaying) return;
    const timer = setInterval(() => {
      setStep((prev) => (prev + 1) % 4);
    }, 2800);
    return () => clearInterval(timer);
  }, [isPlaying]);

  return (
    <div className="overflow-hidden rounded-2xl border border-slate-700/30 bg-slate-950 shadow-2xl">
      {/* Simulation Header */}
      <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between border-b border-slate-800 bg-slate-900/90 px-4 py-3 text-slate-300 gap-2">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
          <span className="h-3 w-3 rounded-full bg-rose-500/80 inline-block" />
          <span className="h-3 w-3 rounded-full bg-amber-500/80 inline-block" />
          <span className="h-3 w-3 rounded-full bg-emerald-500/80 inline-block" />
          <span className="mr-2 text-xs font-bold text-white flex items-center gap-1.5">
            <span className="inline-block h-2 w-2 rounded-full bg-emerald-400 animate-ping" />
            הדמיה מונפשת
          </span>
        </div>
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => setIsPlaying(!isPlaying)}
            className="flex items-center gap-1 rounded-lg bg-slate-800 px-3 py-1 text-xs font-bold text-slate-200 hover:bg-slate-700 transition"
          >
            <span>{isPlaying ? "⏸️ השהה" : "▶️ נגן"}</span>
          </button>
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => setStep(0)}
            className="rounded-lg bg-slate-800 px-2 py-1 text-xs font-bold text-slate-300 hover:bg-slate-700 transition"
            title="הפעל מחדש"
          >
            🔄
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Screen Backdrop */}
      <div className="relative bg-slate-900/50 p-3 sm:p-6 select-none overflow-hidden min-h-[520px] flex items-center justify-center backdrop-blur-xs">
        {/* Real MissingReportsModal Replica */}
        <div className="w-full max-w-2xl rounded-3xl bg-white shadow-2xl border border-slate-200 overflow-hidden flex flex-col">
          {/* Header */}
          <div className="flex items-center justify-between border-b border-slate-100 p-4 sm:px-6">
            <span className="p-1 text-slate-400">
              <XIcon className="h-5 w-5" />
            </span>
            <div className="flex items-center gap-3 text-right">
              <div>
                <h3 className="text-base font-black text-slate-900">דיווחים חסרים במערכת</h3>
                <span className="inline-block rounded-md bg-blue-50 px-2 py-0.5 text-xs font-semibold text-blue-700 mt-0.5">
                  {step === 0 ? "נמצאו 7 ימי עבודה בעבר ללא דיווח (כולל חודשים קודמים)" : step === 1 ? "נמצאו 6 ימי עבודה בעבר ללא דיווח" : "נמצאו 5 ימי עבודה בעבר ללא דיווח"}
                </span>
              </div>
              <span className="flex h-10 w-10 items-center justify-center rounded-2xl bg-amber-100 text-amber-600">
                <AlertTriangleIcon className="h-5 w-5" />
              </span>
            </div>
          </div>

          {/* Search & Filter Bar */}
          <div className="border-b border-slate-100 bg-slate-50/50 p-4 sm:px-6">
            <div className="flex gap-3">
              <div className="flex-1 rounded-xl border border-slate-200 bg-white px-3 py-2 text-xs text-slate-400">

              <div className="rounded-xl border border-slate-200 bg-white px-3 py-2 text-xs font-bold text-slate-700 flex items-center gap-1">

                <span>▼</span>

            </div>

          <div className="p-4 sm:px-6 space-y-2.5 max-h-[320px] overflow-hidden">

              <span>פעולות מהירות לעדכון</span>
              <div className="flex items-center gap-2 relative">
                <span className={`transition ${step === 2 ? "font-bold text-slate-800" : ""}`}>
                  בחר הכל ({step === 0 ? "7" : step === 1 ? "6" : "5"})
                </span>
                <input
                  type="checkbox"
                  readOnly
                  checked={step === 2}
                  className={`rounded border-slate-300 transition ${
                    step === 2 ? "ring-2 ring-brand-400 bg-brand-600" : ""
                  }`}
                />
                {step === 2 && (
                  <div className="absolute -bottom-7 left-2 z-40 pointer-events-none drop-shadow-xl animate-pulse">
                    <div className="relative">
                      <svg className="w-6 h-6 text-slate-950 fill-slate-950 drop-shadow-md -rotate-45" viewBox="0 0 24 24">

                      </svg>
                      <span className="absolute -top-1 -right-1 h-6 w-6 rounded-full bg-brand-500/50 animate-ping pointer-events-none" />
                    </div>
                  </div>
                )}
              </div>
            </div>

            {/* Row 1: 12/10/2026 */}
            <div className={`flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-3 rounded-2xl border p-3 transition duration-300 ${
              step >= 1 ? "border-emerald-300 bg-emerald-50/90 shadow-2xs" : "border-slate-100 bg-white"
            }`}>
              {/* Quick actions (Left in RTL) */}
              {step >= 1 ? (
                <span className="text-xs font-bold text-emerald-800 bg-white/90 border border-emerald-200 rounded-xl px-3 py-1 shadow-2xs">
                  ✅ 9.0 שעות הוזנו בהצלחה!
                </span>
              ) : (
                <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5">
                  <div className="relative inline-flex">
                    <span className={`rounded-xl border px-2.5 py-1 text-xs font-bold transition shadow-xs ${
                      step === 0
                        ? "border-blue-500 bg-blue-600 text-white ring-2 ring-blue-200 scale-105"
                        : "border-blue-200 bg-blue-50/80 text-blue-700"
                    }`}>
                      ⏱️ שעות תקן
                    </span>
                    {step === 0 && (
                      <div className="absolute -bottom-7 left-1/2 -translate-x-1/2 z-40 pointer-events-none drop-shadow-xl animate-pulse">
                        <div className="relative">
                          <svg className="w-6 h-6 text-slate-950 fill-slate-950 drop-shadow-md -rotate-45" viewBox="0 0 24 24">
                            <path d="M4 0l16 12.279-6.951 1.17 4.325 8.817-3.596 1.734-4.35-8.879-5.428 5.43z" />
                          </svg>
                          <span className="absolute -top-1 -right-1 h-6 w-6 rounded-full bg-blue-500/50 animate-ping pointer-events-none" />
                        </div>
                      </div>
                    )}
                  </div>
                  <span className="rounded-xl border border-purple-200 bg-purple-50/80 px-2.5 py-1 text-xs font-semibold text-purple-700">
                    🏖️ חופש
                  </span>
                  <span className="rounded-xl border border-amber-200 bg-amber-50/80 px-2.5 py-1 text-xs font-semibold text-amber-700">
                    🩹 מחלה
                  </span>
                  <span className="rounded-xl border border-slate-200 bg-white p-1 text-slate-400">
                    <PencilIcon className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
                  </span>
                </div>
              )}

              {/* Day info (Right in RTL) */}
              <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
                <div className="text-right">
                  <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                    <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 rounded-full bg-amber-100 px-2 py-0.5 text-[10px] font-bold text-amber-800 border border-amber-200">
                      ⚠️ לא מולא
                    </span>
                    <span className="rounded-md bg-slate-100 px-2 py-0.5 text-[11px] font-medium text-slate-600">
                      שני
                    </span>
                    <span className="font-bold text-slate-900 text-sm">
                      12/10/2026
                    </span>
                  </div>
                  <div className="text-[11px] text-slate-400 mt-0.5">
                    תקן יומי: 9 שעות
                  </div>
                </div>
                <input type="checkbox" readOnly className="rounded border-slate-300" />
              </div>
            </div>

            {/* Row 2: 11/10/2026 */}
            <div className={`flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-3 rounded-2xl border p-3 transition duration-300 ${
              step >= 2 ? "border-purple-300 bg-purple-50/90 shadow-2xs" : "border-slate-100 bg-white"
            }`}>
              {/* Quick actions (Left in RTL) */}
              {step >= 2 ? (
                <span className="text-xs font-bold text-purple-800 bg-white/90 border border-purple-200 rounded-xl px-3 py-1 shadow-2xs">
                  🏖️ עודכן כיום חופש מלא!
                </span>
              ) : (
                <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5">
                  <span className="rounded-xl border border-blue-200 bg-blue-50/80 px-2.5 py-1 text-xs font-semibold text-blue-700">
                    ⏱️ שעות תקן
                  </span>
                  <div className="relative inline-flex">
                    <span className={`rounded-xl border px-2.5 py-1 text-xs font-bold transition shadow-xs ${
                      step === 1
                        ? "border-purple-500 bg-purple-600 text-white ring-2 ring-purple-200 scale-105"
                        : "border-purple-200 bg-purple-50/80 text-purple-700"
                    }`}>
                      🏖️ חופש
                    </span>
                    {step === 1 && (
                      <div className="absolute -bottom-7 left-1/2 -translate-x-1/2 z-40 pointer-events-none drop-shadow-xl animate-pulse">
                        <div className="relative">
                          <svg className="w-6 h-6 text-slate-950 fill-slate-950 drop-shadow-md -rotate-45" viewBox="0 0 24 24">
                            <path d="M4 0l16 12.279-6.951 1.17 4.325 8.817-3.596 1.734-4.35-8.879-5.428 5.43z" />
                          </svg>
                          <span className="absolute -top-1 -right-1 h-6 w-6 rounded-full bg-purple-500/50 animate-ping pointer-events-none" />
                        </div>
                      </div>
                    )}
                  </div>
                  <span className="rounded-xl border border-amber-200 bg-amber-50/80 px-2.5 py-1 text-xs font-semibold text-amber-700">
                    🩹 מחלה
                  </span>
                  <span className="rounded-xl border border-slate-200 bg-white p-1 text-slate-400">
                    <PencilIcon className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
                  </span>
                </div>
              )}

              {/* Day info (Right in RTL) */}
              <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
                <div className="text-right">
                  <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                    <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 rounded-full bg-amber-100 px-2 py-0.5 text-[10px] font-bold text-amber-800 border border-amber-200">
                      ⚠️ לא מולא
                    </span>
                    <span className="rounded-md bg-slate-100 px-2 py-0.5 text-[11px] font-medium text-slate-600">
                      ראשון
                    </span>
                    <span className="font-bold text-slate-900 text-sm">
                      11/10/2026
                    </span>
                  </div>
                  <div className="text-[11px] text-slate-400 mt-0.5">
                    תקן יומי: 9 שעות
                  </div>
                </div>
                <input type="checkbox" readOnly className="rounded border-slate-300" />
              </div>
            </div>

            {/* Row 3: 14/09/2026 */}
            <div className="flex items-center justify-between rounded-2xl border border-slate-100 bg-white p-3 opacity-60">
              <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 text-xs text-slate-400">
                <span>⏱️ תקן</span>
                <span>🏖️ חופש</span>
              </div>
              <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
                <div className="text-right">
                  <span className="font-bold text-slate-900 text-sm">14/09/2026 • שני</span>
                  <div className="text-[11px] text-slate-400">תקן יומי: 9 שעות</div>
                </div>
                <input type="checkbox" readOnly className="rounded border-slate-300" />
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>

          {/* Footer */}
          <div className="border-t border-slate-100 bg-slate-50 p-4 sm:px-6 flex items-center justify-between text-xs">
            <span className="rounded-xl border border-slate-200 bg-white px-4 py-2 font-bold text-slate-700 shadow-2xs">
              סגירה
            </span>
            <span className="font-bold text-slate-500">
              {step === 3 ? "🎉 עודכנו 2 ימים תוך שניות!" : `סה"כ ${step === 0 ? "7" : step === 1 ? "6" : "5"} דיווחים חסרים`}
            </span>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Progress Timeline Tabs */}
      <div className="border-t border-slate-800 bg-slate-950 p-4">
        <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-2 sm:grid-cols-4 text-center text-xs">
          {[
            { num: 1, label: "איתור ימי עבודה חסרים" },
            { num: 2, label: "קליק על 'שעות תקן'" },
            { num: 3, label: "קליק על 'חופש'" },
            { num: 4, label: "סיום עדכון מהיר!" },
          ].map((st, i) => (
            <button
              key={st.num}
              type="button"
              onClick={() => setStep(i)}
              className={`rounded-xl p-2.5 transition text-center min-h-[58px] flex flex-col justify-center ${
                step === i
                  ? "bg-brand-600 text-white font-black shadow-md ring-2 ring-brand-400/50"
                  : "bg-slate-900 text-slate-400 hover:bg-slate-800 hover:text-slate-200"
              }`}
            >
              <div className="text-[10px] opacity-75 font-semibold">שלב {st.num}</div>
              <div className="text-xs font-bold mt-0.5 leading-snug break-words">{st.label}</div>
            </button>
          ))}
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}
```

## `components/guide/ReportsTableDemo.tsx`

```tsx
"use client";

import { useState, useEffect } from "react";
import { PencilIcon, CheckCircleIcon } from "../Icons";

export default function ReportsTableDemo() {
  const [step, setStep] = useState(0);
  const [isPlaying, setIsPlaying] = useState(true);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!isPlaying) return;
    const timer = setInterval(() => {
      setStep((prev) => (prev + 1) % 4);
    }, 2800);
    return () => clearInterval(timer);
  }, [isPlaying]);

  return (
    <div className="overflow-hidden rounded-2xl border border-slate-700/30 bg-slate-950 shadow-2xl">
      {/* Simulation Header */}
      <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between border-b border-slate-800 bg-slate-900/90 px-4 py-3 text-slate-300 gap-2">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
          <span className="h-3 w-3 rounded-full bg-rose-500/80 inline-block" />
          <span className="h-3 w-3 rounded-full bg-amber-500/80 inline-block" />
          <span className="h-3 w-3 rounded-full bg-emerald-500/80 inline-block" />
          <span className="mr-2 text-xs font-bold text-white flex items-center gap-1.5">
            <span className="inline-block h-2 w-2 rounded-full bg-emerald-400 animate-ping" />
            הדמיה מונפשת
          </span>
        </div>
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => setIsPlaying(!isPlaying)}
            className="flex items-center gap-1 rounded-lg bg-slate-800 px-3 py-1 text-xs font-bold text-slate-200 hover:bg-slate-700 transition"
          >
            <span>{isPlaying ? "⏸️ השהה" : "▶️ נגן"}</span>
          </button>
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => setStep(0)}
            className="rounded-lg bg-slate-800 px-2 py-1 text-xs font-bold text-slate-300 hover:bg-slate-700 transition"
            title="הפעל מחדש"
          >
            🔄
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Screen Area */}
      <div className="relative bg-slate-100 p-4 sm:p-6 select-none overflow-hidden min-h-[400px]">
        {/* Table Mockup (1:1 with real ReportsScreen table) */}
        <div className="rounded-2xl bg-white shadow-xl border border-slate-200 overflow-hidden">
          <div className="bg-slate-50 px-4 py-3 border-b border-slate-200 flex items-center justify-between text-xs">
            <span className="font-extrabold text-slate-800">דיווחים חודשיים • אוקטובר 2026</span>
            <span className="text-slate-500 font-medium">31 ימים ברישום</span>
          </div>

          <div className="overflow-x-auto">
            <table className="w-full text-right text-xs">
              <thead>
                <tr className="border-b border-slate-100 bg-slate-50/60 text-slate-500">
                  <th className="px-3 py-2.5 font-medium">תאריך</th>
                  <th className="px-3 py-2.5 text-center font-medium">כניסה - יציאה</th>
                  <th className="px-3 py-2.5 text-center font-medium">סה״כ</th>
                  <th className="px-3 py-2.5 text-center font-medium">סיווג</th>
                  <th className="px-3 py-2.5 text-center font-medium">פעולה</th>
                </tr>
              </thead>
              <tbody className="divide-y divide-slate-100">
                {/* Row 1: Regular day */}
                <tr className="hover:bg-slate-50/80 transition">
                  <td className="px-3 py-2.5 font-bold text-slate-800">
                    <div>01/10/2026</div>
                    <div className="text-[10px] text-slate-400 font-normal">חמישי</div>
                  </td>
                  <td className="px-3 py-2.5 text-center font-medium text-slate-700" dir="ltr">
                    08:00 — 16:30
                  </td>
                  <td className="px-3 py-2.5 text-center font-bold text-slate-800">
                    8.5
                  </td>
                  <td className="px-3 py-2.5 text-center">
                    <span className="inline-block rounded-full bg-emerald-50 text-emerald-700 px-2.5 py-0.5 font-medium text-[11px] border border-emerald-100">
                      עבודה
                    </span>
                  </td>
                  <td className="px-3 py-2.5 text-center">
                    <span className="p-1 text-slate-400">
                      <PencilIcon className="w-3.5 h-3.5 inline-block" />
                    </span>
                  </td>
                </tr>

                {/* Row 2: Target row for edit (04/10/2026 with 2 segments) */}
                <tr
                  className={`transition-all duration-300 ${
                    step === 1
                      ? "bg-brand-50/90 ring-2 ring-brand-300"
                      : step >= 2
                      ? "bg-emerald-50/70"
                      : "hover:bg-slate-50/80"
                  }`}
                >
                  <td className="px-3 py-2.5 font-bold text-slate-800">

                    <div className="text-[10px] text-slate-400 font-normal">ראשון</div>
                  </td>
                  <td className="px-3 py-2.5 text-center">
                    <div className="font-bold text-brand-900" dir="ltr">07:45 — 20:00</div>
                    <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 rounded-full bg-brand-50 px-2 py-0.2 text-[10px] font-bold text-brand-700 border border-brand-200 mt-0.5">
                      2 מקטעים
                    </span>
                  </td>
                  <td className="px-3 py-2.5 text-center relative">
                    <div className="font-extrabold text-slate-900">11.75</div>
                    <div className="text-[10px] font-extrabold text-indigo-600 bg-indigo-50 border border-indigo-100 rounded-full px-1.5 py-0.2 mt-0.5 inline-block">
                      +2.75 ש"נ
                    </div>
                    {/* Step 3 Cursor on updated hours */}
                    {step === 3 && (
                      <div className="absolute -top-7 left-1/2 -translate-x-1/2 z-40 pointer-events-none drop-shadow-xl animate-pulse">
                        <div className="relative">
                          <svg className="w-6 h-6 text-slate-950 fill-slate-950 drop-shadow-md" viewBox="0 0 24 24">
                            <path d="M4 0l16 12.279-6.951 1.17 4.325 8.817-3.596 1.734-4.35-8.879-5.428 5.43z" />
                          </svg>
                          <span className="absolute -top-1 -right-1 h-6 w-6 rounded-full bg-emerald-500/50 animate-ping pointer-events-none" />
                        </div>
                      </div>
                    )}
                  </td>
                  <td className="px-3 py-2.5 text-center">
                    <span className="inline-block rounded-full bg-emerald-50 text-emerald-700 px-2.5 py-0.5 font-medium text-[11px] border border-emerald-100">
                      עבודה
                    </span>
                  </td>
                  <td className="px-3 py-2.5 text-center">
                    <div className="relative inline-flex">
                      <button
                        type="button"
                        className={`rounded-lg p-1.5 transition ${
                          step === 1
                            ? "bg-brand-600 text-white shadow-xs ring-2 ring-brand-300 scale-125"
                            : "text-brand-600 hover:bg-brand-50"
                        }`}
                        title="עריכת דיווח"
                      >
                        <PencilIcon className="w-4 h-4" />
                      </button>

                      {/* Step 1 Virtual Cursor sitting DIRECTLY on the pencil button */}
                      {step === 1 && (
                        <div className="absolute -bottom-7 left-1/2 -translate-x-1/2 z-40 pointer-events-none drop-shadow-xl animate-pulse">
                          <div className="relative">
                            <svg className="w-6 h-6 text-slate-950 fill-slate-950 drop-shadow-md -rotate-45" viewBox="0 0 24 24">
                              <path d="M4 0l16 12.279-6.951 1.17 4.325 8.817-3.596 1.734-4.35-8.879-5.428 5.43z" />
                            </svg>
                            <span className="absolute -top-1 -right-1 h-6 w-6 rounded-full bg-brand-500/50 animate-ping pointer-events-none" />
                          </div>
                        </div>
                      )}
                    </div>
                  </td>
                </tr>

                {/* Row 3: Holiday */}
                <tr className="bg-purple-50/40">
                  <td className="px-3 py-2.5 font-bold text-slate-800">
                    <div>05/10/2026</div>
                    <div className="text-[10px] text-slate-400 font-normal">שני</div>
                  </td>
                  <td className="px-3 py-2.5 text-center text-slate-400">—</td>
                  <td className="px-3 py-2.5 text-center font-bold text-purple-700">0.0</td>
                  <td className="px-3 py-2.5 text-center">
                    <span className="inline-block rounded-full bg-purple-100 text-purple-800 px-2.5 py-0.5 font-bold text-[11px] border border-purple-200">
                      חג סוכות
                    </span>
                  </td>
                  <td className="px-3 py-2.5 text-center">
                    <span className="p-1 text-slate-400">
                      <PencilIcon className="w-3.5 h-3.5 inline-block" />
                    </span>
                  </td>
                </tr>
              </tbody>
            </table>
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Modal Overlay in Step 2 */}
        {step === 2 && (
          <div className="absolute inset-0 z-20 flex items-center justify-center bg-slate-900/50 backdrop-blur-xs p-4 animate-fadeIn">
            <div className="w-full max-w-sm rounded-2xl bg-white p-4 shadow-2xl border border-slate-200">
              <div className="flex items-center justify-between border-b border-slate-100 pb-2 mb-3">
                <h5 className="text-xs font-black text-slate-900">עריכת דיווח: 04/10/2026</h5>
                <span className="text-xs text-slate-400">✕</span>
              </div>
              <div className="space-y-2 text-xs">
                <div className="rounded-lg bg-brand-50 p-2 text-brand-800 font-bold">
                  מקטע 1: 07:45 - 12:00 (4.25 שעות)
                </div>
                <div className="rounded-lg bg-brand-50 p-2 text-brand-800 font-bold">
                  מקטע 2: 12:30 - 20:00 (7.5 שעות)
                </div>
                <div className="relative inline-block w-full">
                  <div className="rounded-lg bg-emerald-600 text-white font-bold p-2 text-center shadow-xs flex items-center justify-center gap-1">
                    <CheckCircleIcon className="w-4 h-4 text-emerald-200" />
                    <span>שמור שינויים ביום זה</span>
                  </div>
                  {/* Step 2 Cursor on save button in modal */}
                  <div className="absolute -bottom-6 left-1/2 -translate-x-1/2 z-40 pointer-events-none drop-shadow-xl animate-pulse">
                    <div className="relative">
                      <svg className="w-6 h-6 text-slate-950 fill-slate-950 drop-shadow-md -rotate-45" viewBox="0 0 24 24">
                        <path d="M4 0l16 12.279-6.951 1.17 4.325 8.817-3.596 1.734-4.35-8.879-5.428 5.43z" />
                      </svg>
                      <span className="absolute -top-1 -right-1 h-6 w-6 rounded-full bg-emerald-400/60 animate-ping pointer-events-none" />
                    </div>
                  </div>
                </div>
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>
        )}

        {/* Toast in Step 3 */}
        {step === 3 && (
          <div className="mt-3 rounded-xl bg-emerald-600 text-white p-2.5 text-center text-xs font-bold shadow-lg animate-fadeIn flex items-center justify-center gap-1.5">
            <span>🎉</span>
            <span>השורה עודכנה בהצלחה: סה״כ 11.75 שעות נרשמו בדיוק של 100%!</span>

      </div>

      {/* Progress Timeline Tabs */}

            { num: 1, label: "תצוגת הטבלה והסיווגים" },
            { num: 2, label: "לחיצה על העיפרון" },
            { num: 3, label: "עריכה ישירה בחלון" },
            { num: 4, label: "שמירה ועדכון שורה" },
          ].map((st, i) => (
            <button
              key={st.num}
              type="button"
              onClick={() => setStep(i)}
              className={`rounded-xl p-2.5 transition text-center min-h-[58px] flex flex-col justify-center ${
                step === i
                  ? "bg-brand-600 text-white font-black shadow-md ring-2 ring-brand-400/50"
                  : "bg-slate-900 text-slate-400 hover:bg-slate-800 hover:text-slate-200"
              }`}
            >
              <div className="text-[10px] opacity-75 font-semibold">שלב {st.num}</div>
              <div className="text-xs font-bold mt-0.5 leading-snug break-words">{st.label}</div>
            </button>
          ))}
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}
```

```tsx
"use client";

import { useState, useEffect } from "react";
import { ShieldIcon, ClockIcon } from "../Icons";

export default function SettingsDemo() {
  const [step, setStep] = useState(0);
  const [isPlaying, setIsPlaying] = useState(true);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!isPlaying) return;
    const timer = setInterval(() => {
      setStep((prev) => (prev + 1) % 4);
    }, 2800);
    return () => clearInterval(timer);
  }, [isPlaying]);

  return (
    <div className="overflow-hidden rounded-2xl border border-slate-700/30 bg-slate-950 shadow-2xl">
      {/* Header */}
      <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between border-b border-slate-800 bg-slate-900/90 px-4 py-3 text-slate-300 gap-2">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
          <span className="h-3 w-3 rounded-full bg-rose-500/80 inline-block" />
          <span className="h-3 w-3 rounded-full bg-amber-500/80 inline-block" />
          <span className="h-3 w-3 rounded-full bg-emerald-500/80 inline-block" />
          <span className="mr-2 text-xs font-bold text-white flex items-center gap-1.5">
            <span className="inline-block h-2 w-2 rounded-full bg-emerald-400 animate-ping" />
            הדמיה מונפשת
          </span>
        </div>
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => setIsPlaying(!isPlaying)}
            className="flex items-center gap-1 rounded-lg bg-slate-800 px-3 py-1 text-xs font-bold text-slate-200 hover:bg-slate-700 transition"
          >
            <span>{isPlaying ? "⏸️ השהה" : "▶️ נגן"}</span>
          </button>
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => setStep(0)}
            className="rounded-lg bg-slate-800 px-2 py-1 text-xs font-bold text-slate-300 hover:bg-slate-700 transition"
            title="הפעל מחדש"
          >
            🔄
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Screen Area */}
      <div className="relative bg-slate-100 p-4 sm:p-6 select-none overflow-hidden min-h-[400px]">
        {/* Settings Simulator Mockup (1:1 with real SettingsScreen) */}
        <div className="max-w-lg mx-auto bg-white rounded-2xl p-5 shadow-xl border border-slate-200 space-y-4">
          <div className="flex items-center justify-between border-b border-slate-100 pb-3">
            <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
              <span className="flex h-8 w-8 items-center justify-center rounded-xl bg-brand-50 text-brand-700 font-bold text-xs">
                💼
              </span>
              <div>
                <h5 className="text-xs font-black text-slate-900">הגדרות תנאי העסקה ושכר</h5>
                <p className="text-[10px] text-slate-500">חישוב אוטומטי של שעות נוספות וערך כספי</p>
              </div>
            </div>
            <span className="rounded-full bg-brand-50 text-brand-700 text-[10px] font-bold px-2 py-0.5 border border-brand-200">
              פעיל
            </span>
          </div>

          {/* Form fields */}
          <div className="space-y-3 text-xs">
            {/* Field 1: Employment Type */}
            <div className={`p-2.5 rounded-xl border transition-all duration-300 ${
              step === 0 ? "bg-brand-50/80 border-brand-300 ring-2 ring-brand-200 shadow-xs" : "bg-slate-50 border-slate-200"
            }`}>
              <span className="block font-bold text-slate-700 mb-1">סוג העסקה:</span>
              <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-2">
                <div className="relative">
                  <span className="block rounded-lg bg-brand-600 text-white font-extrabold text-center py-1.5 shadow-2xs">
                    עובד גלובלי
                  </span>
                  {/* Step 0 Virtual Cursor on global worker option */}
                  {step === 0 && (
                    <div className="absolute -bottom-6 left-1/2 -translate-x-1/2 z-40 pointer-events-none drop-shadow-xl animate-pulse">
                      <div className="relative">
                        <svg className="w-6 h-6 text-slate-950 fill-slate-950 drop-shadow-md -rotate-45" viewBox="0 0 24 24">
                          <path d="M4 0l16 12.279-6.951 1.17 4.325 8.817-3.596 1.734-4.35-8.879-5.428 5.43z" />
                        </svg>
                        <span className="absolute -top-1 -right-1 h-6 w-6 rounded-full bg-brand-500/50 animate-ping pointer-events-none" />
                      </div>
                    </div>
                  )}
                </div>
                <span className="rounded-lg bg-white border border-slate-200 text-slate-600 text-center py-1.5">
                  עובד שעתי
                </span>
              </div>
            </div>

            {/* Field 2: Hourly Rate */}
            <div className={`p-2.5 rounded-xl border transition-all duration-300 ${
              step === 1 ? "bg-blue-50/80 border-blue-300 ring-2 ring-blue-200 shadow-xs" : "bg-slate-50 border-slate-200"
            }`}>
              <span className="block font-bold text-slate-700 mb-1">ערך שעה (לצורך חישוב שעות נוספות):</span>
              <div className="relative">
                <div className="flex items-center justify-between bg-white border border-slate-200 rounded-lg px-3 py-1.5 font-bold">
                  <span className="text-brand-700 text-sm">100.00 ₪</span>
                  <span className="text-[10px] text-slate-400">לפי בסיס שכר</span>
                </div>
                {/* Step 1 Virtual Cursor on rate input */}
                {step === 1 && (
                  <div className="absolute -bottom-6 left-1/2 -translate-x-1/2 z-40 pointer-events-none drop-shadow-xl animate-pulse">
                    <div className="relative">
                      <svg className="w-6 h-6 text-slate-950 fill-slate-950 drop-shadow-md -rotate-45" viewBox="0 0 24 24">
                        <path d="M4 0l16 12.279-6.951 1.17 4.325 8.817-3.596 1.734-4.35-8.879-5.428 5.43z" />
                      </svg>
                      <span className="absolute -top-1 -right-1 h-6 w-6 rounded-full bg-blue-500/50 animate-ping pointer-events-none" />
                    </div>
                  </div>
                )}
              </div>
            </div>

            {/* Field 3: Overtime Breakdown preview */}
            <div className={`p-3 rounded-xl border transition-all duration-300 ${
              step >= 2 ? "bg-indigo-50/90 border-indigo-300 ring-2 ring-indigo-200 shadow-xs" : "bg-slate-50 border-slate-200"
            }`}>
              <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-1.5">
                <span className="font-extrabold text-indigo-900">מדרגות שעות נוספות לפי חוק</span>
                <span className="text-[10px] font-bold bg-indigo-100 text-indigo-800 px-2 py-0.5 rounded">
                  אוטומטי
                </span>
              </div>
              <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-2 text-[11px] relative">
                <div className="bg-white p-2 rounded-lg border border-indigo-100">
                  <div className="text-slate-500">שעתיים ראשונות:</div>
                  <div className="font-black text-indigo-700">125% (125 ₪/שעה)</div>
                </div>
                <div className="bg-white p-2 rounded-lg border border-indigo-100">
                  <div className="text-slate-500">החל מהשעה ה-3:</div>
                  <div className="font-black text-indigo-700">150% (150 ₪/שעה)</div>
                </div>
                {/* Step 2 Virtual Cursor on overtime breakdown */}
                {step === 2 && (
                  <div className="absolute -bottom-6 left-1/2 -translate-x-1/2 z-40 pointer-events-none drop-shadow-xl animate-pulse">
                    <div className="relative">
                      <svg className="w-6 h-6 text-slate-950 fill-slate-950 drop-shadow-md -rotate-45" viewBox="0 0 24 24">
                        <path d="M4 0l16 12.279-6.951 1.17 4.325 8.817-3.596 1.734-4.35-8.879-5.428 5.43z" />
                      </svg>
                      <span className="absolute -top-1 -right-1 h-6 w-6 rounded-full bg-indigo-500/50 animate-ping pointer-events-none" />
                    </div>
                  </div>
                )}
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>

          {/* Dynamic Value Badge on Step 3 */}
          {step === 3 && (
            <div className="relative">
              <div className="rounded-xl bg-gradient-to-r from-indigo-700 to-brand-700 p-2.5 text-center text-white text-xs font-black shadow-lg animate-fadeIn">
                💰 אומדן תוספת שעות נוספות לחודש הנוכחי: 1,625 ₪!
              </div>
              {/* Step 3 Virtual Cursor on total value */}
              <div className="absolute -bottom-6 left-1/2 -translate-x-1/2 z-40 pointer-events-none drop-shadow-xl animate-pulse">
                <div className="relative">
                  <svg className="w-6 h-6 text-slate-950 fill-slate-950 drop-shadow-md -rotate-45" viewBox="0 0 24 24">
                    <path d="M4 0l16 12.279-6.951 1.17 4.325 8.817-3.596 1.734-4.35-8.879-5.428 5.43z" />
                  </svg>
                  <span className="absolute -top-1 -right-1 h-6 w-6 rounded-full bg-amber-400/60 animate-ping pointer-events-none" />
                </div>
              </div>
            </div>
          )}
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Progress Timeline Tabs */}
      <div className="border-t border-slate-800 bg-slate-950 p-4">
        <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-2 sm:grid-cols-4 text-center text-xs">
          {[
            { num: 1, label: "סוג העסקה" },
            { num: 2, label: "הגדרת ערך שעה" },
            { num: 3, label: "מדרגות 125% ו-150%" },
            { num: 4, label: "הערכת שווי כספי" },
          ].map((st, i) => (
            <button
              key={st.num}
              type="button"
              onClick={() => setStep(i)}
              className={`rounded-xl p-2.5 transition text-center min-h-[58px] flex flex-col justify-center ${
                step === i
                  ? "bg-brand-600 text-white font-black shadow-md ring-2 ring-brand-400/50"
                  : "bg-slate-900 text-slate-400 hover:bg-slate-800 hover:text-slate-200"
              }`}
            >
              <div className="text-[10px] opacity-75 font-semibold">שלב {st.num}</div>
              <div className="text-xs font-bold mt-0.5 leading-snug break-words">{st.label}</div>
            </button>
          ))}
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}
```
