## `lib/settingsStore.ts`

```ts
"use client";

import { HolidaySetting, DEFAULT_HOLIDAYS } from "./holidays";
export type { HolidaySetting };
export { DEFAULT_HOLIDAYS };

export const DEFAULT_CLASSIFICATIONS = [
  "עבודה",
  "חופש",
  "מחלה",
  "מילואים",
  "חג",
  "ערב חג",
  "סופ\"ש",
  "עבודה במילואים",
];

export const DEFAULT_ORDER_TYPES = [
  "מילואים רגילים",
  "צו 8",
];

export function getClassifications(): string[] {
  if (typeof window === "undefined") return DEFAULT_CLASSIFICATIONS;
  try {
    const val = localStorage.getItem("work-hours-classifications");
    return val ? JSON.parse(val) : DEFAULT_CLASSIFICATIONS;
  } catch {
    return DEFAULT_CLASSIFICATIONS;
  }
}

export function saveClassifications(list: string[]): void {
  if (typeof window === "undefined") return;
  localStorage.setItem("work-hours-classifications", JSON.stringify(list));
}

export function getOrderTypes(): string[] {
  if (typeof window === "undefined") return DEFAULT_ORDER_TYPES;
  try {
    const val = localStorage.getItem("work-hours-ordertypes");
    return val ? JSON.parse(val) : DEFAULT_ORDER_TYPES;
  } catch {
    return DEFAULT_ORDER_TYPES;
  }
}

export function saveOrderTypes(list: string[]): void {
  if (typeof window === "undefined") return;
  localStorage.setItem("work-hours-ordertypes", JSON.stringify(list));
}

export function getHolidays(): HolidaySetting[] {
  if (typeof window === "undefined") return DEFAULT_HOLIDAYS;
  try {
    const val = localStorage.getItem("work-hours-holidays");
    return val ? JSON.parse(val) : DEFAULT_HOLIDAYS;
  } catch {
    return DEFAULT_HOLIDAYS;
  }
}

export function saveHolidays(list: HolidaySetting[]): void {
  if (typeof window === "undefined") return;
  localStorage.setItem("work-hours-holidays", JSON.stringify(list));
}

export function getHolidayForDate(dateIso: string): HolidaySetting | undefined {
  const holidays = getHolidays();
  return holidays.find((h) => h.date === dateIso);
}

export async function syncHolidaysFromHebcal(
  year: number,
  mode: "work" | "all" = "work"
): Promise<{ count: number; added: number; holidays: HolidaySetting[] }> {
  const res = await fetch(`/api/holidays?year=${year}&mode=${mode}`);
  const json = await res.json();
  if (!res.ok) throw new Error(json.error || "שגיאה בסנכרון חגים מ-Hebcal");

  const fetched: HolidaySetting[] = json.holidays || [];
  const current = getHolidays();
  const dateMap = new Map<string, HolidaySetting>();

  for (const h of current) {
    dateMap.set(h.date, h);
  }

  let added = 0;
  for (const h of fetched) {
    if (!dateMap.has(h.date)) {
      added++;
    }
    dateMap.set(h.date, h);
  }

  const merged = Array.from(dateMap.values()).sort((a, b) => a.date.localeCompare(b.date));
  saveHolidays(merged);

  return {
    count: fetched.length,
    added,
    holidays: merged,
  };
}

export const DEFAULT_HOLIDAY_NAMES = [
  "ערב פסח",
  "פסח א'",
  "שביעי של פסח",
  "ערב שבועות",
  "שבועות",
  "ערב ראש השנה",
  "ראש השנה א'",
  "ראש השנה ב'",
  "ערב יום כיפור",
  "יום כיפור",
  "ערב סוכות",
  "סוכות א'",
  "שמיני עצרת / שמחת תורה",
  "יום העצמאות",
  "יום הזיכרון",
  "פורים",
  "חנוכה",
];

export function getHolidayNames(): string[] {
  if (typeof window === "undefined") return DEFAULT_HOLIDAY_NAMES;
  try {
    const val = localStorage.getItem("work-hours-holidaynames");
    return val ? JSON.parse(val) : DEFAULT_HOLIDAY_NAMES;
  } catch {
    return DEFAULT_HOLIDAY_NAMES;
  }
}

export function saveHolidayNames(list: string[]): void {
  if (typeof window === "undefined") return;
  localStorage.setItem("work-hours-holidaynames", JSON.stringify(list));
}

export const DEFAULT_NON_WORKING_DAYS = [5, 6]; // Friday & Saturday

export function getNonWorkingDays(): number[] {
  if (typeof window === "undefined") return DEFAULT_NON_WORKING_DAYS;
  try {
    const val = localStorage.getItem("work-hours-non-working-days");
    return val ? JSON.parse(val) : DEFAULT_NON_WORKING_DAYS;
  } catch {
    return DEFAULT_NON_WORKING_DAYS;
  }
}

export function saveNonWorkingDays(days: number[]): void {
  if (typeof window === "undefined") return;
  localStorage.setItem("work-hours-non-working-days", JSON.stringify(days));
}

export interface ChartPalette {
  work: string;
  vacation: string;
  reserve: string;
  sick: string;
  holiday: string;
  missing: string;
  weekend: string;
}

export const DEFAULT_CHART_PALETTE: ChartPalette = {
  work: "#2563eb",
  vacation: "#a855f7",
  reserve: "#10b981",
  sick: "#f59e0b",
  holiday: "#06b6d4",
  missing: "#eab308",
  weekend: "#cbd5e1",
};

export function getChartPalette(): ChartPalette {
  if (typeof window === "undefined") return DEFAULT_CHART_PALETTE;
  try {
    const val = localStorage.getItem("work-hours-chart-palette");
    return val ? { ...DEFAULT_CHART_PALETTE, ...JSON.parse(val) } : DEFAULT_CHART_PALETTE;
  } catch {
    return DEFAULT_CHART_PALETTE;
  }
}

export function saveChartPalette(palette: ChartPalette): void {
  if (typeof window === "undefined") return;
  localStorage.setItem("work-hours-chart-palette", JSON.stringify(palette));
  window.dispatchEvent(new Event("storage"));
}
```

## `lib/supabase/server.ts`

```ts
import "server-only";
import { createServerClient } from "@supabase/ssr";
import { cookies } from "next/headers";

export function createSupabaseServerClient() {
  const cookieStore = cookies();
  
  return createServerClient(
    process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_URL || "https://placeholder-url.supabase.co",
    process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_ANON_KEY || "placeholder-anon-key",
    {
      cookies: {
        getAll() {
          return cookieStore.getAll();
        },
        setAll(cookiesToSet) {
          try {
            cookiesToSet.forEach(({ name, value, options }) =>
              cookieStore.set(name, value, options)
            );
          } catch {
            // Safe to ignore when called from a Server Component
          }
        },
      },
    }
  );
}

export function createSupabaseAdminClient() {
  const { createClient } = require("@supabase/supabase-js");
  return createClient(
    process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_URL || "https://placeholder-url.supabase.co",
    process.env.SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY || "placeholder-service-role-key"
  );
}
```

## `lib/types.ts`

```ts
export interface ReportShift {
  entry: string; // כניסה HH:mm
  exit: string; // יציאה HH:mm
  description?: string; // תיאור מקטע (למשל: "נתוני שעון - נוכחות", "עבודה מחוץ לאתר")
}

/** One journal row = one calendar date. Mirrors the 31-column sheet schema (A:AE). */
export interface DayRecord {
  /** 1-based row index in the sheet (header = row 1). */
  rowIndex: number;
  /** Tab (sheet) name this record lives in; "" = default/first tab. */
  tab: string;
  /** ISO date yyyy-mm-dd */
  date: string;
  /** dd/mm/yyyy as stored in the sheet */
  dateDisplay: string;
  entry: string; // כניסה HH:mm
  exit: string; // יציאה HH:mm
  shifts?: ReportShift[]; // מקטעי עבודה מפוצלים באותו יום
  totalHours: string; // סה"כ שעות HH:mm
  totalHoursDecimal: number; // תרגום שעות לעשרוני
  dailyStandard: number; // תקן יומי (עשרוני)
  overtimeDecimal: number; // שעות נוספות עשרוני
  regularHoursDecimal?: number; // שעות רגילות (עד תקן)
  overtime125Decimal?: number; // ש"נ 125% (שעתיים ראשונות)
  overtime150Decimal?: number; // ש"נ 150% (מעבר לשעתיים / סופ"ש)
  vacationDays: number; // ימי חופש
  reserveDays: number; // ימי מילואים
  sickDays: number; // ימי מחלה
  classification: string; // סיווג
  notes: string; // הערות
  uniqueNotes: string; // הערות ייחודיות
  orderType: string; // סוג צו
  dayName: string; // יום
  vacationBalance: number; // יתרת חופשה
  monthlyStandardHours: number; // תקן שעות חודשי
}

export interface MonthSummary {
  year: number;
  month: number; // 1-12
  targetHours: number; // יעד שעות
  reportedHours: number; // דווח בפועל
  remainingHours: number; // יתרה להשלמה
  remainingWorkdays: number; // ימי עבודה פוטנציאליים שנותרו
  forecastHoursPerDay: number; // תחזית שעות/יום
  approxRemainingDays: number; // כ-X ימים (יתרה חלקי תקן יומי ממוצע)
  vacationUsedThisMonth: number;
  sickDaysThisMonth: number;
  reserveDaysThisMonth: number;
  vacationBalance: number; // יתרת חופשה לניצול
  standardWorkDays: number; // תקן ימי עבודה לחודש (מהגדרות)
  estimatedVacationBalanceYearEnd?: number; // הערכה לאחר חופשות עתידיות
  activeEmploymentTerm?: EmploymentTerm; // תנאי העסקה שבתוקף בחודש זה
  regularHoursThisMonth?: number; // סה"כ שעות רגילות
  overtimeHoursThisMonth?: number; // סה"כ שעות נוספות שבוצעו החודש
  ot125HoursThisMonth?: number; // סה"כ שעות נוספות 125%
  ot150HoursThisMonth?: number; // סה"כ שעות נוספות 150%
  overtimeAdditionPay?: number; // תוספת שעות נוספות נטו לתלוש (תוספת 25% ו-50% לעובד חודשי)
  overtimeFullPay?: number; // שווי שעות נוספות מלא (125% ו-150%)
  totalEstimatedPay?: number; // שכר כולל משוער לחודש (שעות רגילות + ש"נ)
  estimatedEarnings?: number; // תאימות לאחור
}

export type EmploymentType = "monthly_overtime" | "global" | "hourly" | "custom";

export interface EmploymentTerm {
  id?: string;
  userId?: string;
  name: string;
  employmentType: EmploymentType;
  startDate: string; // ISO YYYY-MM-DD
  endDate?: string | null; // ISO YYYY-MM-DD or null for current
  jobScopePct: number; // 100 = 100%
  dailyStandardSunWed: number; // e.g. 9.0
  dailyStandardThu: number; // e.g. 8.5
  overtimeEligible: boolean;
  hourlyRate?: number | null; // ערך שעה ב-₪ (לשעתיים או לחודשיים עם שעות נוספות)
  notes?: string;
}

/** Diagnostics about the whole journal, shown after sync. */
export interface JournalInfo {
  tabs: string[];
  totalRecords: number;
  firstDate: string; // ISO
  lastDate: string; // ISO
}

export interface MonthData {
  records: DayRecord[];
  summary: MonthSummary;
  mode: "live" | "mock";
  info: JournalInfo;
}

export type MissingReportType = "full" | "missing_entry" | "missing_exit";

export interface MissingReportItem {
  date: string; // ISO yyyy-mm-dd
  dateDisplay: string; // dd/mm/yyyy
  dayName: string; // יום חמישי...
  year: number;
  month: number;
  dailyStandard: number;
  notes?: string;
  uniqueNotes?: string;
  classification?: string;
  entry?: string;
  exit?: string;
  missingType?: MissingReportType;
}

export interface MissingReportsResponse {
  count: number;
  breakdown?: {
    full: number;
    missingEntry: number;
    missingExit: number;
  };
  missing: MissingReportItem[];
}

/** Reserve-duty aggregation for the drill-down chart. */
export interface ReserveBucket {
  /** Year (years view) or month 1-12 (months view). */
  key: number;
  tzav8: number; // ימי מילואים בצו 8
  regular: number; // ימי מילואים רגילים
}

export interface ReserveSummary {
  years: ReserveBucket[];
  /** key = year, value = 12 monthly buckets (only months with data). */
  months: Record<string, ReserveBucket[]>;
}

/** Payload written back to the sheet (raw fields only — formulas do the rest). */
export interface ReportInput {
  date: string; // ISO yyyy-mm-dd
  entry?: string; // HH:mm
  exit?: string; // HH:mm
  shifts?: ReportShift[]; // מקטעי עבודה מפוצלים באותו יום
  classification: string;
  vacationDays?: number;
  reserveDays?: number;
  sickDays?: number;
  notes?: string;
  uniqueNotes?: string;
  orderType?: string;
}

export interface StandardSettings {
  annualVacationQuota: number; // ימים בשנה
  /** key = year, value = 12 monthly standard work-day counts (Jan..Dec) */
  years: Record<string, number[]>;
}

export const CLASSIFICATIONS = [
  "עבודה",
  "חופש",
  "מחלה",
  "מילואים",
  "חג",
  "ערב חג",
  "סופ\"ש",
  "עבודה במילואים",
] as const;

export const HEBREW_MONTHS = [
  "ינואר",
  "פברואר",

  "אפריל",
  "מאי",
  "יוני",
  "יולי",
  "אוגוסט",
  "ספטמבר",
  "אוקטובר",
  "נובמבר",
  "דצמבר",
] as const;

export const HEBREW_DAYS = [
  "ראשון",
  "שני",
  "שלישי",
  "רביעי",
  "חמישי",
  "שישי",
  "שבת",
] as const;
```

## `scripts/admin_user_deletion.sql`

```
-- ==============================================================================
-- Admin User Deletion & Cascade Data Wipe
-- Allows authorized admin (michael.liarzi@gmail.com) to delete any user and all their data
-- ==============================================================================

-- 1. Ensure profiles SELECT policy allows the admin to view all users
DROP POLICY IF EXISTS "Users can view their own profile" ON public.profiles;
DROP POLICY IF EXISTS "Users can view profiles" ON public.profiles;

CREATE POLICY "Users can view profiles" ON public.profiles
  FOR SELECT USING (
    auth.uid() = id
    OR (auth.jwt() ->> 'email') = 'michael.liarzi@gmail.com'
    OR (auth.jwt() ->> 'role') = 'service_role'
  );

-- 2. Function to delete a user by Email and wipe all their data
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.delete_user_by_email(target_email text)
RETURNS json
LANGUAGE plpgsql
SECURITY DEFINER
SET search_path = public, auth
AS $$
DECLARE
  v_user_id uuid;
  v_caller_email text;
  v_reports_count int := 0;
  v_standards_count int := 0;
BEGIN
  -- Permission check if invoked via API
  IF auth.uid() IS NOT NULL THEN
    SELECT email INTO v_caller_email FROM auth.users WHERE id = auth.uid();
    IF v_caller_email != 'michael.liarzi@gmail.com' AND (auth.jwt() ->> 'role') != 'service_role' THEN
      RETURN json_build_object('success', false, 'error', 'אין הרשאת מנהל למחוק משתמשים');
    END IF;
  END IF;

  -- Find user in auth.users or profiles
  SELECT id INTO v_user_id FROM auth.users WHERE LOWER(email) = LOWER(TRIM(target_email)) LIMIT 1;
  IF v_user_id IS NULL THEN
    SELECT id INTO v_user_id FROM public.profiles WHERE LOWER(email) = LOWER(TRIM(target_email)) LIMIT 1;
  END IF;

  IF v_user_id IS NULL THEN
    RETURN json_build_object('success', false, 'error', 'לא נמצא משתמש עם כתובת האימייל: ' || target_email);
  END IF;

  -- Count affected records
  SELECT COUNT(*) INTO v_reports_count FROM public.reports WHERE user_id = v_user_id;
  SELECT COUNT(*) INTO v_standards_count FROM public.workday_standards WHERE user_id = v_user_id;

  -- Delete from auth.users (Cascades to profiles, reports, workday_standards)
  DELETE FROM auth.users WHERE id = v_user_id;
  DELETE FROM public.profiles WHERE id = v_user_id;

  RETURN json_build_object(
    'success', true,
    'message', 'המשתמש ' || target_email || ' וכל הנתונים שלו נמחקו לצמיתות (' || v_reports_count || ' דיווחים, ' || v_standards_count || ' רשומות תקן).'
  );
END;
$$;

-- 3. Function to delete a user by UUID and wipe all their data
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.delete_user_by_id(target_user_id uuid)
RETURNS json
LANGUAGE plpgsql
SECURITY DEFINER
SET search_path = public, auth
AS $$
DECLARE
  v_user_email text;
  v_caller_email text;
  v_reports_count int := 0;
  v_standards_count int := 0;
BEGIN
  -- Permission check if invoked via API
  IF auth.uid() IS NOT NULL THEN
    SELECT email INTO v_caller_email FROM auth.users WHERE id = auth.uid();
    IF v_caller_email != 'michael.liarzi@gmail.com' AND (auth.jwt() ->> 'role') != 'service_role' THEN
      RETURN json_build_object('success', false, 'error', 'אין הרשאת מנהל למחוק משתמשים');
    END IF;
  END IF;

  SELECT email INTO v_user_email FROM auth.users WHERE id = target_user_id;
  IF v_user_email IS NULL THEN
    SELECT email INTO v_user_email FROM public.profiles WHERE id = target_user_id;
  END IF;

  SELECT COUNT(*) INTO v_reports_count FROM public.reports WHERE user_id = target_user_id;
  SELECT COUNT(*) INTO v_standards_count FROM public.workday_standards WHERE user_id = target_user_id;

  DELETE FROM auth.users WHERE id = target_user_id;
  DELETE FROM public.profiles WHERE id = target_user_id;

  RETURN json_build_object(
    'success', true,
    'message', 'המשתמש ' || COALESCE(v_user_email, target_user_id::text) || ' וכל הנתונים שלו נמחקו לצמיתות (' || v_reports_count || ' דיווחים, ' || v_standards_count || ' רשומות תקן).'
  );
END;
$$;

-- Grant execution permissions
GRANT EXECUTE ON FUNCTION public.delete_user_by_email(text) TO authenticated, service_role, anon;
GRANT EXECUTE ON FUNCTION public.delete_user_by_id(uuid) TO authenticated, service_role, anon;
```

## `scripts/backup-db.js`

```
const { Client } = require('pg');
const fs = require('fs');
const path = require('path');

const BACKUP_DIR = path.join(__dirname, '..', 'backups');
const MAX_BACKUPS = 2; // Always keep 2 daily backups (yesterday and day before yesterday)

const DB_CONFIG = {
  host: process.env.PGHOST || '10.0.0.16',
  port: parseInt(process.env.PGPORT || '54320', 10),
  user: process.env.PGUSER || 'postgres.your-tenant-id',
  password: process.env.PGPASSWORD || 'a6UAzV7BJSdNjPiLUFZ3NfDoS5kj12',
  database: process.env.PGDATABASE || 'postgres',
};

const TABLES = ['profiles', 'workday_standards', 'reports', 'employment_terms'];

function escapeSqlValue(val) {
  if (val === null || val === undefined) {
    return 'NULL';
  }
  if (typeof val === 'number') {
    return val.toString();
  }
  if (typeof val === 'boolean') {
    return val ? 'TRUE' : 'FALSE';
  }
  if (val instanceof Date) {
    const y = val.getFullYear();
    const m = String(val.getMonth() + 1).padStart(2, '0');
    const d = String(val.getDate()).padStart(2, '0');
    if (val.getHours() === 0 && val.getMinutes() === 0 && val.getSeconds() === 0) {
      return `'${y}-${m}-${d}'`;
    }
    return `'${val.toISOString()}'`;
  }
  if (typeof val === 'object') {
    return `'${JSON.stringify(val).replace(/'/g, "''")}'`;
  }
  const str = String(val).replace(/'/g, "''");
  return `'${str}'`;
}

async function runBackup() {
  if (!fs.existsSync(BACKUP_DIR)) {
    fs.mkdirSync(BACKUP_DIR, { recursive: true });
  }

  const now = new Date();
  const dateStr = now.toISOString().slice(0, 10); // YYYY-MM-DD
  const timeStr = now.toTimeString().slice(0, 8).replace(/:/g, ''); // HHMMSS
  const backupFileName = `backup-${dateStr}-${timeStr}.sql`;
  const backupFilePath = path.join(BACKUP_DIR, backupFileName);

  console.log(`[Backup] Connecting to PostgreSQL at ${DB_CONFIG.host}:${DB_CONFIG.port}...`);
  const client = new Client(DB_CONFIG);
  await client.connect();

  console.log(`[Backup] Generating backup file: ${backupFileName}`);
  const lines = [
    `-- ============================================================`,
    `-- Employee Work Hours Dashboard Database Backup`,
    `-- Date: ${now.toISOString()}`,
    `-- Server: ${DB_CONFIG.host}:${DB_CONFIG.port}/${DB_CONFIG.database}`,
    `-- ============================================================`,
    `BEGIN;`,
    ``
  ];

  let totalRecordsDumped = 0;

  for (const table of TABLES) {
    console.log(`[Backup] Dumping table: ${table}...`);
    const colsRes = await client.query(`
      SELECT column_name 
      FROM information_schema.columns 
      WHERE table_schema = 'public' AND table_name = $1
      ORDER BY ordinal_position;
    `, [table]);

    if (colsRes.rows.length === 0) {
      console.warn(`[Backup] Warning: Table ${table} not found in public schema.`);
      continue;
    }

    const colNames = colsRes.rows.map(r => r.column_name);
    const dataRes = await client.query(`SELECT * FROM public.${table};`);
    console.log(`[Backup] Found ${dataRes.rows.length} rows in ${table}.`);
    totalRecordsDumped += dataRes.rows.length;

    lines.push(`-- Table: public.${table} (${dataRes.rows.length} rows)`);

    if (table === 'profiles') {
      for (const row of dataRes.rows) {
        const values = colNames.map(col => escapeSqlValue(row[col])).join(', ');
        const colsEscaped = colNames.map(c => `"${c}"`).join(', ');
        lines.push(`INSERT INTO public."${table}" (${colsEscaped}) VALUES (${values}) ON CONFLICT (id) DO UPDATE SET ${
          colNames.filter(c => c !== 'id').map(c => `"${c}" = EXCLUDED."${c}"`).join(', ')
        };`);
      }
    } else {
      lines.push(`DELETE FROM public."${table}";`);
      for (const row of dataRes.rows) {
        const values = colNames.map(col => escapeSqlValue(row[col])).join(', ');
        const colsEscaped = colNames.map(c => `"${c}"`).join(', ');
        lines.push(`INSERT INTO public."${table}" (${colsEscaped}) VALUES (${values});`);
      }
    }
    lines.push(``);
  }

  lines.push(`COMMIT;`);
  lines.push(`-- Backup completed successfully: ${totalRecordsDumped} total rows.`);

  fs.writeFileSync(backupFilePath, lines.join('\n'), 'utf8');
  console.log(`[Backup] Backup successfully written to ${backupFilePath} (${(fs.statSync(backupFilePath).size / 1024).toFixed(1)} KB)`);

  await client.end();

  // Retention management: keep only the latest MAX_BACKUPS files
  cleanOldBackups();
}

function cleanOldBackups() {
  const files = fs.readdirSync(BACKUP_DIR)
    .filter(f => f.startsWith('backup-') && f.endsWith('.sql'))
    .map(f => ({
      name: f,
      path: path.join(BACKUP_DIR, f),
      time: fs.statSync(path.join(BACKUP_DIR, f)).mtimeMs
    }))
    .sort((a, b) => b.time - a.time); // newest first

  console.log(`[Backup Retention] Total existing backups found: ${files.length}. Policy: keep latest ${MAX_BACKUPS}.`);

  if (files.length > MAX_BACKUPS) {
    const toDelete = files.slice(MAX_BACKUPS);
    for (const file of toDelete) {
      console.log(`[Backup Retention] Deleting older backup: ${file.name}`);
      try {
        fs.unlinkSync(file.path);
      } catch (e) {
        console.error(`[Backup Retention] Failed to delete ${file.name}:`, e);
      }
    }
  }

  const remaining = fs.readdirSync(BACKUP_DIR).filter(f => f.startsWith('backup-') && f.endsWith('.sql'));
  console.log(`[Backup Retention] Active backups kept:`, remaining);
}

runBackup().catch(err => {
  console.error('[Backup Error]', err);
  process.exit(1);
});
```

## `scripts/fix_new_user_registration_rls.sql`

```
-- ==============================================================================
-- Fix: Allow New User Registration Trigger to Insert Profiles without RLS Failure
-- ==============================================================================

-- 1. Ensure profiles INSERT policy allows trigger & user execution
DROP POLICY IF EXISTS "profiles_insert_own" ON public.profiles;
CREATE POLICY "profiles_insert_own" ON public.profiles
  FOR INSERT WITH CHECK (
    auth.uid() = id 
    OR auth.uid() IS NULL 
    OR (auth.jwt() ->> 'role') = 'service_role'
  );

-- 2. Automatic Trigger Function to create default profile for newly registered users
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.handle_new_user()
RETURNS trigger AS $$
BEGIN
  INSERT INTO public.profiles (id, email, annual_vacation_quota)
  VALUES (NEW.id, NEW.email, 22.0)
  ON CONFLICT (id) DO UPDATE
  SET email = EXCLUDED.email;

  RETURN NEW;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql SECURITY DEFINER SET search_path = public;

-- 3. Recreate trigger on auth.users
DROP TRIGGER IF EXISTS on_auth_user_created ON auth.users;
CREATE TRIGGER on_auth_user_created
  AFTER INSERT ON auth.users
  FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION public.handle_new_user();
```

## `scripts/generate-docs.mjs`

```js
#!/usr/bin/env node
/**
 * Generates docs/CODE.md — a single document containing every source file.
 * Runs automatically before every build/dev via the npm "predev"/"prebuild"
 * hooks, so the code document is always in sync with the code itself.
 */
import { promises as fs } from "fs";
import path from "path";
import { fileURLToPath } from "url";

const root = path.resolve(path.dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url)), "..");

const INCLUDE_DIRS = ["app", "components", "lib", "scripts"];
const INCLUDE_FILES = [
  "package.json",
  "tsconfig.json",
  "next.config.mjs",
  "tailwind.config.ts",
  "postcss.config.mjs",
  ".env.local.example",
  "start.bat",
  "start.sh",
  "CLAUDE.md",
];
const EXT_LANG = {
  ".ts": "ts",
  ".tsx": "tsx",
  ".mjs": "js",
  ".json": "json",
  ".css": "css",
  ".bat": "bat",
  ".sh": "bash",
  ".md": "markdown",
  ".example": "bash",
};

async function walk(dir) {
  const out = [];
  for (const e of await fs.readdir(dir, { withFileTypes: true })) {
    const p = path.join(dir, e.name);
    if (e.isDirectory()) out.push(...(await walk(p)));
    else out.push(p);
  }
  return out;
}

const files = [];
for (const d of INCLUDE_DIRS) {
  try {
    files.push(...(await walk(path.join(root, d))));
  } catch {
    /* dir may not exist */
  }
}
for (const f of INCLUDE_FILES) {
  try {
    await fs.access(path.join(root, f));
    files.push(path.join(root, f));
  } catch {
    /* optional */
  }
}
files.sort();

let md = `# 24H Work Dashboard — Full Source Code

> AUTO-GENERATED by \`scripts/generate-docs.mjs\` — do not edit by hand.
> Regenerated on every \`npm run dev\` / \`npm run build\` (and via \`npm run docs\`).
> Generated: ${new Date().toISOString()} · Files: ${files.length}

`;

for (const f of files) {
  const rel = path.relative(root, f).replace(/\\/g, "/");
  const ext = path.extname(f);
  const lang = EXT_LANG[ext] ?? "";
  const body = await fs.readFile(f, "utf8");
  md += `\n---\n\n## \`${rel}\`\n\n\`\`\`${lang}\n${body.trimEnd()}\n\`\`\`\n`;
}

await fs.mkdir(path.join(root, "docs"), { recursive: true });
await fs.writeFile(path.join(root, "docs", "CODE.md"), md, "utf8");
console.log(`docs/CODE.md generated (${files.length} files, ${(md.length / 1024).toFixed(0)} KB)`);
```

## `scripts/migration_employment_terms_and_rls.sql`

```
-- ==============================================================================
-- 24H Work Dashboard: Migration for Employment Terms with History & Strict RLS
-- ==============================================================================

-- 1. Create employment_terms table if not exists
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS public.employment_terms (
  id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  user_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES auth.users(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  name VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'תנאי העסקה רגילים',
  employment_type VARCHAR(30) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'monthly_overtime', -- 'monthly_overtime' | 'global' | 'hourly' | 'custom'
  start_date DATE NOT NULL,
  end_date DATE, -- NULL represents ongoing/current term
  job_scope_pct NUMERIC(5,2) NOT NULL DEFAULT 100.00,
  daily_standard_sun_wed NUMERIC(4,2) NOT NULL DEFAULT 9.00,
  daily_standard_thu NUMERIC(4,2) NOT NULL DEFAULT 8.50,
  overtime_eligible BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT true,
  hourly_rate NUMERIC(10,2),
  notes TEXT,
  created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW(),
  updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW()
);

-- Indexes for optimal lookup performance by user and date range
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_employment_terms_user_dates 
  ON public.employment_terms(user_id, start_date, end_date);

-- ==============================================================================
-- 2. Enable Row Level Security (RLS) on all public tables
-- ==============================================================================
ALTER TABLE public.profiles ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;
ALTER TABLE public.workday_standards ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;
ALTER TABLE public.reports ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;
ALTER TABLE public.employment_terms ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;

-- ==============================================================================
-- 3. Strict RLS Policies for user data isolation (auth.uid() = user_id)
-- ==============================================================================

-- --- PROFILES ---
DROP POLICY IF EXISTS "profiles_select_own" ON public.profiles;
CREATE POLICY "profiles_select_own" ON public.profiles
  FOR SELECT USING (auth.uid() = id);

DROP POLICY IF EXISTS "profiles_insert_own" ON public.profiles;
CREATE POLICY "profiles_insert_own" ON public.profiles
  FOR INSERT WITH CHECK (auth.uid() = id);

DROP POLICY IF EXISTS "profiles_update_own" ON public.profiles;
CREATE POLICY "profiles_update_own" ON public.profiles
  FOR UPDATE USING (auth.uid() = id) WITH CHECK (auth.uid() = id);

-- --- WORKDAY STANDARDS ---
DROP POLICY IF EXISTS "standards_select_own" ON public.workday_standards;
CREATE POLICY "standards_select_own" ON public.workday_standards
  FOR SELECT USING (auth.uid() = user_id);

DROP POLICY IF EXISTS "standards_insert_own" ON public.workday_standards;
CREATE POLICY "standards_insert_own" ON public.workday_standards
  FOR INSERT WITH CHECK (auth.uid() = user_id);

DROP POLICY IF EXISTS "standards_update_own" ON public.workday_standards;
CREATE POLICY "standards_update_own" ON public.workday_standards
  FOR UPDATE USING (auth.uid() = user_id) WITH CHECK (auth.uid() = user_id);

DROP POLICY IF EXISTS "standards_delete_own" ON public.workday_standards;
CREATE POLICY "standards_delete_own" ON public.workday_standards
  FOR DELETE USING (auth.uid() = user_id);

-- --- REPORTS ---
DROP POLICY IF EXISTS "reports_select_own" ON public.reports;
CREATE POLICY "reports_select_own" ON public.reports
  FOR SELECT USING (auth.uid() = user_id);

DROP POLICY IF EXISTS "reports_insert_own" ON public.reports;
CREATE POLICY "reports_insert_own" ON public.reports
  FOR INSERT WITH CHECK (auth.uid() = user_id);

DROP POLICY IF EXISTS "reports_update_own" ON public.reports;
CREATE POLICY "reports_update_own" ON public.reports
  FOR UPDATE USING (auth.uid() = user_id) WITH CHECK (auth.uid() = user_id);

DROP POLICY IF EXISTS "reports_delete_own" ON public.reports;
CREATE POLICY "reports_delete_own" ON public.reports
  FOR DELETE USING (auth.uid() = user_id);

-- --- EMPLOYMENT TERMS ---
DROP POLICY IF EXISTS "terms_select_own" ON public.employment_terms;
CREATE POLICY "terms_select_own" ON public.employment_terms
  FOR SELECT USING (auth.uid() = user_id);

DROP POLICY IF EXISTS "terms_insert_own" ON public.employment_terms;
CREATE POLICY "terms_insert_own" ON public.employment_terms
  FOR INSERT WITH CHECK (auth.uid() = user_id);

DROP POLICY IF EXISTS "terms_update_own" ON public.employment_terms;
CREATE POLICY "terms_update_own" ON public.employment_terms
  FOR UPDATE USING (auth.uid() = user_id) WITH CHECK (auth.uid() = user_id);

DROP POLICY IF EXISTS "terms_delete_own" ON public.employment_terms;
CREATE POLICY "terms_delete_own" ON public.employment_terms
  FOR DELETE USING (auth.uid() = user_id);

-- ==============================================================================
-- 4. Automatically populate initial default term for existing users (if none exist)
-- ==============================================================================
INSERT INTO public.employment_terms (
  user_id,
  name,
  employment_type,
  start_date,
  end_date,
  job_scope_pct,
  daily_standard_sun_wed,
  daily_standard_thu,
  overtime_eligible,
  notes
)
SELECT 
  p.id,
  'חודשי עם שעות נוספות (ברירת מחדל)',
  'monthly_overtime',
  '2019-01-01'::DATE,
  NULL,
  100.00,
  9.00,
  8.50,
  true,
  'נוצר אוטומטית כבסיס היסטורי'
FROM public.profiles p
WHERE NOT EXISTS (
  SELECT 1 FROM public.employment_terms t WHERE t.user_id = p.id
);
```

## `scripts/nas-backup-manager.js`

```
const { Client } = require('pg');

const DB_CONFIG = {
  host: process.env.PGHOST || '10.0.0.16',
  port: parseInt(process.env.PGPORT || '54320', 10),
  user: process.env.PGUSER || 'postgres.your-tenant-id',
  password: process.env.PGPASSWORD || 'a6UAzV7BJSdNjPiLUFZ3NfDoS5kj12',
  database: process.env.PGDATABASE || 'postgres',
};

const SQL_MIGRATION = `
-- 1. System Config Table for retention days
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS public.system_config (
    key TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
    value JSONB NOT NULL,
    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT now()
);

INSERT INTO public.system_config (key, value)
VALUES ('backup_retention_days', '30'::jsonb)
ON CONFLICT (key) DO NOTHING;

GRANT ALL ON TABLE public.system_config TO postgres, authenticated, service_role, anon;

-- Update generate_system_backup to read retention days from system_config
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.generate_system_backup(p_backup_type TEXT DEFAULT 'auto_nas')
RETURNS UUID AS $$
DECLARE
  v_backup_id UUID;
  v_now TIMESTAMPTZ := now();
  v_date_str TEXT := to_char(v_now AT TIME ZONE 'Asia/Jerusalem', 'YYYY-MM-DD');
  v_time_str TEXT := to_char(v_now AT TIME ZONE 'Asia/Jerusalem', 'HH24MISS');
  v_filename TEXT;
  v_reports_count INTEGER := 0;
  v_profiles_count INTEGER := 0;
  v_standards_count INTEGER := 0;
  v_terms_count INTEGER := 0;
  v_backup_data JSONB;
  v_backup_sql TEXT;
  v_size_kb NUMERIC(10, 2);
  v_retention_limit INTEGER := 30;
  v_config_val JSONB;
BEGIN
  -- Read retention setting from system_config
  SELECT value INTO v_config_val FROM public.system_config WHERE key = 'backup_retention_days';
  IF v_config_val IS NOT NULL THEN
    v_retention_limit := coalesce(
      (v_config_val->>0)::integer,
      (v_config_val #>> '{}')::integer,
      30
    );
  END IF;
  IF v_retention_limit IS NULL OR v_retention_limit < 1 THEN
    v_retention_limit := 30;
  END IF;

  v_filename := 'backup-nas-' || v_date_str || '-' || v_time_str || '.sql';

  SELECT count(*) INTO v_profiles_count FROM public.profiles;
  SELECT count(*) INTO v_standards_count FROM public.workday_standards;
  SELECT count(*) INTO v_reports_count FROM public.reports;
  SELECT count(*) INTO v_terms_count FROM public.employment_terms;

  v_backup_data := jsonb_build_object(
    'version', '1.0',
    'created_at', v_now,
    'backup_type', p_backup_type,
    'profiles', coalesce((SELECT jsonb_agg(to_jsonb(p)) FROM public.profiles p), '[]'::jsonb),
    'workday_standards', coalesce((SELECT jsonb_agg(to_jsonb(w)) FROM public.workday_standards w), '[]'::jsonb),
    'reports', coalesce((SELECT jsonb_agg(to_jsonb(r)) FROM public.reports r), '[]'::jsonb),
    'employment_terms', coalesce((SELECT jsonb_agg(to_jsonb(e)) FROM public.employment_terms e), '[]'::jsonb)
  );

  v_backup_sql := '-- ============================================================' || E'\n' ||
    '-- Employee Work Hours Dashboard - NAS System Backup' || E'\n' ||
    '-- Created: ' || to_char(v_now, 'YYYY-MM-DD HH24:MI:SS UTC') || E'\n' ||
    '-- Type: ' || p_backup_type || E'\n' ||
    '-- Reports: ' || v_reports_count || ', Profiles: ' || v_profiles_count || E'\n' ||
    '-- ============================================================' || E'\n' ||
    'BEGIN;' || E'\n\n' ||
    'SELECT public.restore_system_backup_data(' || quote_literal(v_backup_data::text) || '::jsonb);' || E'\n\n' ||
    'COMMIT;' || E'\n';

  v_size_kb := round((octet_length(v_backup_data::text)::numeric / 1024.0), 2);

  INSERT INTO public.system_backups (
    filename, backup_type, reports_count, profiles_count,
    standards_count, terms_count, backup_data, backup_sql, file_size_kb, created_at
  ) VALUES (
    v_filename, p_backup_type, v_reports_count, v_profiles_count,
    v_standards_count, v_terms_count, v_backup_data, v_backup_sql, v_size_kb, v_now
  ) RETURNING id INTO v_backup_id;

  -- Enforce retention: keep newest v_retention_limit backups on NAS
  DELETE FROM public.system_backups
  WHERE id NOT IN (
    SELECT id FROM public.system_backups ORDER BY created_at DESC LIMIT v_retention_limit
  );

  RETURN v_backup_id;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql SECURITY DEFINER;

-- 2. Upgrade user_backups table structure
ALTER TABLE public.user_backups ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS terms_count INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
ALTER TABLE public.user_backups ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS backup_data JSONB;
ALTER TABLE public.user_backups ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS backup_type TEXT DEFAULT 'manual_user';
ALTER TABLE public.user_backups ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS file_size_kb NUMERIC(10, 2) DEFAULT 0;

CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_user_backups_user_created ON public.user_backups(user_id, created_at DESC);

-- Enable RLS on user_backups
ALTER TABLE public.user_backups ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;

DROP POLICY IF EXISTS "Users can read own backups" ON public.user_backups;
CREATE POLICY "Users can read own backups" ON public.user_backups
  FOR SELECT TO authenticated USING (auth.uid() = user_id OR auth.jwt() ->> 'email' = 'michael.liarzi@gmail.com');

DROP POLICY IF EXISTS "Users can insert own backups" ON public.user_backups;
CREATE POLICY "Users can insert own backups" ON public.user_backups
  FOR INSERT TO authenticated WITH CHECK (auth.uid() = user_id OR auth.jwt() ->> 'email' = 'michael.liarzi@gmail.com');

DROP POLICY IF EXISTS "Users can delete own backups" ON public.user_backups;
CREATE POLICY "Users can delete own backups" ON public.user_backups
  FOR DELETE TO authenticated USING (auth.uid() = user_id OR auth.jwt() ->> 'email' = 'michael.liarzi@gmail.com');

GRANT ALL ON TABLE public.user_backups TO postgres, authenticated, service_role, anon;

-- 3. Function to generate personal user backup
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.generate_user_backup(p_user_id UUID, p_backup_type TEXT DEFAULT 'manual_user')
RETURNS UUID AS $$
DECLARE
  v_backup_id UUID;
  v_now TIMESTAMPTZ := now();
  v_date_str TEXT := to_char(v_now AT TIME ZONE 'Asia/Jerusalem', 'YYYY-MM-DD');
  v_time_str TEXT := to_char(v_now AT TIME ZONE 'Asia/Jerusalem', 'HH24MISS');
  v_email TEXT;
  v_safe_email TEXT;
  v_filename TEXT;
  v_reports_count INTEGER := 0;
  v_standards_count INTEGER := 0;
  v_terms_count INTEGER := 0;
  v_backup_data JSONB;
  v_backup_sql TEXT;
  v_size_kb NUMERIC(10, 2);
  v_profile RECORD;
BEGIN
  SELECT * INTO v_profile FROM public.profiles WHERE id = p_user_id;
  v_email := coalesce(v_profile.email, p_user_id::text);
  v_safe_email := regexp_replace(v_email, '[^a-zA-Z0-9_-]', '_', 'g');
  v_filename := 'backup-user-' || v_safe_email || '-' || v_date_str || '-' || v_time_str || '.sql';

  SELECT count(*) INTO v_standards_count FROM public.workday_standards WHERE user_id = p_user_id;
  SELECT count(*) INTO v_reports_count FROM public.reports WHERE user_id = p_user_id;
  SELECT count(*) INTO v_terms_count FROM public.employment_terms WHERE user_id = p_user_id;

  v_backup_data := jsonb_build_object(
    'version', '1.0',
    'created_at', v_now,
    'backup_type', p_backup_type,
    'user_id', p_user_id,
    'email', v_email,
    'profile', to_jsonb(v_profile),
    'workday_standards', coalesce((SELECT jsonb_agg(to_jsonb(w)) FROM public.workday_standards w WHERE w.user_id = p_user_id), '[]'::jsonb),
    'reports', coalesce((SELECT jsonb_agg(to_jsonb(r)) FROM public.reports r WHERE r.user_id = p_user_id), '[]'::jsonb),
    'employment_terms', coalesce((SELECT jsonb_agg(to_jsonb(e)) FROM public.employment_terms e WHERE e.user_id = p_user_id), '[]'::jsonb)
  );

  v_backup_sql := '-- ============================================================' || E'\n' ||
    '-- Employee Work Hours Dashboard - Personal User Backup' || E'\n' ||
    '-- User: ' || v_email || ' (ID: ' || p_user_id || ')' || E'\n' ||
    '-- Created: ' || to_char(v_now, 'YYYY-MM-DD HH24:MI:SS UTC') || E'\n' ||
    '-- Type: ' || p_backup_type || E'\n' ||
    '-- Reports: ' || v_reports_count || ', Standards: ' || v_standards_count || ', Terms: ' || v_terms_count || E'\n' ||
    '-- ============================================================' || E'\n' ||
    'BEGIN;' || E'\n\n' ||
    'SELECT public.restore_user_backup_data(' || quote_literal(v_backup_data::text) || '::jsonb);' || E'\n\n' ||
    'COMMIT;' || E'\n';

  v_size_kb := round((octet_length(v_backup_data::text)::numeric / 1024.0), 2);

  INSERT INTO public.user_backups (
    user_id, email, filename, reports_count, standards_count,
    terms_count, backup_type, backup_data, backup_sql, file_size_kb, created_at
  ) VALUES (
    p_user_id, v_email, v_filename, v_reports_count, v_standards_count,
    v_terms_count, p_backup_type, v_backup_data, v_backup_sql, v_size_kb, v_now
  ) RETURNING id INTO v_backup_id;

  -- Retention for user backups: keep latest 20 personal backups per user
  DELETE FROM public.user_backups
  WHERE user_id = p_user_id AND id NOT IN (
    SELECT id FROM public.user_backups WHERE user_id = p_user_id ORDER BY created_at DESC LIMIT 20
  );

  RETURN v_backup_id;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql SECURITY DEFINER;

-- 4. Function to restore user backup from JSONB data directly
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.restore_user_backup_data(p_data JSONB)
RETURNS JSONB AS $$
DECLARE
  v_user_id UUID;
  v_rep_count INTEGER := 0;
  v_std_count INTEGER := 0;
  v_term_count INTEGER := 0;
  v_item JSONB;
BEGIN
  v_user_id := (p_data->>'user_id')::uuid;
  IF v_user_id IS NULL THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'מזהה משתמש חסר בנתוני הגיבוי';
  END IF;

  -- 1. Restore Profile if present
  IF p_data ? 'profile' AND p_data->'profile' IS NOT NULL AND p_data->'profile' != 'null'::jsonb THEN
    INSERT INTO public.profiles (
      id, email, annual_vacation_quota, created_at, updated_at
    ) VALUES (
      v_user_id,
      p_data->'profile'->>'email',
      (p_data->'profile'->>'annual_vacation_quota')::numeric,
      coalesce((p_data->'profile'->>'created_at')::timestamptz, now()),
      now()
    )
    ON CONFLICT (id) DO UPDATE SET
      annual_vacation_quota = coalesce(EXCLUDED.annual_vacation_quota, public.profiles.annual_vacation_quota),
      updated_at = now();
  END IF;

  -- 2. Restore Workday Standards for THIS user only
  IF p_data ? 'workday_standards' THEN
    DELETE FROM public.workday_standards WHERE user_id = v_user_id;
    FOR v_item IN SELECT jsonb_array_elements(p_data->'workday_standards')
    LOOP
      INSERT INTO public.workday_standards (
        id, user_id, year, m1, m2, m3, m4, m5, m6, m7, m8, m9, m10, m11, m12, created_at, updated_at
      ) VALUES (
        coalesce((v_item->>'id')::uuid, gen_random_uuid()),
        v_user_id,
        (v_item->>'year')::integer,
        (v_item->>'m1')::integer,
        (v_item->>'m2')::integer,
        (v_item->>'m3')::integer,
        (v_item->>'m4')::integer,
        (v_item->>'m5')::integer,
        (v_item->>'m6')::integer,
        (v_item->>'m7')::integer,
        (v_item->>'m8')::integer,
        (v_item->>'m9')::integer,
        (v_item->>'m10')::integer,
        (v_item->>'m11')::integer,
        (v_item->>'m12')::integer,
        coalesce((v_item->>'created_at')::timestamptz, now()),
        now()
      );
      v_std_count := v_std_count + 1;
    END LOOP;
  END IF;

  -- 3. Restore Reports for THIS user only
  IF p_data ? 'reports' THEN
    DELETE FROM public.reports WHERE user_id = v_user_id;
    FOR v_item IN SELECT jsonb_array_elements(p_data->'reports')
    LOOP
      INSERT INTO public.reports (
        id, user_id, date, entry, exit, vacation_days, sick_days, reserve_days,
        classification, notes, order_type, unique_notes, shifts, created_at, updated_at
      ) VALUES (
        coalesce((v_item->>'id')::uuid, gen_random_uuid()),
        v_user_id,
        (v_item->>'date')::date,
        v_item->>'entry',
        v_item->>'exit',
        (v_item->>'vacation_days')::numeric,
        (v_item->>'sick_days')::numeric,
        (v_item->>'reserve_days')::numeric,
        v_item->>'classification',
        v_item->>'notes',
        v_item->>'order_type',
        v_item->>'unique_notes',
        coalesce(v_item->'shifts', '[]'::jsonb),
        coalesce((v_item->>'created_at')::timestamptz, now()),
        now()
      );
      v_rep_count := v_rep_count + 1;
    END LOOP;
  END IF;

  -- 4. Restore Employment Terms for THIS user only
  IF p_data ? 'employment_terms' THEN
    DELETE FROM public.employment_terms WHERE user_id = v_user_id;
    FOR v_item IN SELECT jsonb_array_elements(p_data->'employment_terms')
    LOOP
      INSERT INTO public.employment_terms (
        id, user_id, name, employment_type, start_date, end_date,
        job_scope_pct, daily_standard_sun_wed, daily_standard_thu,
        overtime_eligible, hourly_rate, notes, created_at, updated_at
      ) VALUES (
        coalesce((v_item->>'id')::uuid, gen_random_uuid()),
        v_user_id,
        v_item->>'name',
        v_item->>'employment_type',
        (v_item->>'start_date')::date,
        (v_item->>'end_date')::date,
        (v_item->>'job_scope_pct')::numeric,
        (v_item->>'daily_standard_sun_wed')::numeric,
        (v_item->>'daily_standard_thu')::numeric,
        (v_item->>'overtime_eligible')::boolean,
        (v_item->>'hourly_rate')::numeric,
        v_item->>'notes',
        coalesce((v_item->>'created_at')::timestamptz, now()),
        now()
      );
      v_term_count := v_term_count + 1;
    END LOOP;
  END IF;

  RETURN jsonb_build_object(
    'success', true,
    'user_id', v_user_id,
    'workday_standards_restored', v_std_count,
    'reports_restored', v_rep_count,
    'employment_terms_restored', v_term_count
  );
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql SECURITY DEFINER;

-- 5. Function to restore user backup by ID
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.restore_user_backup_by_id(p_backup_id UUID, p_user_id UUID)
RETURNS JSONB AS $$
DECLARE
  v_backup_rec RECORD;
  v_safety_id UUID;
  v_result JSONB;
BEGIN
  SELECT * INTO v_backup_rec FROM public.user_backups WHERE id = p_backup_id;
  IF NOT FOUND THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'גיבוי בעל מזהה % לא נמצא', p_backup_id;
  END IF;

  -- Ensure user owns this backup or caller is superuser/admin
  IF v_backup_rec.user_id != p_user_id THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'אין הרשאה לשחזר גיבוי של משתמש אחר';
  END IF;

  -- 1. Create automatic personal pre-restore safety backup!
  v_safety_id := public.generate_user_backup(p_user_id, 'pre_restore_safety');

  -- 2. Restore user data
  v_result := public.restore_user_backup_data(v_backup_rec.backup_data);

  RETURN jsonb_build_object(
    'success', true,
    'safety_backup_id', v_safety_id,
    'restored_backup_id', p_backup_id,
    'restored_filename', v_backup_rec.filename,
    'details', v_result
  );
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql SECURITY DEFINER;

-- Grant execution permissions
GRANT EXECUTE ON FUNCTION public.generate_user_backup(UUID, TEXT) TO postgres, authenticated, service_role, anon;
GRANT EXECUTE ON FUNCTION public.restore_user_backup_data(JSONB) TO postgres, authenticated, service_role, anon;
GRANT EXECUTE ON FUNCTION public.restore_user_backup_by_id(UUID, UUID) TO postgres, authenticated, service_role, anon;

NOTIFY pgrst, 'reload schema';
`;

async function run() {
  const client = new Client(DB_CONFIG);
  await client.connect();

  console.log('[Migration] Running SQL migration on NAS PostgreSQL...');
  await client.query(SQL_MIGRATION);
  console.log('[Migration] Migration completed successfully.');

  // Test personal backup for current user
  const userRes = await client.query("SELECT id, email FROM public.profiles LIMIT 1;");
  if (userRes.rows.length > 0) {
    const testUser = userRes.rows[0];
    console.log('[Test User Backup] Generating test backup for user:', testUser.email);
    const testGen = await client.query("SELECT public.generate_user_backup($1, 'manual_user') as id;", [testUser.id]);
    const bId = testGen.rows[0].id;
    console.log('[Test User Backup] Created backup ID:', bId);

    console.log('[Test User Restore] Restoring test backup ID:', bId);
    const testRest = await client.query("SELECT public.restore_user_backup_by_id($1, $2) as result;", [bId, testUser.id]);
    console.log('[Test User Restore] Result:', JSON.stringify(testRest.rows[0].result, null, 2));
  }

  await client.end();
}

run().catch(console.error);
```

## `scripts/package-app.mjs`

```js
#!/usr/bin/env node
/**
 * Builds a clean, secret-free distribution of the app: dist/24h-work-dashboard/
 * (+ ZIP when possible). Includes oauth-client.json so recipients get the
 * one-click Google sign-in; EXCLUDES data/ (tokens!), .env*, node_modules, .next.
 *
 * Usage: npm run package
 */
import { promises as fs } from "fs";
import path from "path";
import { execSync } from "child_process";
import { fileURLToPath } from "url";

const root = path.resolve(path.dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url)), "..");
const OUT_DIR = path.join(root, "dist");
const APP_DIR = path.join(OUT_DIR, "24h-work-dashboard");

const INCLUDE_DIRS = ["app", "components", "lib", "scripts", "docs"];
const INCLUDE_FILES = [
  "package.json",
  "tsconfig.json",
  "next.config.mjs",
  "tailwind.config.ts",
  "postcss.config.mjs",
  ".env.local.example",
  ".gitignore",
  "start.bat",
  "start.sh",
  "README.md",
  "CLAUDE.md",
  "QA-REPORT.md",
  "oauth-client.json", // ships the one-click Google sign-in
];

async function copyDir(src, dst) {
  await fs.mkdir(dst, { recursive: true });
  for (const e of await fs.readdir(src, { withFileTypes: true })) {
    const s = path.join(src, e.name);
    const d = path.join(dst, e.name);
    if (e.isDirectory()) await copyDir(s, d);
    else await fs.copyFile(s, d);
  }
}

await fs.rm(OUT_DIR, { recursive: true, force: true });
await fs.mkdir(APP_DIR, { recursive: true });

let copied = 0;
for (const d of INCLUDE_DIRS) {
  try {
    await copyDir(path.join(root, d), path.join(APP_DIR, d));
    copied++;
  } catch {
    /* optional dir */
  }
}
let missingOauth = false;
for (const f of INCLUDE_FILES) {
  try {
    await fs.copyFile(path.join(root, f), path.join(APP_DIR, f));
    copied++;
  } catch {
    if (f === "oauth-client.json") missingOauth = true;
  }
}

// Safety net: never ship secrets even if include lists change later.
for (const forbidden of ["data", ".env.local", ".env", "node_modules", ".next"]) {
  await fs.rm(path.join(APP_DIR, forbidden), { recursive: true, force: true });
}

// Try to ZIP (PowerShell on Windows, zip elsewhere); folder remains either way.
let zipped = "";
const zipPath = path.join(OUT_DIR, "24h-work-dashboard.zip");
try {
  if (process.platform === "win32") {
    execSync(
      `powershell -NoProfile -Command "Compress-Archive -Path '${APP_DIR}\\*' -DestinationPath '${zipPath}' -Force"`,
      { stdio: "ignore" }
    );
  } else {
    execSync(`cd '${OUT_DIR}' && zip -qr 24h-work-dashboard.zip 24h-work-dashboard`, {
      stdio: "ignore",
      shell: "/bin/bash",
    });
  }
  zipped = zipPath;
} catch {
  /* zip tooling unavailable — folder output is enough */
}

console.log(`Distribution ready: ${APP_DIR}`);
if (zipped) console.log(`ZIP: ${zipped}`);
if (missingOauth) {
  console.warn(
    "\nWARNING: oauth-client.json not found — recipients will NOT get the one-click " +
      "Google sign-in (only the advanced Service Account mode). See README section 'הפצה'."
  );
}
```

## `scripts/restore-db.js`

```
const { Client } = require('pg');
const fs = require('fs');
const path = require('path');
const readline = require('readline');

const BACKUP_DIR = path.join(__dirname, '..', 'backups');

const DB_CONFIG = {
  host: process.env.PGHOST || '10.0.0.16',
  port: parseInt(process.env.PGPORT || '54320', 10),
  user: process.env.PGUSER || 'postgres.your-tenant-id',
  password: process.env.PGPASSWORD || 'a6UAzV7BJSdNjPiLUFZ3NfDoS5kj12',
  database: process.env.PGDATABASE || 'postgres',
};

async function getAvailableBackups() {
  if (!fs.existsSync(BACKUP_DIR)) return [];
  return fs.readdirSync(BACKUP_DIR)
    .filter(f => f.startsWith('backup-') && f.endsWith('.sql'))
    .map(f => {
      const stat = fs.statSync(path.join(BACKUP_DIR, f));
      return {
        name: f,
        path: path.join(BACKUP_DIR, f),
        mtime: stat.mtime,
        sizeKb: (stat.size / 1024).toFixed(1)
      };
    })
    .sort((a, b) => b.mtime - a.mtime);
}

async function runRestore() {
  const backups = await getAvailableBackups();

  if (backups.length === 0) {
    console.error('[Restore Error] No backups found in directory:', BACKUP_DIR);
    process.exit(1);
  }

  console.log('\n=== Available Database Backups ===');
  backups.forEach((b, idx) => {
    console.log(`[${idx + 1}] ${b.name} (${b.mtime.toLocaleString('he-IL')} - ${b.sizeKb} KB)`);
  });

  // Check if a specific file or index was passed via command line argument
  const arg = process.argv[2];
  let selectedBackup = null;

  if (arg) {
    const num = parseInt(arg, 10);
    if (!isNaN(num) && num >= 1 && num <= backups.length) {
      selectedBackup = backups[num - 1];
    } else {
      selectedBackup = backups.find(b => b.name === arg || b.path === arg);
    }
  }

  if (!selectedBackup) {
    // Interactive prompt
    const rl = readline.createInterface({
      input: process.stdin,
      output: process.stdout
    });

    const answer = await new Promise(resolve => {
      rl.question(`\nSelect backup to restore (1-${backups.length}, default 1): `, ans => {
        rl.close();
        resolve(ans.trim());
      });
    });

    const choice = parseInt(answer, 10) || 1;
    selectedBackup = backups[choice - 1] || backups[0];
  }

  console.log(`\n[Restore] Selected backup: ${selectedBackup.name}`);
  console.log(`[Restore] Connecting to PostgreSQL at ${DB_CONFIG.host}:${DB_CONFIG.port}...`);

  const client = new Client(DB_CONFIG);
  await client.connect();

  console.log(`[Restore] Reading SQL backup file...`);
  const sqlContent = fs.readFileSync(selectedBackup.path, 'utf8');

  console.log(`[Restore] Executing restoration transaction on PostgreSQL...`);
  await client.query(sqlContent);

  const countRes = await client.query('SELECT count(*) as total FROM public.reports;');
  console.log(`[Restore] SUCCESS! Restoration complete. Total reports in DB: ${countRes.rows[0].total}`);

  await client.end();
}

runRestore().catch(err => {
  console.error('[Restore Error]', err);
  process.exit(1);
});
```

## `scripts/setup_deleted_backups_table.js`

```
const { Client } = require('pg');

const client = new Client({
  host: process.env.PGHOST || '10.0.0.16',
  port: parseInt(process.env.PGPORT || '54320', 10),
  user: process.env.PGUSER || 'postgres.your-tenant-id',
  password: process.env.PGPASSWORD || 'a6UAzV7BJSdNjPiLUFZ3NfDoS5kj12',
  database: process.env.PGDATABASE || 'postgres',
});

async function run() {
  await client.connect();
  await client.query(`
    CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS public.deleted_user_backups (
      id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
      user_id UUID,
      email TEXT,
      reports_count INTEGER DEFAULT 0,
      backup_data JSONB,
      backup_sql TEXT,
      deleted_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT now(),
      expires_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT (now() + interval '6 months')
    );
    CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_deleted_user_backups_expires ON public.deleted_user_backups(expires_at);

    -- Also create automatic cleanup function for expired backups older than 6 months
    CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.cleanup_expired_deleted_user_backups()
    RETURNS INTEGER AS $$
    DECLARE
      deleted_rows INTEGER;
    BEGIN
      DELETE FROM public.deleted_user_backups
      WHERE expires_at < now();
      GET DIAGNOSTICS deleted_rows = ROW_COUNT;
      RETURN deleted_rows;
    END;
    $$ LANGUAGE plpgsql SECURITY DEFINER;
  `);

  console.log('Successfully set up public.deleted_user_backups table and cleanup function');
  await client.end();
}

run().catch(console.error);
```

## `start.bat`

```bat
@echo off
chcp 65001 >nul
title 24H Work Dashboard
cd /d "%~dp0"

where node >nul 2>nul
if errorlevel 1 (
  echo.
  echo   Node.js is not installed. Opening the download page...
  echo   Install the LTS version, then run start.bat again.
  start https://nodejs.org
  pause
  exit /b 1
)

if not exist node_modules (
  echo.
  echo   First run: installing dependencies ^(one time, ~1 minute^)...
  call npm install --no-audit --no-fund
  if errorlevel 1 ( echo Installation failed. & pause & exit /b 1 )
)

if not exist .next (
  echo.
  echo   Building the app ^(one time^)...
  call npm run build
  if errorlevel 1 ( echo Build failed. & pause & exit /b 1 )
)

echo.
echo   Starting 24H Work Dashboard at http://localhost:3001
echo   Keep this window open. Close it to stop the app.
echo.
start "" http://localhost:3001
npx next start -p 3001
pause
```

## `start.sh`

```bash
#!/usr/bin/env bash
# 24H Work Dashboard launcher (macOS / Linux)
set -e
cd "$(dirname "$0")"

if ! command -v node >/dev/null 2>&1; then
  echo "Node.js is not installed — install the LTS version from https://nodejs.org and re-run."
  exit 1
fi

if [ ! -d node_modules ]; then
  echo "First run: installing dependencies (one time)..."
  npm install --no-audit --no-fund
fi

if [ ! -d .next ]; then
  echo "Building the app (one time)..."
  npm run build
fi

echo "Starting 24H Work Dashboard at http://localhost:3000 (Ctrl+C to stop)"
( sleep 2 && (open http://localhost:3000 2>/dev/null || xdg-open http://localhost:3000 2>/dev/null) ) &
npm run start
```
